> DEVELOPER

Implement the following plan:

# Refactor: Condense at Each Commit (Remove Deferred Condensation)

## Context

The manual-commit strategy currently defers condensation when an agent is ACTIVE during a commit. This creates a complex lifecycle involving `PendingCheckpointID`, the `ACTIVE_COMMITTED` phase, `handleTurnEndCondense`, two-pass PostCommit processing, and shadow branch migration — all of which have been a source of bugs (see `soph/fix-issue-with-reusing-checkpoint-id`).

**Goal:** Always condense at commit time, regardless of whether the agent is active. Each commit gets its own checkpoint ID and metadata entry. This eliminates `PendingCheckpointID`, the `ACTIVE_COMMITTED` phase, and the entire deferred condensation flow.

## Branch

Create `soph/single-commit-per-checkpoint` from `main`.

## What Changes

| Before | After |
|--------|-------|
| 4 phases (IDLE, ACTIVE, ACTIVE_COMMITTED, ENDED) | 3 phases (IDLE, ACTIVE, ENDED) |
| ACTIVE + GitCommit → ACTIVE_COMMITTED + Migrate | ACTIVE + GitCommit → ACTIVE + Condense |
| Condensation at TurnEnd (deferred) | Condensation at commit time (immediate) |
| PendingCheckpointID lifecycle | Eliminated |
| Two-pass PostCommit (condense + migrate) | Single-pass PostCommit |
| handleTurnEndCondense function | Deleted |
| Multiple commits share one checkpoint ID | Each commit gets unique checkpoint ID |

## What Stays

- `LastCheckpointID` — still used for amend trailer restoration
- `migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded` — still needed in SaveChanges/InitializeSession for rebase detection
- `postCommitUpdateBaseCommitOnly` — still needed for commits without trailers
- Attribution correctness — `BaseCommit` still holds old value when `CondenseSession` runs (updated after)
- `AttributionBaseCommit` — kept for backward compat (now redundant but harmless)

## Implementation Steps

### Step 1: State Machine (`cmd/entire/cli/session/phase.go`)

- Remove `PhaseActiveCommitted` constant and from `allPhases`
- `PhaseFromString("active_committed")` → `PhaseActive` (backward compat)
- `IsActive()`: remove `|| p == PhaseActiveCommitted`
- Remove `transitionFromActiveCommitted` function entirely
- Change `transitionFromActive` `EventGitCommit`:
  ```go
  return TransitionResult{
      NewPhase: PhaseActive,
      Actions:  []Action{ActionCondense, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
  }
  ```
- Remove `ActionMigrateShadowBranch` constant, its `String()` case, and passthrough in `ApplyCommonActions`
- Remove `case PhaseActiveCommitted` in `Transition` switch

### Step 2: Session State (`cmd/entire/cli/session/state.go`)

- Remove `PendingCheckpointID string` field from `State` struct (line 62-64)
- Old state files with this field are silently ignored by Go JSON unmarshaling

### Step 3: PostCommit Simplification (`cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go`)

- Remove two-pass architecture: delete `pendingMigration` type, `pendingMigrations` slice, Pass 2 loop (lines 644-647, 728-739)
- Remove `ActionMigrateShadowBranch` case from action dispatch (lines 703-708)
- Shadow branch cleanup: replace `activeSessionsOnBranch` with `condensedOnBranch` tracking — only preserve branches for ACTIVE sessions that were NOT condensed:
  ```go
  condensed := false
  // ... in ActionCondense: condensed = true
  if state.Phase.IsActive() && !condensed {
      uncondensedActiveOnBranch[shadowBranchName] = true
  }
  ```
- In `condenseAndUpdateState` (line 805): remove `state.PendingCheckpointID = ""` (field gone)

### Step 4: PrepareCommitMsg Simplification (`cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go`)

- `handleAmendCommitMsg`: remove `PendingCheckpointID` check (lines 532-536), only check `LastCheckpointID`
- Remove `isRestoringExisting` logic checking `PendingCheckpointID` (lines 419-430)
- Fresh ID generation and `addTrailerForAgentCommit` unchanged (already correct from recent fix)

### Step 5: HandleTurnEnd Simplification (`cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go`)

- Remove `ActionCondense` dispatch from `HandleTurnEnd` (line 1449)
- Delete `handleTurnEndCondense` function entirely (lines 1465-1537)
- Delete `hasOtherActiveSessionsOnBranch` function (lines 1542-1556, only caller was handleTurnEndCondense)
- `HandleTurnEnd` becomes a no-op (ACTIVE + TurnEnd emits only `ActionUpdateLastInteraction`, handled by `ApplyCommonActions`)

### Step 6: InitializeSession & Other Cleanup

- `InitializeSession` (line 1231-1233): remove `PendingCheckpointID` clearing
- `manual_commit_git.go` (lines 118-121): remove `PendingCheckpointID` preservation comment
- `manual_commit_condensation.go` (line 609): remove `state.PendingCheckpointID = ""` in `CondenseSessionByID`

### Step 7: Test Updates

**`session/phase_test.go`:**
- `TestPhaseFromString`: `"active_committed"` → expect `PhaseActive`
- `TestPhase_IsActive`: remove ACTIVE_COMMITTED case
- `TestTransitionFromActive`: GitCommit → ACTIVE + ActionCondense (not ACTIVE_COMMITTED + Migrate)
- Delete `TestTransitionFromActiveCommitted` entirely
- Update `TestApplyCommonActions_*` and `TestMermaidDiagram` references
- Remove `ActionMigrateShadowBranch` from action string tests

**`strategy/phase_postcommit_test.go`:**
- `TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_NoCondensation` → rewrite as `TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_Condenses`
- Delete: `TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_SetsPendingCheckpointID`, `TestPostCommit_ActiveCommitted_MigratesShadowBranch`
- Delete all TurnEnd condensation tests: `TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_*`, `TestTurnEnd_ConcurrentSession_*`
- Rewrite `TestTurnEnd_DeferredCondensation_AttributionUsesOriginalBase` → `TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_AttributionUsesOriginalBase`
- Keep `TestTurnEnd_Active_NoActions` (update comment)
- Add: `TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch`

**`strategy/phase_prepare_commit_msg_test.go`:**
- Rewrite amend test to use `LastCheckpointID` only (remove `PendingCheckpointID`)
- Update `ConsecutiveCommits` test: phase → ACTIVE (not ACTIVE_COMMITTED), remove `PendingCheckpointID`

**`strategy/mid_turn_commit_test.go`:**
- Delete `TestSaveChanges_PreservesPendingCheckpointID`

**`integration_test/phase_transitions_test.go`:**
- `TestShadow_CommitBeforeStop`: verify immediate condensation (not deferred), phase stays ACTIVE (not ACTIVE_COMMITTED), no condensation at TurnEnd

### Step 8: Documentation Updates

**`docs/architecture/sessions-and-checkpoints.md`:**
- Remove `PendingCheckpointID` row and deferred condensation sections (added earlier today)

**`CLAUDE.md`:**
- Remove ACTIVE_COMMITTED from phases, ActionMigrateShadowBranch from actions
- Update transitions: `ACTIVE + GitCommit → ACTIVE + [ActionCondense]`
- Remove PendingCheckpointID references throughout

## Verification

```bash
mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci
```

## Backward Compatibility

- Old state files with `"pending_checkpoint_id"` → silently ignored
- Old state files with `"phase": "active_committed"` → mapped to `"active"` via PhaseFromString
- In-flight ACTIVE_COMMITTED sessions: next commit condenses immediately (one-time migration)

     5→import (

    10→
    11→// Phase represents the lifecycle stage of a session.
    12→type Phase string
    13→
    14→const (
    15→	PhaseActive          Phase = "active"
    16→	PhaseActiveCommitted Phase = "active_committed"
    17→	PhaseIdle            Phase = "idle"
    18→	PhaseEnded           Phase = "ended"
    19→)
    20→
    21→// allPhases is the canonical list of phases for enumeration (e.g., diagram generation).
    22→var allPhases = []Phase{PhaseIdle, PhaseActive, PhaseActiveCommitted, PhaseEnded}
    23→
    24→// PhaseFromString normalizes a phase string, treating empty or unknown values
    25→// as PhaseIdle for backward compatibility with pre-state-machine session files.
    26→func PhaseFromString(s string) Phase {
    27→	switch Phase(s) {
    28→	case PhaseActive:
    29→		return PhaseActive
    30→	case PhaseActiveCommitted:
    31→		return PhaseActiveCommitted
    32→	case PhaseIdle:
    33→		return PhaseIdle
    34→	case PhaseEnded:
    35→		return PhaseEnded
    36→	default:
    37→		return PhaseIdle
    38→	}
    39→}
    40→
    41→// IsActive reports whether the phase represents an active agent turn.
    42→func (p Phase) IsActive() bool {
    43→	return p == PhaseActive || p == PhaseActiveCommitted
    44→}
    45→
    46→// Event represents something that happened to a session.
    47→type Event int
    48→
    49→const (
    50→	EventTurnStart    Event = iota // Agent begins working on a prompt
    51→	EventTurnEnd                   // Agent finishes its turn
    52→	EventGitCommit                 // A git commit was made (PostCommit hook)
    53→	EventSessionStart              // Session process started (SessionStart hook)
    54→	EventSessionStop               // Session process ended (SessionStop hook)
    55→)
    56→
    57→// allEvents is the canonical list of events for enumeration.
    58→var allEvents = []Event{EventTurnStart, EventTurnEnd, EventGitCommit, EventSessionStart, EventSessionStop}
    59→
    60→// String returns a human-readable name for the event.
    61→func (e Event) String() string {
    62→	switch e {
    63→	case EventTurnStart:
    64→		return "TurnStart"
    65→	case EventTurnEnd:
    66→		return "TurnEnd"
    67→	case EventGitCommit:
    68→		return "GitCommit"
    69→	case EventSessionStart:
    70→		return "SessionStart"
    71→	case EventSessionStop:
    72→		return "SessionStop"
    73→	default:
    74→		return fmt.Sprintf("Event(%d)", int(e))
    75→	}
    76→}
    77→
    78→// Action represents a side effect that should be performed after a transition.
    79→// The caller is responsible for executing these -- the state machine only declares them.
    80→type Action int
    81→
    82→const (
    83→	ActionCondense               Action = iota // Condense session data to permanent storage
    84→	ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched               // Condense only if FilesTouched is non-empty
    85→	ActionDiscardIfNoFiles                     // Discard session if FilesTouched is empty
    86→	ActionMigrateShadowBranch                  // Migrate shadow branch to new HEAD
    87→	ActionWarnStaleSession                     // Warn user about stale session(s)
    88→	ActionClearEndedAt                         // Clear EndedAt timestamp (session re-entering)
    89→	ActionUpdateLastInteraction                // Update LastInteractionTime
    90→)
    91→
    92→// String returns a human-readable name for the action.
    93→func (a Action) String() string {
    94→	switch a {
    95→	case ActionCondense:
    96→		return "Condense"
    97→	case ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched:
    98→		return "CondenseIfFilesTouched"
    99→	case ActionDiscardIfNoFiles:
   100→		return "DiscardIfNoFiles"
   101→	case ActionMigrateShadowBranch:
   102→		return "MigrateShadowBranch"
   103→	case ActionWarnStaleSession:
   104→		return "WarnStaleSession"
   105→	case ActionClearEndedAt:
   106→		return "ClearEndedAt"
   107→	case ActionUpdateLastInteraction:
   108→		return "UpdateLastInteraction"
   109→	default:
   110→		return fmt.Sprintf("Action(%d)", int(a))
   111→	}
   112→}
   113→
   114→// TransitionContext provides read-only context for transitions that need
   115→// to inspect session state without mutating it.
   116→type TransitionContext struct {
   117→	HasFilesTouched    bool // len(FilesTouched) > 0
   118→	IsRebaseInProgress bool // .git/rebase-merge/ or .git/rebase-apply/ exists
   119→}
   120→
   121→// TransitionResult holds the outcome of a state machine transition.
   122→type TransitionResult struct {
   123→	NewPhase Phase
   124→	Actions  []Action
   125→}
   126→
   127→// Transition computes the next phase and required actions given the current
   128→// phase and an event. This is a pure function with no side effects.
   129→//
   130→// Empty or unknown phase values are normalized to PhaseIdle for backward
   131→// compatibility with session state files created before phase tracking.
   132→func Transition(current Phase, event Event, ctx TransitionContext) TransitionResult {
   133→	// Normalize empty/unknown phase to idle for backward compatibility.
   134→	current = PhaseFromString(string(current))
   135→
   136→	switch current {
   137→	case PhaseIdle:
   138→		return transitionFromIdle(event, ctx)
   139→	case PhaseActive:
   140→		return transitionFromActive(event, ctx)
   141→	case PhaseActiveCommitted:
   142→		return transitionFromActiveCommitted(event, ctx)
   143→	case PhaseEnded:
   144→		return transitionFromEnded(event, ctx)
   145→	default:
   146→		// PhaseFromString guarantees we only get known phases, but the
   147→		// exhaustive linter requires handling the default case.
   148→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseIdle}
   149→	}
   150→}
   151→
   152→func transitionFromIdle(event Event, ctx TransitionContext) TransitionResult {
   153→	switch event {
   154→	case EventTurnStart:
   155→		return TransitionResult{
   156→			NewPhase: PhaseActive,
   157→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   158→		}
   159→	case EventTurnEnd:
   160→		// Turn end while idle is a no-op (no active turn to end).
   161→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseIdle}
   162→	case EventGitCommit:
   163→		if ctx.IsRebaseInProgress {
   164→			return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseIdle}
   165→		}
   166→		return TransitionResult{
   167→			NewPhase: PhaseIdle,
   168→			Actions:  []Action{ActionCondense, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   169→		}
   170→	case EventSessionStart:
   171→		// Already condensable, no-op.
   172→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseIdle}
   173→	case EventSessionStop:
   174→		return TransitionResult{
   175→			NewPhase: PhaseEnded,
   176→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   177→		}
   178→	default:
   179→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseIdle}
   180→	}
   181→}
   182→
   183→func transitionFromActive(event Event, ctx TransitionContext) TransitionResult {
   184→	switch event {
   185→	case EventTurnStart:
   186→		// Ctrl-C recovery: just continue.
   187→		// This is a degenerate case where the EndTurn is skipped after a in-turn commit.
   188→		// Either the agent crashed or the user interrupted it.
   189→		// We choose to continue, and defer condensation to the next TurnEnd or GitCommit.
   190→		return TransitionResult{
   191→			NewPhase: PhaseActive,
   192→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   193→		}
   194→	case EventTurnEnd:
   195→		return TransitionResult{
   196→			NewPhase: PhaseIdle,
   197→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   198→		}
   199→	case EventGitCommit:
   200→		if ctx.IsRebaseInProgress {
   201→			return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseActive}
   202→		}
   203→		return TransitionResult{
   204→			NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted,
   205→			Actions:  []Action{ActionMigrateShadowBranch, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   206→		}
   207→	case EventSessionStart:
   208→		return TransitionResult{
   209→			NewPhase: PhaseActive,
   210→			Actions:  []Action{ActionWarnStaleSession},
   211→		}
   212→	case EventSessionStop:
   213→		return TransitionResult{
   214→			NewPhase: PhaseEnded,
   215→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   216→		}
   217→	default:
   218→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseActive}
   219→	}
   220→}
   221→
   222→func transitionFromActiveCommitted(event Event, ctx TransitionContext) TransitionResult {
   223→	switch event {
   224→	case EventTurnStart:
   225→		// Ctrl-C recovery after commit.
   226→		// This is a degenerate case where the EndTurn is skipped after a in-turn commit.
   227→		// Either the agent crashed or the user interrupted it.
   228→		// We choose to continue, and defer condensation to the next TurnEnd or GitCommit.
   229→		return TransitionResult{
   230→			NewPhase: PhaseActive,
   231→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   232→		}
   233→	case EventTurnEnd:
   234→		return TransitionResult{
   235→			NewPhase: PhaseIdle,
   236→			Actions:  []Action{ActionCondense, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   237→		}
   238→	case EventGitCommit:
   239→		if ctx.IsRebaseInProgress {
   240→			return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted}
   241→		}
   242→		return TransitionResult{
   243→			NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted,
   244→			Actions:  []Action{ActionMigrateShadowBranch, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   245→		}
   246→	case EventSessionStart:
   247→		return TransitionResult{
   248→			NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted,
   249→			Actions:  []Action{ActionWarnStaleSession},
   250→		}
   251→	case EventSessionStop:
   252→		return TransitionResult{
   253→			NewPhase: PhaseEnded,
   254→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   255→		}
   256→	default:
   257→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted}
   258→	}
   259→}
   260→
   261→func transitionFromEnded(event Event, ctx TransitionContext) TransitionResult {
   262→	switch event {
   263→	case EventTurnStart:
   264→		return TransitionResult{
   265→			NewPhase: PhaseActive,
   266→			Actions:  []Action{ActionClearEndedAt, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   267→		}
   268→	case EventTurnEnd:
   269→		// Turn end while ended is a no-op.
   270→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseEnded}
   271→	case EventGitCommit:
   272→		if ctx.IsRebaseInProgress {
   273→			return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseEnded}
   274→		}
   275→		if ctx.HasFilesTouched {
   276→			return TransitionResult{
   277→				NewPhase: PhaseEnded,
   278→				Actions:  []Action{ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   279→			}
   280→		}
   281→		return TransitionResult{
   282→			NewPhase: PhaseEnded,
   283→			Actions:  []Action{ActionDiscardIfNoFiles, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   284→		}
   285→	case EventSessionStart:
   286→		return TransitionResult{
   287→			NewPhase: PhaseIdle,
   288→			Actions:  []Action{ActionClearEndedAt},
   289→		}
   290→	case EventSessionStop:
   291→		// Already ended, no-op.
   292→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseEnded}
   293→	default:
   294→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseEnded}
   295→	}
   296→}
   297→
   298→// ApplyCommonActions applies the common (non-strategy-specific) actions from a
   299→// TransitionResult to the given State. It updates Phase, LastInteractionTime,
   300→// and EndedAt as indicated by the transition.
   301→//
   302→// Returns the subset of actions that require strategy-specific handling
   303→// (e.g., ActionCondense, ActionMigrateShadowBranch, ActionWarnStaleSession).
   304→// The caller is responsible for dispatching those.
   305→func ApplyCommonActions(state *State, result TransitionResult) []Action {
   306→	state.Phase = result.NewPhase
   307→
   308→	var remaining []Action
   309→	for _, action := range result.Actions {
   310→		switch action {
   311→		case ActionUpdateLastInteraction:
   312→			now := time.Now()
   313→			state.LastInteractionTime = &now
   314→		case ActionClearEndedAt:
   315→			state.EndedAt = nil
   316→		case ActionCondense, ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched, ActionDiscardIfNoFiles,
   317→			ActionMigrateShadowBranch, ActionWarnStaleSession:
   318→			// Strategy-specific actions — pass through to caller.
   319→			remaining = append(remaining, action)
   320→		}
   321→	}
   322→	return remaining
   323→}
   324→
   325→// MermaidDiagram generates a Mermaid state diagram from the transition table.
   326→// The diagram is derived by calling Transition() for representative context
   327→// variants per phase/event, so it stays in sync with the implementation.
   328→func MermaidDiagram() string {
   329→	var b strings.Builder
   330→	b.WriteString("stateDiagram-v2\n")
   331→
   332→	// State declarations with descriptions.
   333→	b.WriteString("    state \"IDLE\" as idle\n")
   334→	b.WriteString("    state \"ACTIVE\" as active\n")
   335→	b.WriteString("    state \"ACTIVE_COMMITTED\" as active_committed\n")
   336→	b.WriteString("    state \"ENDED\" as ended\n")
   337→	b.WriteString("\n")
   338→
   339→	// Context variants for GitCommit: rebase, files/no-files.
   340→	type contextVariant struct {
   341→		label string
   342→		ctx   TransitionContext
   343→	}
   344→
   345→	// For each phase/event, generate edges.
   346→	for _, phase := range allPhases {
   347→		for _, event := range allEvents {
   348→			var variants []contextVariant
   349→			switch {
   350→			case event == EventGitCommit && phase == PhaseEnded:
   351→				// HasFilesTouched only affects PhaseEnded transitions;
   352→				// other phases produce identical edges for both values.
   353→				variants = []contextVariant{
   354→					{"[files]", TransitionContext{HasFilesTouched: true}},
   355→					{"[no files]", TransitionContext{HasFilesTouched: false}},
   356→					{"[rebase]", TransitionContext{IsRebaseInProgress: true}},
   357→				}
   358→			case event == EventGitCommit:
   359→				variants = []contextVariant{
   360→					{"", TransitionContext{}},
   361→					{"[rebase]", TransitionContext{IsRebaseInProgress: true}},
   362→				}
   363→			default:
   364→				variants = []contextVariant{
   365→					{"", TransitionContext{}},
   366→				}
   367→			}
   368→
   369→			for _, v := range variants {
   370→				result := Transition(phase, event, v.ctx)
   371→
   372→				// Build edge label.
   373→				label := event.String()
   374→				if v.label != "" {
   375→					label += " " + v.label
   376→				}
   377→				if len(result.Actions) > 0 {
   378→					actionNames := make([]string, 0, len(result.Actions))
   379→					for _, a := range result.Actions {
   380→						actionNames = append(actionNames, a.String())
   381→					}
   382→					label += " / " + strings.Join(actionNames, ", ")
   383→				}
   384→
   385→				from := string(phase)
   386→				to := string(result.NewPhase)
   387→
   388→				fmt.Fprintf(&b, "    %s --> %s : %s\n", from, to, label)
   389→			}
   390→		}
   391→	}
   392→
   393→	return b.String()
   394→}

    18→
    19→const (
    20→	// SessionStateDirName is the directory name for session state files within git common dir.
    21→	SessionStateDirName = "entire-sessions"
    22→)
    23→
    24→// State represents the state of an active session.
    25→// This is stored in .git/entire-sessions/<session-id>.json
    26→type State struct {
    27→	// SessionID is the unique session identifier
    28→	SessionID string `json:"session_id"`
    29→
    30→	// CLIVersion is the version of the CLI that created this session
    31→	CLIVersion string `json:"cli_version,omitempty"`
    32→
    33→	// BaseCommit tracks the current shadow branch base. Initially set to HEAD when the
    34→	// session starts, but updated on migration (pull/rebase) and after condensation.
    35→	// Used for shadow branch naming and checkpoint storage — NOT for attribution.
    36→	BaseCommit string `json:"base_commit"`
    37→
    38→	// AttributionBaseCommit is the commit used as the reference point for attribution calculations.
    39→	// Unlike BaseCommit (which tracks the shadow branch and moves with migration), this field
    40→	// preserves the original base commit so deferred condensation can correctly calculate
    41→	// agent vs human line attribution. Updated only after successful condensation.
    42→	AttributionBaseCommit string `json:"attribution_base_commit,omitempty"`
    43→
    44→	// WorktreePath is the absolute path to the worktree root
    45→	WorktreePath string `json:"worktree_path,omitempty"`
    46→
    47→	// WorktreeID is the internal git worktree identifier (empty for main worktree)
    48→	// Derived from .git/worktrees/<name>/, stable across git worktree move
    49→	WorktreeID string `json:"worktree_id,omitempty"`
    50→
    51→	// StartedAt is when the session was started
    52→	StartedAt time.Time `json:"started_at"`
    53→
    54→	// EndedAt is when the session was explicitly closed by the user.
    55→	// nil means the session is still active or was not cleanly closed.
    56→	EndedAt *time.Time `json:"ended_at,omitempty"`
    57→
    58→	// Phase is the lifecycle stage of this session (see phase.go).
    59→	// Empty means idle (backward compat with pre-state-machine files).
    60→	Phase Phase `json:"phase,omitempty"`
    61→
    62→	// PendingCheckpointID is the checkpoint ID for the current commit cycle.
    63→	// Generated once when first needed, reused across all commits in the session.
    64→	PendingCheckpointID string `json:"pending_checkpoint_id,omitempty"`
    65→
    66→	// LastInteractionTime is updated on every hook invocation.
    67→	// Used for stale session detection in "entire doctor".
    68→	LastInteractionTime *time.Time `json:"last_interaction_time,omitempty"`
    69→
    70→	// StepCount is the number of checkpoints/steps created in this session.
    71→	// JSON tag kept as "checkpoint_count" for backward compatibility with existing state files.
    72→	StepCount int `json:"checkpoint_count"`
    73→
    74→	// CheckpointTranscriptStart is the transcript line offset where the current
    75→	// checkpoint cycle began. Set to 0 at session start, updated to current
    76→	// transcript length after each condensation. Used to scope the transcript
    77→	// for checkpoint condensation: "everything since last checkpoint".
    78→	CheckpointTranscriptStart int `json:"checkpoint_transcript_start,omitempty"`
    79→
    80→	// Deprecated: CondensedTranscriptLines is replaced by CheckpointTranscriptStart.
    81→	// Kept for backward compatibility with existing state files.
    82→	// Use NormalizeAfterLoad() to migrate.
    83→	CondensedTranscriptLines int `json:"condensed_transcript_lines,omitempty"`
    84→
    85→	// UntrackedFilesAtStart tracks files that existed at session start (to preserve during rewind)
    86→	UntrackedFilesAtStart []string `json:"untracked_files_at_start,omitempty"`
    87→
    88→	// FilesTouched tracks files modified/created/deleted during this session
    89→	FilesTouched []string `json:"files_touched,omitempty"`
    90→
    91→	// LastCheckpointID is the checkpoint ID from last condensation, reused for subsequent commits without new content
    92→	LastCheckpointID id.CheckpointID `json:"last_checkpoint_id,omitempty"`
    93→
    94→	// AgentType identifies the agent that created this session (e.g., "Claude Code", "Gemini CLI", "Cursor")
    95→	AgentType agent.AgentType `json:"agent_type,omitempty"`
    96→
    97→	// Token usage tracking (accumulated across all checkpoints in this session)
    98→	TokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage `json:"token_usage,omitempty"`
    99→
   100→	// Deprecated: TranscriptLinesAtStart is replaced by CheckpointTranscriptStart.
   101→	// Kept for backward compatibility with existing state files.
   102→	TranscriptLinesAtStart int `json:"transcript_lines_at_start,omitempty"`
   103→
   104→	// TranscriptIdentifierAtStart is the last transcript identifier when the session started.
   105→	// Used for identifier-based transcript scoping (UUID for Claude, message ID for Gemini).
   106→	TranscriptIdentifierAtStart string `json:"transcript_identifier_at_start,omitempty"`
   107→
   108→	// TranscriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection)
   109→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path,omitempty"`
   110→
   111→	// FirstPrompt is the first user prompt that started this session (truncated for display)
   112→	FirstPrompt string `json:"first_prompt,omitempty"`
   113→
   114→	// PromptAttributions tracks user and agent line changes at each prompt start.
   115→	// This enables accurate attribution by capturing user edits between checkpoints.
   116→	PromptAttributions []PromptAttribution `json:"prompt_attributions,omitempty"`
   117→
   118→	// PendingPromptAttribution holds attribution calculated at prompt start (before agent runs).
   119→	// This is moved to PromptAttributions when SaveChanges is called.
   120→	PendingPromptAttribution *PromptAttribution `json:"pending_prompt_attribution,omitempty"`
   121→}
   122→
   123→// PromptAttribution captures line-level attribution data at the start of each prompt.
   124→// By recording what changed since the last checkpoint BEFORE the agent works,
   125→// we can accurately separate user edits from agent contributions.
   126→type PromptAttribution struct {
   127→	// CheckpointNumber is which checkpoint this was recorded before (1-indexed)
   128→	CheckpointNumber int `json:"checkpoint_number"`
   129→
   130→	// UserLinesAdded is lines added by user since the last checkpoint
   131→	UserLinesAdded int `json:"user_lines_added"`
   132→
   133→	// UserLinesRemoved is lines removed by user since the last checkpoint
   134→	UserLinesRemoved int `json:"user_lines_removed"`
   135→
   136→	// AgentLinesAdded is total agent lines added so far (base → last checkpoint).
   137→	// Always 0 for checkpoint 1 since there's no previous checkpoint to measure against.
   138→	AgentLinesAdded int `json:"agent_lines_added"`
   139→
   140→	// AgentLinesRemoved is total agent lines removed so far (base → last checkpoint).
   141→	// Always 0 for checkpoint 1 since there's no previous checkpoint to measure against.
   142→	AgentLinesRemoved int `json:"agent_lines_removed"`
   143→
   144→	// UserAddedPerFile tracks per-file user additions for accurate modification tracking.
   145→	// This enables distinguishing user self-modifications from agent modifications.
   146→	// See docs/architecture/attribution.md for details.
   147→	UserAddedPerFile map[string]int `json:"user_added_per_file,omitempty"`
   148→}
   149→
   150→// NormalizeAfterLoad applies backward-compatible migrations to state loaded from disk.
   151→// Call this after deserializing a State from JSON.
   152→func (s *State) NormalizeAfterLoad() {
   153→	// Migrate transcript fields: CheckpointTranscriptStart replaces both
   154→	// CondensedTranscriptLines and TranscriptLinesAtStart from older state files.
   155→	if s.CheckpointTranscriptStart == 0 {
   156→		if s.CondensedTranscriptLines > 0 {
   157→			s.CheckpointTranscriptStart = s.CondensedTranscriptLines
   158→		} else if s.TranscriptLinesAtStart > 0 {
   159→			s.CheckpointTranscriptStart = s.TranscriptLinesAtStart
   160→		}
   161→	}
   162→	// Clear deprecated fields so they aren't re-persisted.
   163→	// Note: this is a one-way migration. If the state is re-saved, older CLI versions
   164→	// will see 0 for these fields and fall back to scoping from the transcript start.
   165→	// This is acceptable since CLI upgrades are monotonic and the worst case is
   166→	// redundant transcript content in a condensation, not data loss.
   167→	s.CondensedTranscriptLines = 0
   168→	s.TranscriptLinesAtStart = 0
   169→
   170→	// Backfill AttributionBaseCommit for sessions created before this field existed.
   171→	// Without this, a mid-turn commit would migrate BaseCommit and the fallback in
   172→	// calculateSessionAttributions would use the migrated value, producing zero attribution.
   173→	if s.AttributionBaseCommit == "" && s.BaseCommit != "" {
   174→		s.AttributionBaseCommit = s.BaseCommit
   175→	}
   176→}
   177→
   178→// StateStore provides low-level operations for managing session state files.
   179→//
   180→// StateStore is a primitive for session state persistence. It is NOT the same as
   181→// the Sessions interface - it only handles state files in .git/entire-sessions/,
   182→// not the full session data which includes checkpoint content.
   183→//
   184→// Use StateStore directly in strategies for performance-critical state operations.
   185→// Use the Sessions interface (when implemented) for high-level session management.
   186→type StateStore struct {
   187→	// stateDir is the directory where session state files are stored
   188→	stateDir string
   189→}
   190→
   191→// NewStateStore creates a new state store.
   192→// Uses the git common dir to store session state (shared across worktrees).
   193→func NewStateStore() (*StateStore, error) {
   194→	commonDir, err := getGitCommonDir()
   195→	if err != nil {
   196→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get git common dir: %w", err)
   197→	}
   198→	return &StateStore{
   199→		stateDir: filepath.Join(commonDir, SessionStateDirName),
   200→	}, nil
   201→}
   202→
   203→// NewStateStoreWithDir creates a new state store with a custom directory.
   204→// This is useful for testing.
   205→func NewStateStoreWithDir(stateDir string) *StateStore {
   206→	return &StateStore{stateDir: stateDir}
   207→}
   208→
   209→// Load loads the session state for the given session ID.
   210→// Returns (nil, nil) when session file doesn't exist (not an error condition).
   211→func (s *StateStore) Load(ctx context.Context, sessionID string) (*State, error) {
   212→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   213→
   214→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   215→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   216→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   217→	}
   218→
   219→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(sessionID)
   220→
   221→	data, err := os.ReadFile(stateFile) //nolint:gosec // stateFile is derived from sessionID
   222→	if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   223→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil,nil indicates session not found (expected case)
   224→	}
   225→	if err != nil {
   226→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session state: %w", err)
   227→	}
   228→
   229→	var state State
   230→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
   231→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal session state: %w", err)
   232→	}
   233→	state.NormalizeAfterLoad()
   234→	return &state, nil
   235→}
   236→
   237→// Save saves the session state atomically.
   238→func (s *StateStore) Save(ctx context.Context, state *State) error {
   239→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   240→
   241→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   242→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(state.SessionID); err != nil {
   243→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   244→	}
   245→
   246→	if err := os.MkdirAll(s.stateDir, 0o750); err != nil {
   247→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session state directory: %w", err)
   248→	}
   249→
   250→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(state, "", "  ")
   251→	if err != nil {
   252→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal session state: %w", err)
   253→	}
   254→
   255→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(state.SessionID)
   256→
   257→	// Atomic write: write to temp file, then rename
   258→	tmpFile := stateFile + ".tmp"
   259→	if err := os.WriteFile(tmpFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   260→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write session state: %w", err)
   261→	}
   262→	if err := os.Rename(tmpFile, stateFile); err != nil {
   263→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to rename session state file: %w", err)
   264→	}
   265→	return nil
   266→}
   267→
   268→// Clear removes the session state file for the given session ID.
   269→func (s *StateStore) Clear(ctx context.Context, sessionID string) error {
   270→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   271→
   272→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   273→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   274→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   275→	}
   276→
   277→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(sessionID)
   278→
   279→	if err := os.Remove(stateFile); err != nil {
   280→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   281→			return nil // Already gone, not an error
   282→		}
   283→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session state file: %w", err)
   284→	}
   285→	return nil
   286→}
   287→
   288→// RemoveAll removes the entire session state directory.
   289→// This is used during uninstall to completely remove all session state.
   290→func (s *StateStore) RemoveAll() error {
   291→	if err := os.RemoveAll(s.stateDir); err != nil {
   292→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session state directory: %w", err)
   293→	}
   294→	return nil
   295→}
   296→
   297→// List returns all session states.
   298→func (s *StateStore) List(ctx context.Context) ([]*State, error) {
   299→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   300→
   301→	entries, err := os.ReadDir(s.stateDir)
   302→	if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   303→		return nil, nil
   304→	}
   305→	if err != nil {
   306→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session state directory: %w", err)
   307→	}
   308→
   309→	var states []*State
   310→	for _, entry := range entries {
   311→		if entry.IsDir() || !strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json") {
   312→			continue
   313→		}
   314→		if strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), ".tmp") {
   315→			continue // Skip temp files
   316→		}
   317→
   318→		sessionID := strings.TrimSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json")
   319→		state, err := s.Load(ctx, sessionID)
   320→		if err != nil {
   321→			continue // Skip corrupted state files
   322→		}
   323→		if state == nil {
   324→			continue
   325→		}
   326→
   327→		states = append(states, state)
   328→	}
   329→	return states, nil
   330→}
   331→
   332→// stateFilePath returns the path to a session state file.
   333→func (s *StateStore) stateFilePath(sessionID string) string {
   334→	return filepath.Join(s.stateDir, sessionID+".json")
   335→}
   336→
   337→// getGitCommonDir returns the path to the shared git directory.
   338→// In a regular checkout, this is .git/
   339→// In a worktree, this is the main repo's .git/ (not .git/worktrees/<name>/)
   340→func getGitCommonDir() (string, error) {
   341→	ctx := context.Background()
   342→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--git-common-dir")
   343→	cmd.Dir = "."
   344→	output, err := cmd.Output()
   345→	if err != nil {
   346→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get git common dir: %w", err)
   347→	}
   348→
   349→	commonDir := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
   350→
   351→	// git rev-parse --git-common-dir returns relative paths from the working directory,
   352→	// so we need to make it absolute if it isn't already
   353→	if !filepath.IsAbs(commonDir) {
   354→		commonDir = filepath.Join(".", commonDir)
   355→	}
   356→
   357→	return filepath.Clean(commonDir), nil
   358→}
