    29→
    30→// listCheckpoints returns all checkpoints from the metadata branch.
    31→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.ListCommitted() for reading from entire/checkpoints/v1.
    32→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) listCheckpoints() ([]CheckpointInfo, error) {
    33→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    34→	if err != nil {
    35→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    36→	}
    37→
    38→	committed, err := store.ListCommitted(context.Background())
    39→	if err != nil {
    40→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list committed checkpoints: %w", err)
    41→	}
    42→
    43→	// Convert from checkpoint.CommittedInfo to strategy.CheckpointInfo
    44→	result := make([]CheckpointInfo, 0, len(committed))
    45→	for _, c := range committed {
    46→		result = append(result, CheckpointInfo{
    47→			CheckpointID:     c.CheckpointID,
    48→			SessionID:        c.SessionID,
    49→			CreatedAt:        c.CreatedAt,
    50→			CheckpointsCount: c.CheckpointsCount,
    51→			FilesTouched:     c.FilesTouched,
    52→			Agent:            c.Agent,
    53→			IsTask:           c.IsTask,
    54→			ToolUseID:        c.ToolUseID,
    55→			SessionCount:     c.SessionCount,
    56→			SessionIDs:       c.SessionIDs,
    57→		})
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	return result, nil
    61→}
    62→
    63→// getCheckpointsForSession returns all checkpoints for a session ID.
    64→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getCheckpointsForSession(sessionID string) ([]CheckpointInfo, error) {
    65→	all, err := s.listCheckpoints()
    66→	if err != nil {
    67→		return nil, err
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	var result []CheckpointInfo
    71→	for _, cp := range all {
    72→		if cp.SessionID == sessionID || strings.HasPrefix(cp.SessionID, sessionID) {
    73→			result = append(result, cp)
    74→		}
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	if len(result) == 0 {
    78→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no checkpoints for session: %s", sessionID)
    79→	}
    80→
    81→	return result, nil
    82→}
    83→
    84→// getCheckpointLog returns the transcript for a specific checkpoint ID.
    85→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.ReadCommitted() for reading from entire/checkpoints/v1.
    86→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getCheckpointLog(checkpointID id.CheckpointID) ([]byte, error) {
    87→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    88→	if err != nil {
    89→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    90→	}
    91→
    92→	content, err := store.ReadLatestSessionContent(context.Background(), checkpointID)
    93→	if err != nil {
    94→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
    95→	}
    96→	if content == nil {
    97→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("checkpoint not found: %s", checkpointID)
    98→	}
    99→	if len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   100→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no transcript found for checkpoint: %s", checkpointID)
   101→	}
   102→
   103→	return content.Transcript, nil
   104→}
   105→
   106→// CondenseSession condenses a session's shadow branch to permanent storage.
   107→// checkpointID is the 12-hex-char value from the Entire-Checkpoint trailer.
   108→// Metadata is stored at sharded path: <checkpoint_id[:2]>/<checkpoint_id[2:]>/
   109→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.WriteCommitted for the git operations.
   110→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CondenseSession(repo *git.Repository, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, state *SessionState) (*CondenseResult, error) {
   111→	// Get shadow branch
   112→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   113→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   114→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   115→	if err != nil {
   116→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("shadow branch not found: %w", err)
   117→	}
   118→
   119→	// Extract session data from the shadow branch (with live transcript fallback).
   120→	// Use tracked files from session state instead of collecting all files from tree.
   121→	// Pass agent type to handle different transcript formats (JSONL for Claude, JSON for Gemini).
   122→	// Pass live transcript path so condensation reads the current file rather than a
   123→	// potentially stale shadow branch copy (SaveChanges may have been skipped if the
   124→	// last turn had no code changes).
   125→	// Pass CheckpointTranscriptStart for accurate token calculation (line offset for Claude, message index for Gemini).
   126→	sessionData, err := s.extractSessionData(repo, ref.Hash(), state.SessionID, state.FilesTouched, state.AgentType, state.TranscriptPath, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   127→	if err != nil {
   128→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to extract session data: %w", err)
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	// Get checkpoint store
   132→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   133→	if err != nil {
   134→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	// Get author info
   138→	authorName, authorEmail := GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo)
   139→	attribution := calculateSessionAttributions(repo, ref, sessionData, state)
   140→	// Get current branch name
   141→	branchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
   142→
   143→	// Generate summary if enabled
   144→	var summary *cpkg.Summary
   145→	if settings.IsSummarizeEnabled() && len(sessionData.Transcript) > 0 {
   146→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
   147→		summarizeCtx := logging.WithComponent(logCtx, "summarize")
   148→
   149→		// Scope transcript to this checkpoint's portion.
   150→		// SliceFromLine only works for JSONL (Claude Code) transcripts where
   151→		// CheckpointTranscriptStart is a line offset. For Gemini JSON transcripts,
   152→		// CheckpointTranscriptStart is a message index and the transcript is a single
   153→		// JSON blob, so line-based slicing would produce malformed content.
   154→		// The summarizer (ParseFromBytes) only supports JSONL, so skip scoping for Gemini.
   155→		var scopedTranscript []byte
   156→		if state.AgentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   157→			scopedTranscript = sessionData.Transcript
   158→		} else {
   159→			scopedTranscript = transcript.SliceFromLine(sessionData.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   160→		}
   161→		if len(scopedTranscript) > 0 {
   162→			var err error
   163→			summary, err = summarize.GenerateFromTranscript(summarizeCtx, scopedTranscript, sessionData.FilesTouched, nil)
   164→			if err != nil {
   165→				logging.Warn(summarizeCtx, "summary generation failed",
   166→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   167→					slog.String("error", err.Error()))
   168→				// Continue without summary - non-blocking
   169→			} else {
   170→				logging.Info(summarizeCtx, "summary generated",
   171→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID))
   172→			}
   173→		}
   174→	}
   175→
   176→	// Write checkpoint metadata using the checkpoint store
   177→	if err := store.WriteCommitted(context.Background(), cpkg.WriteCommittedOptions{
   178→		CheckpointID:                checkpointID,
   179→		SessionID:                   state.SessionID,
   180→		Strategy:                    StrategyNameManualCommit,
   181→		Branch:                      branchName,
   182→		Transcript:                  sessionData.Transcript,
   183→		Prompts:                     sessionData.Prompts,
   184→		Context:                     sessionData.Context,
   185→		FilesTouched:                sessionData.FilesTouched,
   186→		CheckpointsCount:            state.StepCount,
   187→		EphemeralBranch:             shadowBranchName,
   188→		AuthorName:                  authorName,
   189→		AuthorEmail:                 authorEmail,
   190→		Agent:                       state.AgentType,
   191→		TranscriptIdentifierAtStart: state.TranscriptIdentifierAtStart,
   192→		CheckpointTranscriptStart:   state.CheckpointTranscriptStart,
   193→		TokenUsage:                  sessionData.TokenUsage,
   194→		InitialAttribution:          attribution,
   195→		Summary:                     summary,
   196→		SessionTranscriptPath:       homeRelativePath(state.TranscriptPath),
   197→	}); err != nil {
   198→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to write checkpoint metadata: %w", err)
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	return &CondenseResult{
   202→		CheckpointID:         checkpointID,
   203→		SessionID:            state.SessionID,
   204→		CheckpointsCount:     state.StepCount,
   205→		FilesTouched:         sessionData.FilesTouched,
   206→		TotalTranscriptLines: sessionData.FullTranscriptLines,
   207→	}, nil
   208→}
   209→
   210→func calculateSessionAttributions(repo *git.Repository, shadowRef *plumbing.Reference, sessionData *ExtractedSessionData, state *SessionState) *cpkg.InitialAttribution {
   211→	// Calculate initial attribution using accumulated prompt attribution data.
   212→	// This uses user edits captured at each prompt start (before agent works),
   213→	// plus any user edits after the final checkpoint (shadow → head).
   214→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
   215→	var attribution *cpkg.InitialAttribution
   216→	headRef, headErr := repo.Head()
   217→	if headErr != nil {
   218→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get HEAD",
   219→			slog.String("error", headErr.Error()))
   220→	} else {
   221→		headCommit, commitErr := repo.CommitObject(headRef.Hash())
   222→		if commitErr != nil {
   223→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get HEAD commit",
   224→				slog.String("error", commitErr.Error()))
   225→		} else {
   226→			headTree, treeErr := headCommit.Tree()
   227→			if treeErr != nil {
   228→				logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get HEAD tree",
   229→					slog.String("error", treeErr.Error()))
   230→			} else {
   231→				// Get shadow branch tree (checkpoint tree - what the agent wrote)
   232→				shadowCommit, shadowErr := repo.CommitObject(shadowRef.Hash())
   233→				if shadowErr != nil {
   234→					logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get shadow commit",
   235→						slog.String("error", shadowErr.Error()),
   236→						slog.String("shadow_ref", shadowRef.Hash().String()))
   237→				} else {
   238→					shadowTree, shadowTreeErr := shadowCommit.Tree()
   239→					if shadowTreeErr != nil {
   240→						logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get shadow tree",
   241→							slog.String("error", shadowTreeErr.Error()))
   242→					} else {
   243→						// Get base tree (state before session started)
   244→						var baseTree *object.Tree
   245→						attrBase := state.AttributionBaseCommit
   246→						if attrBase == "" {
   247→							attrBase = state.BaseCommit // backward compat
   248→						}
   249→						if baseCommit, baseErr := repo.CommitObject(plumbing.NewHash(attrBase)); baseErr == nil {
   250→							if tree, baseTErr := baseCommit.Tree(); baseTErr == nil {
   251→								baseTree = tree
   252→							} else {
   253→								logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution: base tree unavailable",
   254→									slog.String("error", baseTErr.Error()))
   255→							}
   256→						} else {
   257→							logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution: base commit unavailable",
   258→								slog.String("error", baseErr.Error()),
   259→								slog.String("attribution_base", attrBase))
   260→						}
   261→
   262→						// Log accumulated prompt attributions for debugging
   263→						var totalUserAdded, totalUserRemoved int
   264→						for i, pa := range state.PromptAttributions {
   265→							totalUserAdded += pa.UserLinesAdded
   266→							totalUserRemoved += pa.UserLinesRemoved
   267→							logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution data",
   268→								slog.Int("checkpoint", pa.CheckpointNumber),
   269→								slog.Int("user_added", pa.UserLinesAdded),
   270→								slog.Int("user_removed", pa.UserLinesRemoved),
   271→								slog.Int("agent_added", pa.AgentLinesAdded),
   272→								slog.Int("agent_removed", pa.AgentLinesRemoved),
   273→								slog.Int("index", i))
   274→						}
   275→
   276→						attribution = CalculateAttributionWithAccumulated(
   277→							baseTree,
   278→							shadowTree,
   279→							headTree,
   280→							sessionData.FilesTouched,
   281→							state.PromptAttributions,
   282→						)
   283→
   284→						if attribution != nil {
   285→							logging.Info(logCtx, "attribution calculated",
   286→								slog.Int("agent_lines", attribution.AgentLines),
   287→								slog.Int("human_added", attribution.HumanAdded),
   288→								slog.Int("human_modified", attribution.HumanModified),
   289→								slog.Int("human_removed", attribution.HumanRemoved),
   290→								slog.Int("total_committed", attribution.TotalCommitted),
   291→								slog.Float64("agent_percentage", attribution.AgentPercentage),
   292→								slog.Int("accumulated_user_added", totalUserAdded),
   293→								slog.Int("accumulated_user_removed", totalUserRemoved),
   294→								slog.Int("files_touched", len(sessionData.FilesTouched)))
   295→						}
   296→					}
   297→				}
   298→			}
   299→		}
   300→	}
   301→	return attribution
   302→}
   303→
   304→// extractSessionData extracts session data from the shadow branch.
   305→// filesTouched is the list of files tracked during the session (from SessionState.FilesTouched).
   306→// agentType identifies the agent (e.g., "Gemini CLI", "Claude Code") to determine transcript format.
   307→// liveTranscriptPath, when non-empty and readable, is preferred over the shadow branch copy.
   308→// This handles the case where SaveChanges was skipped (no code changes) but the transcript
   309→// continued growing — the shadow branch copy would be stale.
   310→// checkpointTranscriptStart is the line offset (Claude) or message index (Gemini) where the current checkpoint began.
   311→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractSessionData(repo *git.Repository, shadowRef plumbing.Hash, sessionID string, filesTouched []string, agentType agent.AgentType, liveTranscriptPath string, checkpointTranscriptStart int) (*ExtractedSessionData, error) {
   312→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(shadowRef)
   313→	if err != nil {
   314→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
   315→	}
   316→
   317→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   318→	if err != nil {
   319→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
   320→	}
   321→
   322→	data := &ExtractedSessionData{}
   323→	// sessionID is already an "entire session ID" (with date prefix)
   324→	metadataDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   325→
   326→	// Extract transcript — prefer the live file when available, fall back to shadow branch.
   327→	// The shadow branch copy may be stale if the last turn ended without code changes
   328→	// (SaveChanges is only called when there are file modifications).
   329→	var fullTranscript string
   330→	if liveTranscriptPath != "" {
   331→		if liveData, readErr := os.ReadFile(liveTranscriptPath); readErr == nil && len(liveData) > 0 { //nolint:gosec // path from session state
   332→			fullTranscript = string(liveData)
   333→		}
   334→	}
   335→	if fullTranscript == "" {
   336→		// Fall back to shadow branch copy
   337→		if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName); fileErr == nil {
   338→			if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   339→				fullTranscript = content
   340→			}
   341→		} else if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy); fileErr == nil {
   342→			if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   343→				fullTranscript = content
   344→			}
   345→		}
   346→	}
   347→
   348→	// Process transcript based on agent type
   349→	if fullTranscript != "" {
   350→		data.Transcript = []byte(fullTranscript)
   351→		data.FullTranscriptLines = countTranscriptItems(agentType, fullTranscript)
   352→		data.Prompts = extractUserPrompts(agentType, fullTranscript)
   353→		data.Context = generateContextFromPrompts(data.Prompts)
   354→	}
   355→
   356→	// Use tracked files from session state (not all files in tree)
   357→	data.FilesTouched = filesTouched
   358→
   359→	// Calculate token usage from the extracted transcript portion
   360→	if len(data.Transcript) > 0 {
   361→		data.TokenUsage = calculateTokenUsage(agentType, data.Transcript, checkpointTranscriptStart)
   362→	}
   363→
   364→	return data, nil
   365→}
   366→
   367→// countTranscriptItems counts lines (JSONL) or messages (JSON) in a transcript.
   368→// For Claude Code and JSONL-based agents, this counts lines.
   369→// For Gemini CLI and JSON-based agents, this counts messages.
   370→// Returns 0 if the content is empty or malformed.
   371→func countTranscriptItems(agentType agent.AgentType, content string) int {
   372→	if content == "" {
   373→		return 0
   374→	}
   375→
   376→	// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown
   377→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {
   378→		transcript, err := geminicli.ParseTranscript([]byte(content))
   379→		if err == nil && transcript != nil && len(transcript.Messages) > 0 {
   380→			return len(transcript.Messages)
   381→		}
   382→		// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return 0
   383→		if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   384→			return 0
   385→		}
   386→		// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type
   387→	}
   388→
   389→	// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents
   390→	allLines := strings.Split(content, "\n")
   391→	// Trim trailing empty lines (from final \n in JSONL)
   392→	for len(allLines) > 0 && strings.TrimSpace(allLines[len(allLines)-1]) == "" {
   393→		allLines = allLines[:len(allLines)-1]
   394→	}
   395→	return len(allLines)
   396→}
   397→
   398→// extractUserPrompts extracts all user prompts from transcript content.
   399→// Returns prompts with IDE context tags stripped (e.g., <ide_opened_file>).
   400→func extractUserPrompts(agentType agent.AgentType, content string) []string {
   401→	if content == "" {
   402→		return nil
   403→	}
   404→
   405→	// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown
   406→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {
   407→		prompts, err := geminicli.ExtractAllUserPrompts([]byte(content))
   408→		if err == nil && len(prompts) > 0 {
   409→			// Strip IDE context tags for consistency with Claude Code handling
   410→			cleaned := make([]string, 0, len(prompts))
   411→			for _, prompt := range prompts {
   412→				if stripped := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(prompt); stripped != "" {
   413→					cleaned = append(cleaned, stripped)
   414→				}
   415→			}
   416→			return cleaned
   417→		}
   418→		// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return nil
   419→		if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   420→			return nil
   421→		}
   422→		// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type
   423→	}
   424→
   425→	// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents
   426→	return extractUserPromptsFromLines(strings.Split(content, "\n"))
   427→}
   428→
   429→// calculateTokenUsage calculates token usage from raw transcript data.
   430→// startOffset is the line number (Claude Code) or message index (Gemini CLI)
   431→// where the current checkpoint began, allowing calculation for only the portion
   432→// of the transcript since the last checkpoint.
   433→func calculateTokenUsage(agentType agent.AgentType, data []byte, startOffset int) *agent.TokenUsage {
   434→	if len(data) == 0 {
   435→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   436→	}
   437→
   438→	// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown
   439→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {
   440→		// Attempt to parse as Gemini JSON
   441→		transcript, err := geminicli.ParseTranscript(data)
   442→		if err == nil && transcript != nil && len(transcript.Messages) > 0 {
   443→			return geminicli.CalculateTokenUsage(data, startOffset)
   444→		}
   445→		// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return empty usage
   446→		if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   447→			return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   448→		}
   449→		// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type
   450→	}
   451→
   452→	// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents
   453→	lines, err := claudecode.ParseTranscript(data)
   454→	if err != nil || len(lines) == 0 {
   455→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   456→	}
   457→	// Slice transcript lines to only include checkpoint portion
   458→	if startOffset > 0 && startOffset < len(lines) {
   459→		lines = lines[startOffset:]
   460→	}
   461→	return claudecode.CalculateTokenUsage(lines)
   462→}
   463→
   464→// extractUserPromptsFromLines extracts user prompts from JSONL transcript lines.
   465→// IDE-injected context tags (like <ide_opened_file>) are stripped from the results.
   466→func extractUserPromptsFromLines(lines []string) []string {
   467→	var prompts []string
   468→	for _, line := range lines {
   469→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   470→		if line == "" {
   471→			continue
   472→		}
   473→
   474→		var entry map[string]interface{}
   475→		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry); err != nil {
   476→			continue
   477→		}
   478→
   479→		// Check for user message (supports both "human" and "user" types)
   480→		msgType, ok := entry["type"].(string)
   481→		if !ok || (msgType != "human" && msgType != "user") {
   482→			continue
   483→		}
   484→
   485→		// Extract message content
   486→		message, ok := entry["message"].(map[string]interface{})
   487→		if !ok {
   488→			continue
   489→		}
   490→
   491→		// Handle string content
   492→		if content, ok := message["content"].(string); ok && content != "" {
   493→			cleaned := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(content)
   494→			if cleaned != "" {
   495→				prompts = append(prompts, cleaned)
   496→			}
   497→			continue
   498→		}
   499→
   500→		// Handle array content (e.g., multiple text blocks from VSCode)
   501→		if arr, ok := message["content"].([]interface{}); ok {
   502→			var texts []string
   503→			for _, item := range arr {
   504→				if m, ok := item.(map[string]interface{}); ok {
   505→					if m["type"] == "text" {
   506→						if text, ok := m["text"].(string); ok {
   507→							texts = append(texts, text)
   508→						}
   509→					}
   510→				}
   511→			}
   512→			if len(texts) > 0 {
   513→				cleaned := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(strings.Join(texts, "\n\n"))
   514→				if cleaned != "" {
   515→					prompts = append(prompts, cleaned)
   516→				}
   517→			}
   518→		}
   519→	}
   520→	return prompts
   521→}
   522→
   523→// generateContextFromPrompts generates context.md content from a list of prompts.
   524→func generateContextFromPrompts(prompts []string) []byte {
   525→	if len(prompts) == 0 {
   526→		return nil
   527→	}
   528→
   529→	var buf strings.Builder
   530→	buf.WriteString("# Session Context\n\n")
   531→	buf.WriteString("## User Prompts\n\n")
   532→
   533→	for i, prompt := range prompts {
   534→		// Truncate very long prompts for readability
   535→		displayPrompt := prompt
   536→		if len(displayPrompt) > 500 {
   537→			displayPrompt = displayPrompt[:500] + "..."
   538→		}
   539→		buf.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("### Prompt %d\n\n", i+1))
   540→		buf.WriteString(displayPrompt)
   541→		buf.WriteString("\n\n")
   542→	}
   543→
   544→	return []byte(buf.String())
   545→}
   546→
   547→// CondenseSessionByID force-condenses a session by its ID and cleans up.
   548→// This is used by "entire doctor" to salvage stuck sessions.
   549→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CondenseSessionByID(sessionID string) error {
   550→	ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "condense-by-id")
   551→
   552→	// Load session state
   553→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   554→	if err != nil {
   555→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   556→	}
   557→	if state == nil {
   558→		return fmt.Errorf("session not found: %s", sessionID)
   559→	}
   560→
   561→	// Open repository
   562→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   563→	if err != nil {
   564→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   565→	}
   566→
   567→	// Generate a checkpoint ID
   568→	checkpointID, err := id.Generate()
   569→	if err != nil {
   570→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   571→	}
   572→
   573→	// Check if shadow branch exists (required for condensation)
   574→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   575→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   576→	_, refErr := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   577→	hasShadowBranch := refErr == nil
   578→
   579→	if !hasShadowBranch {
   580→		// No shadow branch means no checkpoint data to condense.
   581→		// Just clean up the state file.
   582→		logging.Info(ctx, "no shadow branch for session, clearing state only",
   583→			slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   584→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   585→		)
   586→		if err := s.clearSessionState(sessionID); err != nil {
   587→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to clear session state: %w", err)
   588→		}
   589→		return nil
   590→	}
   591→
   592→	// Condense the session
   593→	result, err := s.CondenseSession(repo, checkpointID, state)
   594→	if err != nil {
   595→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to condense session: %w", err)
   596→	}
   597→
   598→	logging.Info(ctx, "session condensed by ID",
   599→		slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   600→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", result.CheckpointID.String()),
   601→		slog.Int("checkpoints_condensed", result.CheckpointsCount),
   602→	)
   603→
   604→	// Update session state: reset step count and transition to idle
   605→	state.StepCount = 0
   606→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = result.TotalTranscriptLines
   607→	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
   608→	state.LastCheckpointID = checkpointID
   609→	state.PendingCheckpointID = "" // Clear after condensation (amend handler uses LastCheckpointID)
   610→	state.AttributionBaseCommit = state.BaseCommit
   611→	state.PromptAttributions = nil
   612→	state.PendingPromptAttribution = nil
   613→
   614→	if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   615→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   616→	}
   617→
   618→	// Clean up shadow branch if no other sessions need it
   619→	if err := s.cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused(repo, shadowBranchName, sessionID); err != nil {
   620→		logging.Warn(ctx, "failed to clean up shadow branch",
   621→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   622→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   623→		)
   624→		// Non-fatal: condensation succeeded, shadow branch cleanup is best-effort
   625→	}
   626→
   627→	return nil
   628→}
   629→
   630→// cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused deletes a shadow branch if no other active sessions reference it.
   631→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused(_ *git.Repository, shadowBranchName, excludeSessionID string) error {
   632→	// List all session states to check if any other session uses this shadow branch
   633→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   634→	if err != nil {
   635→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
   636→	}
   637→
   638→	for _, state := range allStates {
   639→		if state.SessionID == excludeSessionID {
   640→			continue
   641→		}
   642→		otherShadow := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   643→		if otherShadow == shadowBranchName && state.StepCount > 0 {
   644→			// Another session still needs this shadow branch
   645→			return nil
   646→		}
   647→	}
   648→
   649→	// No other sessions need it, delete the shadow branch via CLI
   650→	// (go-git v5's RemoveReference doesn't persist with packed refs/worktrees)
   651→	if err := DeleteBranchCLI(shadowBranchName); err != nil {
   652→		// Branch already gone is not an error
   653→		if errors.Is(err, ErrBranchNotFound) {
   654→			return nil
   655→		}
   656→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove shadow branch: %w", err)
   657→	}
   658→	return nil
   659→}
   660→

    21→// SaveChanges saves a checkpoint to the shadow branch.
    22→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.WriteTemporary for git operations.
    23→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) SaveChanges(ctx SaveContext) error {
    24→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
    25→	if err != nil {
    26→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
    27→	}
    28→
    29→	// Extract session ID from metadata dir
    30→	sessionID := filepath.Base(ctx.MetadataDir)
    31→
    32→	// Load or initialize session state
    33→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
    34→	if err != nil {
    35→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
    36→	}
    37→	// Initialize if state is nil OR BaseCommit is empty (can happen with partial state from warnings)
    38→	if state == nil || state.BaseCommit == "" {
    39→		agentType := resolveAgentType(ctx.AgentType, state)
    40→		state, err = s.initializeSession(repo, sessionID, agentType, "", "") // No transcript/prompt in fallback
    41→		if err != nil {
    42→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to initialize session: %w", err)
    43→		}
    44→	}
    45→
    46→	// Check if HEAD has changed (e.g., Claude did a rebase via tool call) and migrate if needed
    47→	if err := s.migrateAndPersistIfNeeded(repo, state); err != nil {
    48→		return err
    49→	}
    50→
    51→	// Get checkpoint store
    52→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    53→	if err != nil {
    54→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	// Check if shadow branch exists to report whether we created it
    58→	shadowBranchName := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
    59→	branchExisted := store.ShadowBranchExists(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
    60→
    61→	// Use the pending attribution calculated at prompt start (in InitializeSession)
    62→	// This was calculated BEFORE the agent made changes, so it accurately captures user edits
    63→	var promptAttr PromptAttribution
    64→	if state.PendingPromptAttribution != nil {
    65→		promptAttr = *state.PendingPromptAttribution
    66→		state.PendingPromptAttribution = nil // Clear after use
    67→	} else {
    68→		// No pending attribution (e.g., first checkpoint or session initialized without it)
    69→		promptAttr = PromptAttribution{CheckpointNumber: state.StepCount + 1}
    70→	}
    71→
    72→	// Log the prompt attribution for debugging
    73→	attrLogCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
    74→	logging.Debug(attrLogCtx, "prompt attribution at checkpoint save",
    75→		slog.Int("checkpoint_number", promptAttr.CheckpointNumber),
    76→		slog.Int("user_added", promptAttr.UserLinesAdded),
    77→		slog.Int("user_removed", promptAttr.UserLinesRemoved),
    78→		slog.Int("agent_added", promptAttr.AgentLinesAdded),
    79→		slog.Int("agent_removed", promptAttr.AgentLinesRemoved),
    80→		slog.String("session_id", sessionID))
    81→
    82→	// Use WriteTemporary to create the checkpoint
    83→	isFirstCheckpointOfSession := state.StepCount == 0
    84→	result, err := store.WriteTemporary(context.Background(), checkpoint.WriteTemporaryOptions{
    85→		SessionID:         sessionID,
    86→		BaseCommit:        state.BaseCommit,
    87→		WorktreeID:        state.WorktreeID,
    88→		ModifiedFiles:     ctx.ModifiedFiles,
    89→		NewFiles:          ctx.NewFiles,
    90→		DeletedFiles:      ctx.DeletedFiles,
    91→		MetadataDir:       ctx.MetadataDir,
    92→		MetadataDirAbs:    ctx.MetadataDirAbs,
    93→		CommitMessage:     ctx.CommitMessage,
    94→		AuthorName:        ctx.AuthorName,
    95→		AuthorEmail:       ctx.AuthorEmail,
    96→		IsFirstCheckpoint: isFirstCheckpointOfSession,
    97→	})
    98→	if err != nil {
    99→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write temporary checkpoint: %w", err)
   100→	}
   101→
   102→	// If checkpoint was skipped due to deduplication (no changes), return early
   103→	if result.Skipped {
   104→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   105→		logging.Info(logCtx, "checkpoint skipped (no changes)",
   106→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   107→			slog.String("checkpoint_type", "session"),
   108→			slog.Int("checkpoint_count", state.StepCount),
   109→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   110→		)
   111→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipped checkpoint (no changes since last checkpoint)\n")
   112→		return nil
   113→	}
   114→
   115→	// Update session state
   116→	state.StepCount++
   117→
   118→	// Note: PendingCheckpointID is intentionally NOT cleared here.
   119→	// It is set by PostCommit (ACTIVE → ACTIVE_COMMITTED) and consumed by
   120→	// handleTurnEndCondense. Clearing it here would cause a mismatch between
   121→	// the checkpoint ID in the commit trailer and the condensed metadata.
   122→
   123→	// Store the prompt attribution we calculated before saving
   124→	state.PromptAttributions = append(state.PromptAttributions, promptAttr)
   125→
   126→	// Track touched files (modified, new, and deleted)
   127→	state.FilesTouched = mergeFilesTouched(state.FilesTouched, ctx.ModifiedFiles, ctx.NewFiles, ctx.DeletedFiles)
   128→
   129→	// On first checkpoint, record the transcript identifier for this session
   130→	if state.StepCount == 1 {
   131→		state.TranscriptIdentifierAtStart = ctx.StepTranscriptIdentifier
   132→	}
   133→
   134→	// Accumulate token usage
   135→	if ctx.TokenUsage != nil {
   136→		state.TokenUsage = accumulateTokenUsage(state.TokenUsage, ctx.TokenUsage)
   137→	}
   138→
   139→	// Save updated state
   140→	if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   141→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   142→	}
   143→
   144→	if !branchExisted {
   145→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created shadow branch '%s' and committed changes\n", shadowBranchName)
   146→	} else {
   147→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed changes to shadow branch '%s'\n", shadowBranchName)
   148→	}
   149→
   150→	// Log checkpoint creation
   151→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   152→	logging.Info(logCtx, "checkpoint saved",
   153→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   154→		slog.String("checkpoint_type", "session"),
   155→		slog.Int("checkpoint_count", state.StepCount),
   156→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(ctx.ModifiedFiles)),
   157→		slog.Int("new_files", len(ctx.NewFiles)),
   158→		slog.Int("deleted_files", len(ctx.DeletedFiles)),
   159→		slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   160→		slog.Bool("branch_created", !branchExisted),
   161→	)
   162→
   163→	return nil
   164→}

   166→// SaveTaskCheckpoint saves a task checkpoint to the shadow branch.
   167→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.WriteTemporaryTask for git operations.
   168→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx TaskCheckpointContext) error {
   169→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   170→	if err != nil {
   171→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   172→	}
   173→
   174→	// Load session state
   175→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(ctx.SessionID)
   176→	if err != nil || state == nil || state.BaseCommit == "" {
   177→		agentType := resolveAgentType(ctx.AgentType, state)
   178→		state, err = s.initializeSession(repo, ctx.SessionID, agentType, "", "") // No transcript/prompt in fallback
   179→		if err != nil {
   180→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to initialize session for task checkpoint: %w", err)
   181→		}
   182→	}
   183→
   184→	// Check if HEAD has changed (e.g., Claude did a rebase via tool call) and migrate if needed
   185→	if err := s.migrateAndPersistIfNeeded(repo, state); err != nil {
   186→		return err
   187→	}
   188→
   189→	// Get checkpoint store
   190→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   191→	if err != nil {
   192→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   193→	}
   194→
   195→	// Check if shadow branch exists to report whether we created it
   196→	shadowBranchName := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   197→	branchExisted := store.ShadowBranchExists(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   198→
   199→	// Compute metadata paths for commit message
   200→	sessionMetadataDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(ctx.SessionID)
   201→	taskMetadataDir := TaskMetadataDir(sessionMetadataDir, ctx.ToolUseID)
   202→
   203→	// Generate commit message
   204→	shortToolUseID := ctx.ToolUseID
   205→	if len(shortToolUseID) > 12 {
   206→		shortToolUseID = shortToolUseID[:12]
   207→	}
   208→
   209→	var messageSubject string
   210→	if ctx.IsIncremental {
   211→		messageSubject = FormatIncrementalSubject(
   212→			ctx.IncrementalType,
   213→			ctx.SubagentType,
   214→			ctx.TaskDescription,
   215→			ctx.TodoContent,
   216→			ctx.IncrementalSequence,
   217→			shortToolUseID,
   218→		)
   219→	} else {
   220→		messageSubject = FormatSubagentEndMessage(ctx.SubagentType, ctx.TaskDescription, shortToolUseID)
   221→	}
   222→	commitMsg := trailers.FormatShadowTaskCommit(
   223→		messageSubject,
   224→		taskMetadataDir,
   225→		ctx.SessionID,
   226→	)
   227→
   228→	// Use WriteTemporaryTask to create the checkpoint
   229→	_, err = store.WriteTemporaryTask(context.Background(), checkpoint.WriteTemporaryTaskOptions{
   230→		SessionID:              ctx.SessionID,
   231→		BaseCommit:             state.BaseCommit,
   232→		WorktreeID:             state.WorktreeID,
   233→		ToolUseID:              ctx.ToolUseID,
   234→		AgentID:                ctx.AgentID,
   235→		ModifiedFiles:          ctx.ModifiedFiles,
   236→		NewFiles:               ctx.NewFiles,
   237→		DeletedFiles:           ctx.DeletedFiles,
   238→		TranscriptPath:         ctx.TranscriptPath,
   239→		SubagentTranscriptPath: ctx.SubagentTranscriptPath,
   240→		CheckpointUUID:         ctx.CheckpointUUID,
   241→		CommitMessage:          commitMsg,
   242→		AuthorName:             ctx.AuthorName,
   243→		AuthorEmail:            ctx.AuthorEmail,
   244→		IsIncremental:          ctx.IsIncremental,
   245→		IncrementalSequence:    ctx.IncrementalSequence,
   246→		IncrementalType:        ctx.IncrementalType,
   247→		IncrementalData:        ctx.IncrementalData,
   248→	})
   249→	if err != nil {
   250→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write task checkpoint: %w", err)
   251→	}
   252→
   253→	// Track touched files (modified, new, and deleted)
   254→	state.FilesTouched = mergeFilesTouched(state.FilesTouched, ctx.ModifiedFiles, ctx.NewFiles, ctx.DeletedFiles)
   255→
   256→	// Save updated state
   257→	if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   258→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   259→	}
   260→
   261→	if !branchExisted {
   262→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created shadow branch '%s' and committed task checkpoint\n", shadowBranchName)
   263→	} else {
   264→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed task checkpoint to shadow branch '%s'\n", shadowBranchName)
   265→	}
   266→
   267→	// Log task checkpoint creation
   268→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   269→	attrs := []any{
   270→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   271→		slog.String("checkpoint_type", "task"),
   272→		slog.String("checkpoint_uuid", ctx.CheckpointUUID),
   273→		slog.String("tool_use_id", ctx.ToolUseID),
   274→		slog.String("subagent_type", ctx.SubagentType),
   275→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(ctx.ModifiedFiles)),
   276→		slog.Int("new_files", len(ctx.NewFiles)),
   277→		slog.Int("deleted_files", len(ctx.DeletedFiles)),
   278→		slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   279→		slog.Bool("branch_created", !branchExisted),
   280→	}
   281→	if ctx.IsIncremental {
   282→		attrs = append(attrs,
   283→			slog.Bool("is_incremental", true),
   284→			slog.String("incremental_type", ctx.IncrementalType),
   285→			slog.Int("incremental_sequence", ctx.IncrementalSequence),
   286→		)
   287→	}
   288→	logging.Info(logCtx, "task checkpoint saved", attrs...)
   289→
   290→	return nil
   291→}
   292→
   293→// mergeFilesTouched merges multiple file lists into existing touched files, deduplicating.
   294→func mergeFilesTouched(existing []string, fileLists ...[]string) []string {
   295→	seen := make(map[string]bool)
   296→	for _, f := range existing {
   297→		seen[f] = true
   298→	}
   299→
   300→	for _, list := range fileLists {
   301→		for _, f := range list {
   302→			seen[f] = true
   303→		}
   304→	}
   305→
   306→	result := make([]string, 0, len(seen))
   307→	for f := range seen {
   308→		result = append(result, f)
   309→	}
   310→
   311→	// Sort for deterministic output
   312→	sort.Strings(result)
   313→	return result
   314→}
   315→
   316→// accumulateTokenUsage adds new token usage to existing accumulated usage.
   317→// If existing is nil, returns a copy of incoming. If incoming is nil, returns existing unchanged.
   318→func accumulateTokenUsage(existing, incoming *agent.TokenUsage) *agent.TokenUsage {
   319→	if incoming == nil {
   320→		return existing
   321→	}
   322→	if existing == nil {
   323→		// Return a copy to avoid sharing the pointer
   324→		return &agent.TokenUsage{
   325→			InputTokens:         incoming.InputTokens,
   326→			CacheCreationTokens: incoming.CacheCreationTokens,
   327→			CacheReadTokens:     incoming.CacheReadTokens,
   328→			OutputTokens:        incoming.OutputTokens,
   329→			APICallCount:        incoming.APICallCount,
   330→			SubagentTokens:      incoming.SubagentTokens,
   331→		}
   332→	}
   333→
   334→	// Accumulate values
   335→	existing.InputTokens += incoming.InputTokens
   336→	existing.CacheCreationTokens += incoming.CacheCreationTokens
   337→	existing.CacheReadTokens += incoming.CacheReadTokens
   338→	existing.OutputTokens += incoming.OutputTokens
   339→	existing.APICallCount += incoming.APICallCount
   340→
   341→	// Accumulate subagent tokens if present
   342→	if incoming.SubagentTokens != nil {
   343→		existing.SubagentTokens = accumulateTokenUsage(existing.SubagentTokens, incoming.SubagentTokens)
   344→	}
   345→
   346→	return existing
   347→}
   348→
   349→// deleteShadowBranch deletes a shadow branch by name.
   350→// Returns nil if the branch doesn't exist (idempotent).
   351→// Uses git CLI instead of go-git's RemoveReference because go-git v5
   352→// doesn't properly persist deletions with packed refs or worktrees.
   353→func deleteShadowBranch(_ *git.Repository, branchName string) error {
   354→	err := DeleteBranchCLI(branchName)
   355→	if err != nil {
   356→		// If the branch doesn't exist, treat as idempotent - not an error condition.
   357→		if errors.Is(err, ErrBranchNotFound) {
   358→			return nil
   359→		}
   360→		return err
   361→	}
   362→	return nil
   363→}

    11→func TestPhaseFromString(t *testing.T) {

    13→
    14→	tests := []struct {
    15→		name  string
    16→		input string
    17→		want  Phase
    18→	}{
    19→		{name: "active", input: "active", want: PhaseActive},
    20→		{name: "active_committed", input: "active_committed", want: PhaseActiveCommitted},
    21→		{name: "idle", input: "idle", want: PhaseIdle},
    22→		{name: "ended", input: "ended", want: PhaseEnded},
    23→		{name: "empty_string_defaults_to_idle", input: "", want: PhaseIdle},
    24→		{name: "unknown_string_defaults_to_idle", input: "bogus", want: PhaseIdle},
    25→		{name: "uppercase_treated_as_unknown", input: "ACTIVE", want: PhaseIdle},

   105→// transitionCase is a single row in the transition table test.
   106→type transitionCase struct {

   114→
   115→// runTransitionTests runs a slice of transition cases as parallel subtests.
   116→func runTransitionTests(t *testing.T, tests []transitionCase) {
   117→	t.Helper()
   118→	for _, tt := range tests {
   119→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   120→			t.Parallel()
   121→			result := Transition(tt.current, tt.event, tt.ctx)
   122→			assert.Equal(t, tt.wantPhase, result.NewPhase, "unexpected NewPhase")
   123→			assert.Equal(t, tt.wantActions, result.Actions, "unexpected Actions")
   124→		})
   125→	}
   126→}

   380→func TestTransition_rebase_GitCommit_is_noop_for_all_phases(t *testing.T) {
   381→	t.Parallel()
   382→
   383→	rebaseCtx := TransitionContext{IsRebaseInProgress: true}
   384→
   385→	for _, phase := range allPhases {
   386→		result := Transition(phase, EventGitCommit, rebaseCtx)
   387→		assert.Empty(t, result.Actions,
   388→			"rebase should produce empty actions for phase %s", phase)
   389→		assert.Equal(t, phase, result.NewPhase,
   390→			"rebase should not change phase for %s", phase)
   391→	}
   392→}
   393→
   394→func TestTransition_all_phase_event_combinations_are_defined(t *testing.T) {
   395→	t.Parallel()
   396→
   397→	// Verify that calling Transition for every (phase, event) combination
   398→	// does not panic and returns a valid phase.
   399→	for _, phase := range allPhases {
   400→		for _, event := range allEvents {
   401→			result := Transition(phase, event, TransitionContext{})
   402→			assert.NotEmpty(t, string(result.NewPhase),
   403→				"Transition(%s, %s) returned empty phase", phase, event)
   404→
   405→			// Verify the resulting phase is a known phase.
   406→			normalized := PhaseFromString(string(result.NewPhase))
   407→			assert.Equal(t, result.NewPhase, normalized,
   408→				"Transition(%s, %s) returned non-canonical phase %q",
   409→				phase, event, result.NewPhase)
   410→		}
   411→	}
   412→}

    23→// TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_NoCondensation verifies that PostCommit on an
    24→// ACTIVE session transitions to ACTIVE_COMMITTED without condensing.
    25→// The shadow branch must be preserved because the session is still active.
    26→func TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_NoCondensation(t *testing.T) {
    27→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
    28→	t.Chdir(dir)
    29→
    30→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
    31→	require.NoError(t, err)
    32→
    33→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
    34→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-active"
    35→
    36→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint so there is shadow branch content
    37→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
    38→
    39→	// Set phase to ACTIVE (simulating agent mid-turn)
    40→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
    41→	require.NoError(t, err)
    42→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
    43→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
    44→
    45→	// Create a commit WITH the Entire-Checkpoint trailer on the main branch
    46→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "a1b2c3d4e5f6")
    47→
    48→	// Run PostCommit
    49→	err = s.PostCommit()
    50→	require.NoError(t, err)
    51→
    52→	// Verify phase transitioned to ACTIVE_COMMITTED
    53→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
    54→	require.NoError(t, err)
    55→	require.NotNil(t, state)
    56→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase,
    57→		"ACTIVE session should transition to ACTIVE_COMMITTED on GitCommit")
    58→
    59→	// Verify shadow branch is NOT deleted (session is still active).
    60→	// After PostCommit, BaseCommit is updated to new HEAD, so use the current state.
    61→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
    62→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
    63→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
    64→	assert.NoError(t, err,
    65→		"shadow branch should be preserved when session is still active")
    66→}
    67→
    68→// TestPostCommit_IdleSession_Condenses verifies that PostCommit on an IDLE
    69→// session condenses session data and cleans up the shadow branch.
    70→func TestPostCommit_IdleSession_Condenses(t *testing.T) {
    71→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
    72→	t.Chdir(dir)
    73→
    74→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
    75→	require.NoError(t, err)
    76→
    77→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
    78→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-idle"
    79→
    80→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint so there is shadow branch content
    81→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
    82→
    83→	// Set phase to IDLE (agent turn finished, waiting for user)
    84→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
    85→	require.NoError(t, err)
    86→	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
    87→	state.LastInteractionTime = nil
    88→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
    89→
    90→	// Record shadow branch name before PostCommit
    91→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
    92→
    93→	// Create a commit WITH the Entire-Checkpoint trailer
    94→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "b2c3d4e5f6a1")
    95→
    96→	// Run PostCommit
    97→	err = s.PostCommit()
    98→	require.NoError(t, err)
    99→
   100→	// Verify condensation happened: the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist
   101→	sessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   102→	require.NoError(t, err, "entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist after condensation")
   103→	assert.NotNil(t, sessionsRef)
   104→
   105→	// Verify shadow branch IS deleted after condensation
   106→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   107→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   108→	assert.Error(t, err,
   109→		"shadow branch should be deleted after condensation for IDLE session")
   110→}
   111→
   112→// TestPostCommit_RebaseDuringActive_SkipsTransition verifies that PostCommit
   113→// is a no-op during rebase operations, leaving the session phase unchanged.
   114→func TestPostCommit_RebaseDuringActive_SkipsTransition(t *testing.T) {
   115→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   116→	t.Chdir(dir)
   117→
   118→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   119→	require.NoError(t, err)
   120→
   121→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   122→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-rebase"
   123→
   124→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   125→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   126→
   127→	// Set phase to ACTIVE
   128→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   129→	require.NoError(t, err)
   130→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   131→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   132→
   133→	// Capture shadow branch name BEFORE any state changes
   134→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   135→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   136→
   137→	// Simulate rebase in progress by creating .git/rebase-merge/ directory
   138→	gitDir := filepath.Join(dir, ".git")
   139→	rebaseMergeDir := filepath.Join(gitDir, "rebase-merge")
   140→	require.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(rebaseMergeDir, 0o755))
   141→	defer os.RemoveAll(rebaseMergeDir)
   142→
   143→	// Create a commit WITH the checkpoint trailer
   144→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "c3d4e5f6a1b2")
   145→
   146→	// Run PostCommit
   147→	err = s.PostCommit()
   148→	require.NoError(t, err)
   149→
   150→	// Verify phase stayed ACTIVE (no transition during rebase)
   151→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   152→	require.NoError(t, err)
   153→	require.NotNil(t, state)
   154→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActive, state.Phase,
   155→		"session should stay ACTIVE during rebase (no transition)")
   156→
   157→	// Verify StepCount was NOT reset (no condensation happened)
   158→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   159→		"StepCount should be unchanged - no condensation during rebase")
   160→
   161→	// Verify NO condensation happened (entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should not exist)
   162→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   163→	require.Error(t, err,
   164→		"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should NOT exist - no condensation during rebase")
   165→
   166→	// Verify shadow branch still exists (not cleaned up during rebase)
   167→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   168→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   169→	assert.NoError(t, err,
   170→		"shadow branch should be preserved during rebase")
   171→}
   172→
   173→// TestPostCommit_ShadowBranch_PreservedWhenActiveSessionExists verifies that
   174→// the shadow branch is preserved when ANY session on it is still active,
   175→// even if another session on the same branch is IDLE and gets condensed.
   176→func TestPostCommit_ShadowBranch_PreservedWhenActiveSessionExists(t *testing.T) {
   177→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   178→	t.Chdir(dir)
   179→
   180→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   181→	require.NoError(t, err)
   182→
   183→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   184→	idleSessionID := "test-postcommit-idle-multi"
   185→	activeSessionID := "test-postcommit-active-multi"
   186→
   187→	// Initialize the idle session with a checkpoint
   188→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, idleSessionID)
   189→
   190→	// Get worktree path and base commit from the idle session
   191→	idleState, err := s.loadSessionState(idleSessionID)
   192→	require.NoError(t, err)
   193→	worktreePath := idleState.WorktreePath
   194→	baseCommit := idleState.BaseCommit
   195→	worktreeID := idleState.WorktreeID
   196→
   197→	// Set idle session to IDLE phase
   198→	idleState.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
   199→	idleState.LastInteractionTime = nil
   200→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(idleState))
   201→
   202→	// Create a second session with the SAME base commit and worktree (concurrent session)
   203→	// Save the active session with ACTIVE phase and some checkpoints
   204→	now := time.Now()
   205→	activeState := &SessionState{
   206→		SessionID:           activeSessionID,
   207→		BaseCommit:          baseCommit,
   208→		WorktreePath:        worktreePath,
   209→		WorktreeID:          worktreeID,
   210→		StartedAt:           now,
   211→		Phase:               session.PhaseActive,
   212→		LastInteractionTime: &now,
   213→		StepCount:           1,
   214→	}
   215→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(activeState))
   216→
   217→	// Create a commit WITH the checkpoint trailer
   218→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "d4e5f6a1b2c3")
   219→
   220→	// Run PostCommit
   221→	err = s.PostCommit()
   222→	require.NoError(t, err)
   223→
   224→	// Verify the ACTIVE session's phase is now ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   225→	activeState, err = s.loadSessionState(activeSessionID)
   226→	require.NoError(t, err)
   227→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, activeState.Phase,
   228→		"ACTIVE session should transition to ACTIVE_COMMITTED on GitCommit")
   229→
   230→	// Verify the IDLE session actually condensed (entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist)
   231→	idleState, err = s.loadSessionState(idleSessionID)
   232→	require.NoError(t, err)
   233→	sessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   234→	require.NoError(t, err, "entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist after IDLE session condensation")
   235→	require.NotNil(t, sessionsRef)
   236→
   237→	// Verify IDLE session's StepCount was reset by condensation
   238→	assert.Equal(t, 0, idleState.StepCount,
   239→		"IDLE session StepCount should be reset after condensation")
   240→
   241→	// Verify shadow branch is NOT deleted because the ACTIVE session still needs it.
   242→	// After PostCommit, BaseCommit is updated to new HEAD via migration.
   243→	newShadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(activeState.BaseCommit, activeState.WorktreeID)
   244→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(newShadowBranch)
   245→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   246→	assert.NoError(t, err,
   247→		"shadow branch should be preserved when an active session still exists on it")
   248→}
   249→
   250→// TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch verifies that when
   251→// condensation fails (corrupted shadow branch), BaseCommit is NOT updated.
   252→func TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {
   253→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   254→	t.Chdir(dir)
   255→
   256→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   257→	require.NoError(t, err)
   258→
   259→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   260→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-condense-fail-idle"
   261→
   262→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   263→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   264→
   265→	// Set phase to IDLE
   266→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   267→	require.NoError(t, err)
   268→	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
   269→	state.LastInteractionTime = nil
   270→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   271→
   272→	// Record original BaseCommit and StepCount before corruption
   273→	originalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit
   274→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   275→
   276→	// Corrupt shadow branch by pointing it at ZeroHash
   277→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   278→	corruptRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch), plumbing.ZeroHash)
   279→	require.NoError(t, repo.Storer.SetReference(corruptRef))
   280→
   281→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   282→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "e5f6a1b2c3d4")
   283→
   284→	// Run PostCommit
   285→	err = s.PostCommit()
   286→	require.NoError(t, err, "PostCommit should not return error even when condensation fails")
   287→
   288→	// Verify BaseCommit was NOT updated (condensation failed)
   289→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   290→	require.NoError(t, err)
   291→	assert.Equal(t, originalBaseCommit, state.BaseCommit,
   292→		"BaseCommit should NOT be updated when condensation fails")
   293→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   294→		"StepCount should NOT be reset when condensation fails")
   295→
   296→	// Verify entire/checkpoints/v1 branch does NOT exist (condensation failed)
   297→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   298→	require.Error(t, err,
   299→		"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should NOT exist when condensation fails")
   300→
   301→	// Phase transition still applies even when condensation fails
   302→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseIdle, state.Phase,
   303→		"phase should remain IDLE when condensation fails")
   304→}
   305→
   306→// TestPostCommit_IdleSession_NoNewContent_UpdatesBaseCommit verifies that when
   307→// an IDLE session has no new transcript content since last condensation,
   308→// PostCommit skips condensation but still updates BaseCommit.
   309→func TestPostCommit_IdleSession_NoNewContent_UpdatesBaseCommit(t *testing.T) {
   310→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   311→	t.Chdir(dir)
   312→
   313→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   314→	require.NoError(t, err)
   315→
   316→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   317→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-idle-no-content"
   318→
   319→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   320→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   321→
   322→	// Set phase to IDLE with CheckpointTranscriptStart matching transcript length (2 lines)
   323→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   324→	require.NoError(t, err)
   325→	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
   326→	state.LastInteractionTime = nil
   327→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 2 // Transcript has exactly 2 lines
   328→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   329→
   330→	// Record shadow branch name
   331→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   332→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   333→
   334→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   335→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "f6a1b2c3d4e5")
   336→
   337→	// Get new HEAD hash for comparison
   338→	head, err := repo.Head()
   339→	require.NoError(t, err)
   340→	newHeadHash := head.Hash().String()
   341→
   342→	// Run PostCommit
   343→	err = s.PostCommit()
   344→	require.NoError(t, err)
   345→
   346→	// Verify BaseCommit was updated to new HEAD
   347→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   348→	require.NoError(t, err)
   349→	assert.Equal(t, newHeadHash, state.BaseCommit,
   350→		"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD when no new content")
   351→
   352→	// Shadow branch should still exist (not deleted, no condensation)
   353→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   354→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   355→	require.NoError(t, err,
   356→		"shadow branch should still exist when no condensation happened")
   357→
   358→	// entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should NOT exist (no condensation)
   359→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   360→	require.Error(t, err,
   361→		"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should NOT exist when no condensation happened")
   362→
   363→	// StepCount should be unchanged
   364→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   365→		"StepCount should be unchanged when no condensation happened")
   366→}

   368→// TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_Condenses verifies that an ENDED
   369→// session with files touched and new content condenses on commit.
   370→func TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_Condenses(t *testing.T) {
   371→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   372→	t.Chdir(dir)
   373→
   374→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   375→	require.NoError(t, err)
   376→
   377→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   378→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-ended-condenses"
   379→
   380→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   381→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   382→
   383→	// Set phase to ENDED with files touched
   384→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   385→	require.NoError(t, err)
   386→	now := time.Now()
   387→	state.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
   388→	state.EndedAt = &now
   389→	state.FilesTouched = []string{"test.txt"}
   390→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   391→
   392→	// Record shadow branch name
   393→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   394→
   395→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   396→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "a1b2c3d4e5f7")
   397→
   398→	// Run PostCommit
   399→	err = s.PostCommit()
   400→	require.NoError(t, err)
   401→
   402→	// Verify entire/checkpoints/v1 branch exists (condensation happened)
   403→	sessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   404→	require.NoError(t, err, "entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist after condensation")
   405→	assert.NotNil(t, sessionsRef)
   406→
   407→	// Verify old shadow branch is deleted after condensation
   408→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   409→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   410→	require.Error(t, err,
   411→		"shadow branch should be deleted after condensation for ENDED session")
   412→
   413→	// Verify StepCount was reset by condensation
   414→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   415→	require.NoError(t, err)
   416→	assert.Equal(t, 0, state.StepCount,
   417→		"StepCount should be reset after condensation")
   418→
   419→	// Phase stays ENDED
   420→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseEnded, state.Phase,
   421→		"ENDED session should stay ENDED after condensation")
   422→}

   424→// TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_NoNewContent verifies that an ENDED
   425→// session with files touched but no new transcript content skips condensation
   426→// and updates BaseCommit via fallthrough.
   427→func TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_NoNewContent(t *testing.T) {
   428→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   429→	t.Chdir(dir)
   430→
   431→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   432→	require.NoError(t, err)
   433→
   434→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   435→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-ended-no-content"
   436→
   437→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   438→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   439→
   440→	// Set phase to ENDED with files touched but no new content
   441→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   442→	require.NoError(t, err)
   443→	now := time.Now()
   444→	state.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
   445→	state.EndedAt = &now
   446→	state.FilesTouched = []string{"test.txt"}
   447→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 2 // Transcript has exactly 2 lines
   448→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   449→
   450→	// Record shadow branch name and original StepCount
   451→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   452→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   453→
   454→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   455→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "b2c3d4e5f6a2")
   456→
   457→	// Get new HEAD hash
   458→	head, err := repo.Head()
   459→	require.NoError(t, err)
   460→	newHeadHash := head.Hash().String()
   461→
   462→	// Run PostCommit
   463→	err = s.PostCommit()
   464→	require.NoError(t, err)
   465→
   466→	// Verify entire/checkpoints/v1 branch does NOT exist (no condensation)
   467→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   468→	require.Error(t, err,
   469→		"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should NOT exist when no new content")
   470→
   471→	// Shadow branch should still exist
   472→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   473→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   474→	require.NoError(t, err,
   475→		"shadow branch should still exist when no condensation happened")
   476→
   477→	// BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD via fallthrough
   478→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   479→	require.NoError(t, err)
   480→	assert.Equal(t, newHeadHash, state.BaseCommit,
   481→		"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD via fallthrough")
   482→
   483→	// StepCount unchanged
   484→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   485→		"StepCount should be unchanged when no condensation happened")
   486→}

   488→// TestPostCommit_EndedSession_NoFilesTouched_Discards verifies that an ENDED
   489→// session with no files touched takes the discard path, updating BaseCommit.
   490→func TestPostCommit_EndedSession_NoFilesTouched_Discards(t *testing.T) {

   507→	state.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
   508→	state.EndedAt = &now

   510→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))

   512→	// Record original StepCount
   513→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   514→
   515→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   516→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "c3d4e5f6a1b3")
   517→
   518→	// Get new HEAD hash
   519→	head, err := repo.Head()
   520→	require.NoError(t, err)
   521→	newHeadHash := head.Hash().String()
   522→
   523→	// Run PostCommit

   526→
   527→	// Verify entire/checkpoints/v1 branch does NOT exist (no condensation for discard path)
   528→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   529→	require.Error(t, err,

   532→	// BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD
