   537→
   538→	// StepCount unchanged
   539→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   540→		"StepCount should be unchanged on discard path")
   541→
   542→	// Phase stays ENDED
   543→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseEnded, state.Phase,
   544→		"ENDED session should stay ENDED on discard path")
   545→}
   546→
   547→// TestPostCommit_ActiveCommitted_MigratesShadowBranch verifies that an
   548→// ACTIVE_COMMITTED session receiving another commit migrates the shadow branch
   549→// to the new HEAD and stays in ACTIVE_COMMITTED.
   550→func TestPostCommit_ActiveCommitted_MigratesShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {
   551→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   552→	t.Chdir(dir)
   553→
   554→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   555→	require.NoError(t, err)
   556→
   557→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   558→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-ac-migrate"
   559→
   560→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   561→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   562→
   563→	// Set phase to ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   564→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   565→	require.NoError(t, err)
   566→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActiveCommitted
   567→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   568→
   569→	// Record original shadow branch name and BaseCommit
   570→	originalShadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   571→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   572→
   573→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   574→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "d4e5f6a1b2c4")
   575→
   576→	// Run PostCommit
   577→	err = s.PostCommit()
   578→	require.NoError(t, err)
   579→
   580→	// Verify phase stays ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   581→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   582→	require.NoError(t, err)
   583→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase,
   584→		"ACTIVE_COMMITTED session should stay ACTIVE_COMMITTED on subsequent commit")
   585→
   586→	// Verify BaseCommit updated to new HEAD
   587→	head, err := repo.Head()
   588→	require.NoError(t, err)
   589→	assert.Equal(t, head.Hash().String(), state.BaseCommit,
   590→		"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD after migration")
   591→
   592→	// Verify new shadow branch exists at new HEAD hash
   593→	newShadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   594→	newRefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(newShadowBranch)
   595→	_, err = repo.Reference(newRefName, true)
   596→	require.NoError(t, err,
   597→		"new shadow branch should exist after migration")
   598→
   599→	// Verify original shadow branch no longer exists (was migrated/renamed)
   600→	oldRefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(originalShadowBranch)
   601→	_, err = repo.Reference(oldRefName, true)
   602→	require.Error(t, err,
   603→		"original shadow branch should no longer exist after migration")
   604→
   605→	// StepCount unchanged (no condensation)
   606→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   607→		"StepCount should be unchanged - no condensation for ACTIVE_COMMITTED")
   608→
   609→	// entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should NOT exist (no condensation)
   610→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   611→	require.Error(t, err,
   612→		"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should NOT exist - no condensation for ACTIVE_COMMITTED")
   613→}
   614→
   615→// TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_EndedSession_PreservesShadowBranch verifies
   616→// that when condensation fails for an ENDED session with files touched,
   617→// BaseCommit is preserved (not updated).
   618→func TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_EndedSession_PreservesShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {
   619→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   620→	t.Chdir(dir)
   621→
   622→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   623→	require.NoError(t, err)
   624→
   625→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   626→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-condense-fail-ended"
   627→
   628→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   629→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   630→
   631→	// Set phase to ENDED with files touched
   632→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   633→	require.NoError(t, err)
   634→	now := time.Now()
   635→	state.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
   636→	state.EndedAt = &now
   637→	state.FilesTouched = []string{"test.txt"}
   638→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   639→
   640→	// Record original BaseCommit and StepCount
   641→	originalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit
   642→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   643→
   644→	// Corrupt shadow branch by pointing it at ZeroHash
   645→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   646→	corruptRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch), plumbing.ZeroHash)
   647→	require.NoError(t, repo.Storer.SetReference(corruptRef))
   648→
   649→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   650→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "e5f6a1b2c3d5")
   651→
   652→	// Run PostCommit
   653→	err = s.PostCommit()
   654→	require.NoError(t, err, "PostCommit should not return error even when condensation fails")
   655→
   656→	// Verify BaseCommit was NOT updated (condensation failed)
   657→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   658→	require.NoError(t, err)
   659→	assert.Equal(t, originalBaseCommit, state.BaseCommit,
   660→		"BaseCommit should NOT be updated when condensation fails for ENDED session")
   661→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   662→		"StepCount should NOT be reset when condensation fails for ENDED session")
   663→
   664→	// Verify entire/checkpoints/v1 branch does NOT exist (condensation failed)
   665→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   666→	require.Error(t, err,
   667→		"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should NOT exist when condensation fails")
   668→
   669→	// Phase stays ENDED
   670→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseEnded, state.Phase,
   671→		"ENDED session should stay ENDED when condensation fails")
   672→}
   673→
   674→// TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_SetsPendingCheckpointID verifies that PostCommit
   675→// stores PendingCheckpointID when transitioning ACTIVE → ACTIVE_COMMITTED.
   676→// This ensures HandleTurnEnd can reuse the same checkpoint ID that's in the
   677→// commit trailer, rather than generating a mismatched one.
   678→func TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_SetsPendingCheckpointID(t *testing.T) {
   679→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   680→	t.Chdir(dir)
   681→
   682→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   683→	require.NoError(t, err)
   684→
   685→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   686→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-pending-cpid"
   687→
   688→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   689→
   690→	// Set phase to ACTIVE (agent mid-turn)
   691→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   692→	require.NoError(t, err)
   693→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   694→	state.PendingCheckpointID = "" // Ensure it starts empty
   695→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   696→
   697→	// Create a commit with a known checkpoint ID
   698→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "a1b2c3d4e5f6")
   699→
   700→	// Run PostCommit
   701→	err = s.PostCommit()
   702→	require.NoError(t, err)
   703→
   704→	// Verify phase transitioned to ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   705→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   706→	require.NoError(t, err)
   707→	require.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase)
   708→
   709→	// Verify PendingCheckpointID was stored from the commit trailer
   710→	assert.Equal(t, "a1b2c3d4e5f6", state.PendingCheckpointID,
   711→		"PendingCheckpointID should be set to the commit's checkpoint ID for deferred condensation")
   712→}
   713→
   714→// TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_ReusesCheckpointID verifies that HandleTurnEnd
   715→// uses PendingCheckpointID (set by PostCommit) rather than generating a new one.
   716→// This ensures the condensed metadata matches the commit trailer.
   717→func TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_ReusesCheckpointID(t *testing.T) {
   718→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   719→	t.Chdir(dir)
   720→
   721→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   722→	require.NoError(t, err)
   723→
   724→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   725→	sessionID := "test-turnend-reuses-cpid"
   726→
   727→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   728→
   729→	// Simulate PostCommit: transition to ACTIVE_COMMITTED with PendingCheckpointID
   730→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   731→	require.NoError(t, err)
   732→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   733→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   734→
   735→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "d4e5f6a1b2c3")
   736→
   737→	err = s.PostCommit()
   738→	require.NoError(t, err)
   739→
   740→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   741→	require.NoError(t, err)
   742→	require.Equal(t, "d4e5f6a1b2c3", state.PendingCheckpointID)
   743→
   744→	// Run TurnEnd
   745→	result := session.Transition(state.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
   746→	remaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(state, result)
   747→
   748→	err = s.HandleTurnEnd(state, remaining)
   749→	require.NoError(t, err)
   750→
   751→	// Verify the condensed checkpoint ID matches the commit trailer
   752→	// The LastCheckpointID is set by condenseAndUpdateState on success
   753→	assert.Equal(t, id.CheckpointID("d4e5f6a1b2c3"), state.LastCheckpointID,
   754→		"condensation should use PendingCheckpointID, not generate a new one")
   755→}
   756→
   757→// TestTurnEnd_ConcurrentSession_PreservesShadowBranch verifies that
   758→// HandleTurnEnd does NOT delete the shadow branch when another active
   759→// session shares it.
   760→func TestTurnEnd_ConcurrentSession_PreservesShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {
   761→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   762→	t.Chdir(dir)
   763→
   764→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   765→	require.NoError(t, err)
   766→
   767→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   768→	sessionID1 := "test-turnend-concurrent-1"
   769→	sessionID2 := "test-turnend-concurrent-2"
   770→
   771→	// Initialize first session and save a checkpoint
   772→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID1)
   773→
   774→	// Get worktree info from first session
   775→	state1, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID1)
   776→	require.NoError(t, err)
   777→	worktreePath := state1.WorktreePath
   778→	baseCommit := state1.BaseCommit
   779→	worktreeID := state1.WorktreeID
   780→
   781→	// Transition first session through PostCommit to ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   782→	state1.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   783→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state1))
   784→
   785→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "e5f6a1b2c3d4")
   786→
   787→	err = s.PostCommit()
   788→	require.NoError(t, err)
   789→
   790→	state1, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID1)
   791→	require.NoError(t, err)
   792→	require.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state1.Phase)
   793→
   794→	// Create a second session with the SAME base commit and worktree (concurrent)
   795→	now := time.Now()
   796→	state2 := &SessionState{
   797→		SessionID:           sessionID2,
   798→		BaseCommit:          state1.BaseCommit, // Same base commit (post-migration)
   799→		WorktreePath:        worktreePath,
   800→		WorktreeID:          worktreeID,
   801→		StartedAt:           now,
   802→		Phase:               session.PhaseActive,
   803→		LastInteractionTime: &now,
   804→		StepCount:           1,
   805→	}
   806→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state2))
   807→
   808→	// Record shadow branch name (shared by both sessions)
   809→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state1.BaseCommit, state1.WorktreeID)
   810→
   811→	// First session ends its turn — should NOT delete shadow branch
   812→	result := session.Transition(state1.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
   813→	remaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(state1, result)
   814→
   815→	err = s.HandleTurnEnd(state1, remaining)
   816→	require.NoError(t, err)
   817→
   818→	// Shadow branch at the pre-condensation BaseCommit should be preserved
   819→	// because session2 is still active on it.
   820→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   821→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   822→	require.NoError(t, err,
   823→		"shadow branch should be preserved when another active session shares it")
   824→
   825→	// Condensation still succeeded for session1
   826→	assert.Equal(t, 0, state1.StepCount,
   827→		"StepCount should be reset after condensation")
   828→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseIdle, state1.Phase,
   829→		"first session should be IDLE after turn end")
   830→
   831→	// Second session is still active and unaffected
   832→	state2, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID2)
   833→	require.NoError(t, err)
   834→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActive, state2.Phase,
   835→		"second session should still be ACTIVE")
   836→	_ = baseCommit // used for documentation
   837→}
   838→
   839→// TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_CondensesSession verifies that HandleTurnEnd
   840→// with ActionCondense (from ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE) condenses the session
   841→// to entire/checkpoints/v1 and cleans up the shadow branch.
   842→func TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_CondensesSession(t *testing.T) {
   843→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   844→	t.Chdir(dir)
   845→
   846→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   847→	require.NoError(t, err)
   848→
   849→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   850→	sessionID := "test-turnend-condenses"
   851→
   852→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint so there is shadow branch content
   853→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   854→
   855→	// Simulate PostCommit: create a commit with trailer and transition to ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   856→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   857→	require.NoError(t, err)
   858→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   859→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   860→
   861→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "a1b2c3d4e5f6")
   862→
   863→	// Run PostCommit so phase transitions to ACTIVE_COMMITTED and PendingCheckpointID is set
   864→	err = s.PostCommit()
   865→	require.NoError(t, err)
   866→
   867→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   868→	require.NoError(t, err)
   869→	require.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase)
   870→
   871→	// Now simulate the TurnEnd transition that the handler dispatches
   872→	result := session.Transition(state.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
   873→	remaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(state, result)
   874→
   875→	// Verify the state machine emits ActionCondense
   876→	require.Contains(t, remaining, session.ActionCondense,
   877→		"ACTIVE_COMMITTED + TurnEnd should emit ActionCondense")
   878→
   879→	// Record shadow branch name BEFORE HandleTurnEnd (BaseCommit may change)
   880→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   881→
   882→	// Call HandleTurnEnd with the remaining actions
   883→	err = s.HandleTurnEnd(state, remaining)
   884→	require.NoError(t, err)
   885→
   886→	// Verify condensation happened: entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist
   887→	sessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   888→	require.NoError(t, err, "entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist after turn-end condensation")
   889→	assert.NotNil(t, sessionsRef)
   890→
   891→	// Verify shadow branch IS deleted after condensation
   892→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   893→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   894→	require.Error(t, err,
   895→		"shadow branch should be deleted after turn-end condensation")
   896→
   897→	// Verify StepCount was reset by condensation
   898→	assert.Equal(t, 0, state.StepCount,
   899→		"StepCount should be reset after condensation")
   900→
   901→	// Verify phase is IDLE (set by ApplyCommonActions above)
   902→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseIdle, state.Phase,
   903→		"phase should be IDLE after TurnEnd")
   904→}
   905→
   906→// TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch verifies
   907→// that when HandleTurnEnd condensation fails, BaseCommit is NOT updated and
   908→// the shadow branch is preserved.
   909→func TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {
   910→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   911→	t.Chdir(dir)
   912→
   913→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   914→	require.NoError(t, err)
   915→
   916→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   917→	sessionID := "test-turnend-condense-fail"
   918→
   919→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   920→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   921→
   922→	// Simulate PostCommit: transition to ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   923→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   924→	require.NoError(t, err)
   925→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   926→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   927→
   928→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "b2c3d4e5f6a1")
   929→
   930→	err = s.PostCommit()
   931→	require.NoError(t, err)
   932→
   933→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   934→	require.NoError(t, err)
   935→	require.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase)
   936→
   937→	// Record original BaseCommit before corruption
   938→	originalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit
   939→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   940→
   941→	// Corrupt shadow branch by pointing it at ZeroHash
   942→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   943→	corruptRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch), plumbing.ZeroHash)
   944→	require.NoError(t, repo.Storer.SetReference(corruptRef))
   945→
   946→	// Run the transition
   947→	result := session.Transition(state.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
   948→	remaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(state, result)
   949→
   950→	// Call HandleTurnEnd — condensation should fail silently
   951→	err = s.HandleTurnEnd(state, remaining)
   952→	require.NoError(t, err, "HandleTurnEnd should not return error even when condensation fails")
   953→
   954→	// BaseCommit should NOT be updated (condensation failed)
   955→	assert.Equal(t, originalBaseCommit, state.BaseCommit,
   956→		"BaseCommit should NOT be updated when condensation fails")
   957→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   958→		"StepCount should NOT be reset when condensation fails")
   959→
   960→	// entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should NOT exist (condensation failed)
   961→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   962→	assert.Error(t, err,
   963→		"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should NOT exist when condensation fails")
   964→}
   965→
   966→// TestTurnEnd_Active_NoActions verifies that HandleTurnEnd with no actions
   967→// is a no-op (normal ACTIVE → IDLE transition has no strategy-specific actions).
   968→func TestTurnEnd_Active_NoActions(t *testing.T) {
   969→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   970→	t.Chdir(dir)
   971→
   972→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   973→	require.NoError(t, err)
   974→
   975→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   976→	sessionID := "test-turnend-no-actions"
   977→
   978→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   979→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   980→
   981→	// Set phase to ACTIVE (normal turn, no commit during turn)
   982→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   983→	require.NoError(t, err)
   984→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   985→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   986→
   987→	originalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit
   988→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   989→
   990→	// ACTIVE + TurnEnd → IDLE with no strategy-specific actions
   991→	result := session.Transition(state.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
   992→	remaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(state, result)
   993→
   994→	// Verify no strategy-specific actions for ACTIVE → IDLE
   995→	assert.Empty(t, remaining,
   996→		"ACTIVE + TurnEnd should not emit strategy-specific actions")
   997→
   998→	// Call HandleTurnEnd with empty actions — should be a no-op
   999→	err = s.HandleTurnEnd(state, remaining)
  1000→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1001→
  1002→	// Verify state is unchanged
  1003→	assert.Equal(t, originalBaseCommit, state.BaseCommit,
  1004→		"BaseCommit should be unchanged for no-op turn end")
  1005→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
  1006→		"StepCount should be unchanged for no-op turn end")
  1007→
  1008→	// Shadow branch should still exist (not cleaned up)
  1009→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(originalBaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
  1010→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
  1011→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1012→	assert.NoError(t, err,
  1013→		"shadow branch should still exist after no-op turn end")
  1014→}
  1015→
  1016→// TestTurnEnd_DeferredCondensation_AttributionUsesOriginalBase verifies that
  1017→// deferred condensation (ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE) uses AttributionBaseCommit
  1018→// instead of BaseCommit for attribution, so the diff is non-zero.
  1019→//
  1020→// Scenario: agent modifies a file, user commits mid-turn, then turn ends.
  1021→// Without the fix, BaseCommit is updated to the new HEAD by PostCommit migration,
  1022→// so baseTree == headTree and attribution shows zero changes.
  1023→// With the fix, AttributionBaseCommit preserves the original base commit.
  1024→func TestTurnEnd_DeferredCondensation_AttributionUsesOriginalBase(t *testing.T) {
  1025→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
  1026→	t.Chdir(dir)
  1027→
  1028→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
  1029→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1030→
  1031→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
  1032→	sessionID := "test-deferred-attribution"
  1033→
  1034→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint that includes a modified file.
  1035→	// The "agent" modifies test.txt before saving the checkpoint.
  1036→	setupSessionWithFileChange(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
  1037→
  1038→	// Record the original base commit (commit A)
  1039→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
  1040→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1041→	originalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit
  1042→
  1043→	// Verify AttributionBaseCommit is set at session init
  1044→	assert.Equal(t, originalBaseCommit, state.AttributionBaseCommit,
  1045→		"AttributionBaseCommit should equal BaseCommit at session start")
  1046→
  1047→	// Set phase to ACTIVE (simulating agent mid-turn)
  1048→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
  1049→	state.FilesTouched = []string{"test.txt"}
  1050→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
  1051→
  1052→	// User commits (creates commit B). This triggers PostCommit which:
  1053→	// - Transitions ACTIVE → ACTIVE_COMMITTED (defers condensation)
  1054→	// - Migrates shadow branch to new HEAD
  1055→	// - Updates BaseCommit to new HEAD
  1056→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "a1b2c3d4e5f6")
  1057→
  1058→	err = s.PostCommit()
  1059→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1060→
  1061→	// Reload state and verify the key invariant:
  1062→	// BaseCommit has moved to the new HEAD, but AttributionBaseCommit stays at original
  1063→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
  1064→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1065→	require.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase)
  1066→
  1067→	head, err := repo.Head()
  1068→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1069→	newHeadHash := head.Hash().String()
  1070→
  1071→	assert.Equal(t, newHeadHash, state.BaseCommit,
  1072→		"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD after migration")
  1073→	assert.Equal(t, originalBaseCommit, state.AttributionBaseCommit,
  1074→		"AttributionBaseCommit should still point to original base (commit A)")
  1075→	assert.NotEqual(t, state.BaseCommit, state.AttributionBaseCommit,
  1076→		"BaseCommit and AttributionBaseCommit should diverge after mid-turn commit")
  1077→
  1078→	// Now simulate TurnEnd (agent finishes) — deferred condensation runs
  1079→	result := session.Transition(state.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
  1080→	remaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(state, result)
  1081→	require.Contains(t, remaining, session.ActionCondense)
  1082→
  1083→	err = s.HandleTurnEnd(state, remaining)
  1084→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1085→
  1086→	// After condensation, verify AttributionBaseCommit is updated to match BaseCommit
  1087→	assert.Equal(t, state.BaseCommit, state.AttributionBaseCommit,
  1088→		"AttributionBaseCommit should be updated after successful condensation")
  1089→
  1090→	// Verify condensation actually happened
  1091→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
  1092→	require.NoError(t, err, "entire/sessions branch should exist after deferred condensation")
  1093→
  1094→	// Read back the committed metadata and verify attribution is non-zero.
  1095→	// The agent modified test.txt (added a line), so AgentLines should be > 0.
  1096→	store := checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
  1097→	cpID := id.MustCheckpointID("a1b2c3d4e5f6")
  1098→	content, err := store.ReadSessionContent(context.Background(), cpID, 0)
  1099→	require.NoError(t, err, "should be able to read condensed session content")
  1100→	require.NotNil(t, content)
  1101→	require.NotNil(t, content.Metadata.InitialAttribution,
  1102→		"condensed metadata should include attribution")
  1103→	assert.Positive(t, content.Metadata.InitialAttribution.TotalCommitted,
  1104→		"attribution TotalCommitted should be non-zero (agent modified test.txt)")
  1105→}

  1107→// TestPostCommit_FilesTouched_ResetsAfterCondensation verifies that FilesTouched
  1108→// is reset after condensation, so subsequent condensations only contain the files
  1109→// touched since the last commit — not the accumulated history.
  1110→func TestPostCommit_FilesTouched_ResetsAfterCondensation(t *testing.T) {
  1111→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)

  1117→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}

  1119→
  1120→	// --- Round 1: Save checkpoint touching files A.txt and B.txt ---
  1121→
  1122→	metadataDir := ".entire/metadata/" + sessionID
  1123→	metadataDirAbs := filepath.Join(dir, metadataDir)
  1124→	require.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(metadataDirAbs, 0o755))
  1125→
  1126→	transcript := `{"type":"human","message":{"content":"round 1 prompt"}}
  1127→{"type":"assistant","message":{"content":"round 1 response"}}
  1128→`
  1129→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(
  1130→		filepath.Join(metadataDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName),
  1131→		[]byte(transcript), 0o644))
  1132→
  1133→	// Create files A.txt and B.txt
  1134→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "A.txt"), []byte("file A"), 0o644))
  1135→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "B.txt"), []byte("file B"), 0o644))
  1136→
  1137→	err = s.SaveChanges(SaveContext{
  1138→		SessionID:      sessionID,
  1139→		ModifiedFiles:  []string{},
  1140→		NewFiles:       []string{"A.txt", "B.txt"},
  1141→		DeletedFiles:   []string{},
  1142→		MetadataDir:    metadataDir,
  1143→		MetadataDirAbs: metadataDirAbs,
  1144→		CommitMessage:  "Checkpoint 1: files A and B",
  1145→		AuthorName:     "Test",
  1146→		AuthorEmail:    "test@test.com",
  1147→	})
  1148→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1149→
  1150→	// Set phase to IDLE so PostCommit triggers immediate condensation
  1151→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
  1152→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1153→	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
  1154→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
  1155→
  1156→	// Verify FilesTouched has A.txt and B.txt before condensation

  1158→		"FilesTouched should contain A.txt and B.txt before first condensation")
  1159→

  1161→	checkpointID1 := "a1a2a3a4a5a6"
  1162→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, checkpointID1)
  1163→
  1164→	err = s.PostCommit()
  1165→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1166→
  1167→	// Verify condensation happened

  1169→	require.NoError(t, err, "entire/checkpoints/v1 should exist after first condensation")
  1170→
  1171→	// Verify first condensation contains A.txt and B.txt

  1180→	// Verify FilesTouched was reset after condensation

  1186→	// --- Round 2: Save checkpoint touching files C.txt and D.txt ---

  1190→{"type":"assistant","message":{"content":"round 2 response"}}

  1192→	f, err := os.OpenFile(

  1243→// setupSessionWithFileChange is like setupSessionWithCheckpoint but also modifies
  1244→// test.txt so the shadow branch checkpoint includes actual file changes.
  1245→// This enables attribution testing: the diff between base commit and the
  1246→// checkpoint/HEAD shows real line changes.
  1247→func setupSessionWithFileChange(t *testing.T, s *ManualCommitStrategy, _ *git.Repository, dir, sessionID string) {
  1248→	t.Helper()

  1251→	testFile := filepath.Join(dir, "test.txt")
  1252→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte("initial content\nagent added line\n"), 0o644))
  1253→

  1255→	metadataDir := ".entire/metadata/" + sessionID

  1258→
  1259→	transcript := `{"type":"human","message":{"content":"test prompt"}}
  1260→{"type":"assistant","message":{"content":"test response"}}
  1261→`
  1262→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(
  1263→		filepath.Join(metadataDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName),
  1264→		[]byte(transcript), 0o644))
  1265→
  1266→	// SaveChanges creates the shadow branch and checkpoint
  1267→	err := s.SaveChanges(SaveContext{
  1268→		SessionID:      sessionID,
  1269→		ModifiedFiles:  []string{"test.txt"},
  1270→		NewFiles:       []string{},

  1274→		CommitMessage:  "Checkpoint 1",
  1275→		AuthorName:     "Test",
  1276→		AuthorEmail:    "test@test.com",
  1277→	})
  1278→	require.NoError(t, err, "SaveChanges should succeed to create shadow branch content")
  1279→}

  1313→// commitWithCheckpointTrailer creates a commit on the current branch with the
  1314→// Entire-Checkpoint trailer in the commit message. This simulates what happens
  1315→// after PrepareCommitMsg adds the trailer and the user completes the commit.
  1316→func commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t *testing.T, repo *git.Repository, dir, checkpointIDStr string) {
  1317→	t.Helper()
  1318→
  1319→	cpID := id.MustCheckpointID(checkpointIDStr)
  1320→
  1321→	// Modify a file so there is something to commit
  1322→	testFile := filepath.Join(dir, "test.txt")
  1323→	content := "updated at " + time.Now().String()
  1324→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte(content), 0o644))
  1325→
  1326→	wt, err := repo.Worktree()
  1327→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1328→
  1329→	_, err = wt.Add("test.txt")
  1330→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1331→
  1332→	commitMsg := "test commit\n\n" + trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + cpID.String() + "\n"
  1333→	_, err = wt.Commit(commitMsg, &git.CommitOptions{
  1334→		Author: &object.Signature{
  1335→			Name:  "Test",
  1336→			Email: "test@test.com",
  1337→			When:  time.Now(),
  1338→		},
  1339→	})
  1340→	require.NoError(t, err, "commit with checkpoint trailer should succeed")
  1341→}

    22→// TestPrepareCommitMsg_AmendPreservesExistingTrailer verifies that when amending
    23→// a commit that already has an Entire-Checkpoint trailer, the trailer is preserved
    24→// unchanged. source="commit" indicates an amend operation.
    25→func TestPrepareCommitMsg_AmendPreservesExistingTrailer(t *testing.T) {
    26→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
    27→	t.Chdir(dir)
    28→
    29→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
    30→
    31→	sessionID := "test-session-amend-preserve"
    32→	err := s.InitializeSession(sessionID, agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode, "", "")
    33→	require.NoError(t, err)
    34→
    35→	// Write a commit message file that already has the trailer
    36→	commitMsgFile := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "COMMIT_EDITMSG")
    37→	existingMsg := "Original commit message\n\nEntire-Checkpoint: abc123def456\n"
    38→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(existingMsg), 0o644))
    39→
    40→	// Call PrepareCommitMsg with source="commit" (amend)
    41→	err = s.PrepareCommitMsg(commitMsgFile, "commit")
    42→	require.NoError(t, err)
    43→
    44→	// Read the file back and verify the trailer is still present
    45→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile)
    46→	require.NoError(t, err)
    47→
    48→	cpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(string(content))
    49→	assert.True(t, found, "trailer should still be present after amend")
    50→	assert.Equal(t, "abc123def456", cpID.String(),
    51→		"trailer should preserve the original checkpoint ID")
    52→}

    54→// TestPrepareCommitMsg_AmendRestoresTrailerFromPendingCheckpointID verifies the amend
    55→// bug fix: when a user does `git commit --amend -m "new message"`, the Entire-Checkpoint
    56→// trailer is lost because the new message replaces the old one. PrepareCommitMsg restores
    57→// the trailer from PendingCheckpointID in session state.
    58→func TestPrepareCommitMsg_AmendRestoresTrailerFromPendingCheckpointID(t *testing.T) {
    59→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
    60→	t.Chdir(dir)
    61→
    62→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}

    68→	// Simulate state after condensation: PendingCheckpointID is set

    75→
    76→	// Write a commit message file with NO trailer (user did --amend -m "new message")

    78→	newMsg := "New amended message\n"

    80→
    81→	// Call PrepareCommitMsg with source="commit" (amend)
    82→	err = s.PrepareCommitMsg(commitMsgFile, "commit")

    88→
    89→	cpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(string(content))
    90→	assert.True(t, found,

   137→// TestPrepareCommitMsg_NormalCommitUsesPendingCheckpointID verifies that during
   138→// a normal commit (source=""), if the session is in ACTIVE_COMMITTED phase with
   139→// a PendingCheckpointID, the pending ID is reused instead of generating a new one.
   140→// This ensures idempotent checkpoint IDs across prepare-commit-msg invocations.
   141→func TestPrepareCommitMsg_NormalCommitUsesPendingCheckpointID(t *testing.T) {
   142→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   143→	t.Chdir(dir)
   144→
   145→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   146→
   147→	sessionID := "test-session-normal-pending"
   148→	err := s.InitializeSession(sessionID, agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode, "", "")
   149→	require.NoError(t, err)
   150→
   151→	// Create content on the shadow branch so filterSessionsWithNewContent finds it
   152→	createShadowBranchWithTranscript(t, dir, sessionID)
   153→
   154→	// Set the session to ACTIVE_COMMITTED with a PendingCheckpointID
   155→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   156→	require.NoError(t, err)
   157→	require.NotNil(t, state)
   158→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActiveCommitted
   159→	state.PendingCheckpointID = "fedcba987654"
   160→	// Ensure StepCount reflects that a checkpoint exists on the shadow branch
   161→	state.StepCount = 1
   162→	err = s.saveSessionState(state)
   163→	require.NoError(t, err)
   164→
   165→	// Write a commit message file with no trailer (normal editor flow)
   166→	commitMsgFile := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "COMMIT_EDITMSG")
   167→	normalMsg := "Feature: add new functionality\n"
   168→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(normalMsg), 0o644))
   169→
   170→	// Call PrepareCommitMsg with source="" (normal commit, editor flow)
   171→	err = s.PrepareCommitMsg(commitMsgFile, "")
   172→	require.NoError(t, err)
   173→
   174→	// Read the file back - trailer should use PendingCheckpointID
   175→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile)
   176→	require.NoError(t, err)
   177→
   178→	cpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(string(content))
   179→	assert.True(t, found,
   180→		"trailer should be present for normal commit with active session content")
   181→	assert.Equal(t, "fedcba987654", cpID.String(),
   182→		"normal commit should reuse PendingCheckpointID instead of generating a new one")
   183→}
   184→
   185→// createShadowBranchWithTranscript creates a shadow branch commit with a minimal
   186→// transcript file so that filterSessionsWithNewContent detects new content.
   187→// This uses low-level go-git plumbing to create the branch directly.
   188→func createShadowBranchWithTranscript(t *testing.T, repoDir string, sessionID string) {
   189→	t.Helper()
   190→
   191→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(repoDir)
   192→	require.NoError(t, err)
   193→
   194→	head, err := repo.Head()
   195→	require.NoError(t, err)
   196→	baseCommit := head.Hash().String()
   197→
   198→	// Build the tree with a transcript file at the expected path
   199→	metadataDir := paths.EntireMetadataDir + "/" + sessionID
   200→	transcriptPath := metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName
   201→	transcriptContent := `{"type":"message","role":"assistant","content":"hello"}` + "\n"
   202→
   203→	// Create blob for transcript
   204→	blobObj := &plumbing.MemoryObject{}
   205→	blobObj.SetType(plumbing.BlobObject)
   206→	blobObj.SetSize(int64(len(transcriptContent)))
   207→	writer, err := blobObj.Writer()
   208→	require.NoError(t, err)
   209→	_, err = writer.Write([]byte(transcriptContent))
   210→	require.NoError(t, err)
   211→	require.NoError(t, writer.Close())
   212→
   213→	blobHash, err := repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(blobObj)
   214→	require.NoError(t, err)
   215→
   216→	// Build nested tree structure: .entire/metadata/<sessionID>/full.jsonl
   217→	// We need to build trees bottom-up
   218→	innerTree := object.Tree{
   219→		Entries: []object.TreeEntry{
   220→			{Name: paths.TranscriptFileName, Mode: 0o100644, Hash: blobHash},
   221→		},
   222→	}
   223→	innerTreeObj := repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
   224→	innerTreeObj.SetType(plumbing.TreeObject)
   225→	require.NoError(t, innerTree.Encode(innerTreeObj))
   226→	innerTreeHash, err := repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(innerTreeObj)
   227→	require.NoError(t, err)
   228→
   229→	// Build .entire/metadata/<sessionID> level
   230→	sessionTree := object.Tree{
   231→		Entries: []object.TreeEntry{
   232→			{Name: sessionID, Mode: 0o040000, Hash: innerTreeHash},
   233→		},
   234→	}
   235→	sessionTreeObj := repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
   236→	sessionTreeObj.SetType(plumbing.TreeObject)
   237→	require.NoError(t, sessionTree.Encode(sessionTreeObj))
   238→	sessionTreeHash, err := repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(sessionTreeObj)
   239→	require.NoError(t, err)
   240→
   241→	// Build .entire/metadata level
   242→	metadataTree := object.Tree{
   243→		Entries: []object.TreeEntry{
   244→			{Name: "metadata", Mode: 0o040000, Hash: sessionTreeHash},
   245→		},
   246→	}
   247→	metadataTreeObj := repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
   248→	metadataTreeObj.SetType(plumbing.TreeObject)
   249→	require.NoError(t, metadataTree.Encode(metadataTreeObj))
   250→	metadataTreeHash, err := repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(metadataTreeObj)
   251→	require.NoError(t, err)
   252→
   253→	// Build .entire level
   254→	entireTree := object.Tree{
   255→		Entries: []object.TreeEntry{
   256→			{Name: ".entire", Mode: 0o040000, Hash: metadataTreeHash},
   257→		},
   258→	}
   259→	entireTreeObj := repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
   260→	entireTreeObj.SetType(plumbing.TreeObject)
   261→	require.NoError(t, entireTree.Encode(entireTreeObj))
   262→	entireTreeHash, err := repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(entireTreeObj)
   263→	require.NoError(t, err)
   264→
   265→	// Create commit on shadow branch
   266→	now := time.Now()
   267→	commitObj := &object.Commit{
   268→		Author: object.Signature{
   269→			Name:  "Test",
   270→			Email: "test@test.com",
   271→			When:  now,
   272→		},
   273→		Committer: object.Signature{
   274→			Name:  "Test",
   275→			Email: "test@test.com",
   276→			When:  now,
   277→		},
   278→		Message:  "checkpoint\n\nEntire-Metadata: " + metadataDir + "\nEntire-Session: " + sessionID + "\nEntire-Strategy: manual-commit\n",
   279→		TreeHash: entireTreeHash,
   280→	}
   281→	commitEnc := repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
   282→	require.NoError(t, commitObj.Encode(commitEnc))
   283→	commitHash, err := repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(commitEnc)
   284→	require.NoError(t, err)
   285→
   286→	// Create the shadow branch reference
   287→	// WorktreeID is empty for main worktree, which matches what setupGitRepo creates
   288→	shadowBranchName := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(baseCommit, "")
   289→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   290→	ref := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, commitHash)
   291→	require.NoError(t, repo.Storer.SetReference(ref))
   292→
   293→	// Verify the transcript is readable
   294→	verifyCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(commitHash)
   295→	require.NoError(t, err)
   296→	verifyTree, err := verifyCommit.Tree()
   297→	require.NoError(t, err)
   298→	file, err := verifyTree.File(transcriptPath)
   299→	require.NoError(t, err, "transcript file should exist at %s", transcriptPath)
   300→	content, err := file.Contents()
   301→	require.NoError(t, err)
   302→	require.NotEmpty(t, content, "transcript should have content")
   303→}

    20→// TestSessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript_NormalizesAbsolutePaths verifies
    21→// that sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript correctly normalizes absolute paths
    22→// from the transcript to repo-relative paths before comparing with staged files.
    23→//
    24→// Bug: ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset returns absolute paths (e.g., /tmp/repo/src/main.go)
    25→// but getStagedFiles returns repo-relative paths (e.g., src/main.go). The exact-string
    26→// comparison in hasOverlappingFiles never matches, causing "no content to link".
    27→func TestSessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript_NormalizesAbsolutePaths(t *testing.T) {
    28→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
    29→	t.Chdir(dir)
    30→
    31→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
    32→	require.NoError(t, err)
    33→
    34→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
    35→
    36→	// Create a file that we'll stage
    37→	srcDir := filepath.Join(dir, "src")
    38→	require.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(srcDir, 0o755))
    39→	testFile := filepath.Join(srcDir, "main.go")
    40→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte("package main\n"), 0o644))
    41→
    42→	wt, err := repo.Worktree()
    43→	require.NoError(t, err)
    44→	_, err = wt.Add("src/main.go")
    45→	require.NoError(t, err)
    46→
    47→	// Get the resolved worktree path first — on macOS, t.TempDir() returns /var/...
    48→	// but git resolves symlinks to /private/var/... . Claude Code uses the resolved
    49→	// path in its transcript, so we must too.
    50→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
    51→	require.NoError(t, err)
    52→	worktreeID, err := paths.GetWorktreeID(worktreePath)
    53→	require.NoError(t, err)
    54→
    55→	// Create a transcript file that references the file by absolute path
    56→	// (this is what Claude Code does — tool_use Write has absolute file_path)
    57→	absFilePath := filepath.Join(worktreePath, "src", "main.go")
    58→	transcriptContent := `{"type":"human","message":{"content":"write a main.go file"}}
    59→{"type":"assistant","message":{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"` + absFilePath + `","content":"package main\n"}}]}}
    60→`
    61→	transcriptPath := filepath.Join(dir, "transcript.jsonl")
    62→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(transcriptPath, []byte(transcriptContent), 0o644))
    63→
    64→	// Create session state: no shadow branch (it was deleted after last condensation),
    65→	// transcript path points to the file, agent type is Claude Code
    66→	now := time.Now()
    67→
    68→	head, err := repo.Head()
    69→	require.NoError(t, err)
    70→
    71→	state := &SessionState{
    72→		SessionID:                 "test-abs-path-normalize",
    73→		BaseCommit:                head.Hash().String(),
    74→		WorktreePath:              worktreePath,
    75→		WorktreeID:                worktreeID,
    76→		StartedAt:                 now,
    77→		Phase:                     session.PhaseActive,
    78→		LastInteractionTime:       &now,
    79→		AgentType:                 agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode,
    80→		TranscriptPath:            transcriptPath,
    81→		CheckpointTranscriptStart: 0, // No prior condensation
    82→	}
    83→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
    84→
    85→	// Call sessionHasNewContent — should fall through to live transcript check
    86→	// since there's no shadow branch
    87→	hasNew, err := s.sessionHasNewContent(repo, state)
    88→	require.NoError(t, err)
    89→	assert.True(t, hasNew,
    90→		"sessionHasNewContent should return true when transcript has absolute paths "+
    91→			"that match repo-relative staged files after normalization")
    92→}
    93→
    94→// TestPostCommit_NoTrailer_UpdatesBaseCommit verifies that when a commit has no
    95→// Entire-Checkpoint trailer, PostCommit still updates BaseCommit for active sessions.
    96→//
    97→// Bug: PostCommit early-returns when no trailer is found (line ~530-536). EventGitCommit
    98→// never fires, BaseCommit never updates. All subsequent commits fail the
    99→// BaseCommit == currentHeadHash filter in PrepareCommitMsg.
   100→func TestPostCommit_NoTrailer_UpdatesBaseCommit(t *testing.T) {
   101→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   102→	t.Chdir(dir)
   103→
   104→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   105→	require.NoError(t, err)
   106→
   107→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   108→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-no-trailer"
   109→
   110→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   111→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   112→
   113→	// Set phase to ACTIVE
   114→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   115→	require.NoError(t, err)
   116→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   117→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   118→
   119→	originalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit
   120→
   121→	// Create a commit WITHOUT a trailer (user removed it, or mid-turn commit
   122→	// where PrepareCommitMsg couldn't add one due to Bug 1)
   123→	testFile := filepath.Join(dir, "test.txt")
   124→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte("no trailer commit"), 0o644))
   125→	wt, err := repo.Worktree()
   126→	require.NoError(t, err)
   127→	_, err = wt.Add("test.txt")
   128→	require.NoError(t, err)
   129→	_, err = wt.Commit("commit without trailer", &git.CommitOptions{
   130→		Author: &object.Signature{
   131→			Name:  "Test",
   132→			Email: "test@test.com",
   133→			When:  time.Now(),
   134→		},
   135→	})
   136→	require.NoError(t, err)
   137→
   138→	// Get the new HEAD
   139→	head, err := repo.Head()
   140→	require.NoError(t, err)
   141→	newHeadHash := head.Hash().String()
   142→	require.NotEqual(t, originalBaseCommit, newHeadHash, "HEAD should have changed")
   143→
   144→	// Run PostCommit
   145→	err = s.PostCommit()
   146→	require.NoError(t, err)
   147→
   148→	// Verify BaseCommit was updated to new HEAD
   149→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   150→	require.NoError(t, err)
   151→	assert.Equal(t, newHeadHash, state.BaseCommit,
   152→		"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD even when commit has no trailer")
   153→
   154→	// Phase should stay ACTIVE (no state machine transition, just BaseCommit update)
   155→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActive, state.Phase,
   156→		"Phase should remain ACTIVE when commit has no trailer")
   157→}
   158→
   159→// TestSaveChanges_PreservesPendingCheckpointID verifies that SaveChanges does NOT
   160→// clear PendingCheckpointID. This field is set by PostCommit for deferred condensation
   161→// and should persist through SaveChanges calls until consumed by handleTurnEndCondense.
   162→//
   163→// Bug: SaveChanges clears PendingCheckpointID at line ~120. When the agent stops,
   164→// handleTurnEndCondense finds it empty, generates a new ID, and the commit trailer
   165→// and condensed data end up with different IDs.
   166→func TestSaveChanges_PreservesPendingCheckpointID(t *testing.T) {
   167→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   168→	t.Chdir(dir)
   169→
   170→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   171→	require.NoError(t, err)
   172→
   173→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   174→	sessionID := "test-preserve-pending-cpid"
   175→
   176→	// Initialize session and save first checkpoint
   177→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   178→
   179→	// Set PendingCheckpointID (simulating what PostCommit does)
   180→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   181→	require.NoError(t, err)
   182→	const testPendingCpID = "abc123def456"
   183→	state.PendingCheckpointID = testPendingCpID
   184→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActiveCommitted
   185→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   186→
   187→	// Create metadata for a new checkpoint
   188→	metadataDir := ".entire/metadata/" + sessionID
   189→	metadataDirAbs := filepath.Join(dir, metadataDir)
   190→	// Transcript already exists from setupSessionWithCheckpoint
   191→
   192→	// Modify a file so the checkpoint has real changes
   193→	testFile := filepath.Join(dir, "src", "new_file.go")
   194→	require.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(testFile), 0o755))
   195→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte("package src\n"), 0o644))
   196→
   197→	// Call SaveChanges — this should NOT clear PendingCheckpointID
   198→	err = s.SaveChanges(SaveContext{
   199→		SessionID:      sessionID,
   200→		ModifiedFiles:  []string{},
   201→		NewFiles:       []string{"src/new_file.go"},
   202→		DeletedFiles:   []string{},
   203→		MetadataDir:    metadataDir,
   204→		MetadataDirAbs: metadataDirAbs,
   205→		CommitMessage:  "Checkpoint 2",
   206→		AuthorName:     "Test",
   207→		AuthorEmail:    "test@test.com",
   208→	})
   209→	require.NoError(t, err)
   210→
   211→	// Reload state and verify PendingCheckpointID is preserved
   212→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   213→	require.NoError(t, err)
   214→	assert.Equal(t, testPendingCpID, state.PendingCheckpointID,
   215→		"PendingCheckpointID should be preserved across SaveChanges calls, "+
   216→			"not cleared — it's needed for deferred condensation at turn end")
   217→}

     1→//go:build integration
     2→
     3→package integration
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"bytes"
     7→	"encoding/json"
     8→	"os"
     9→	"os/exec"
    10→	"testing"
    11→
    12→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    13→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session"
    14→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"
    15→)
    16→
    17→// TestShadow_CommitBeforeStop tests the "commit while agent is still working" flow.
    18→//
    19→// When the user commits while the agent is in the ACTIVE phase (between
    20→// SimulateUserPromptSubmit and SimulateStop), the session should transition to
    21→// ACTIVE_COMMITTED. This defers condensation because the agent is still working.
    22→// When the agent finishes its turn (SimulateStop), the deferred condensation fires
    23→// and the session transitions to IDLE with metadata persisted to entire/checkpoints/v1.
    24→//
    25→// State machine transitions tested:
    26→//   - ACTIVE + GitCommit -> ACTIVE_COMMITTED (defer condensation, migrate shadow branch)
    27→//   - ACTIVE_COMMITTED + TurnEnd -> IDLE + ActionCondense (deferred condensation fires)
    28→func TestShadow_CommitBeforeStop(t *testing.T) {
    29→	t.Parallel()
    30→
    31→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
    32→
    33→	// ========================================
    34→	// Phase 1: Start session and create initial checkpoint
    35→	// ========================================
    36→	t.Log("Phase 1: Start session and create initial work")
    37→
    38→	sess := env.NewSession()
    39→
    40→	// Pass transcript path in the hook input so it's stored in session state
    41→	// (needed for mid-session commit detection via live transcript)
    42→	submitWithTranscriptPath := func(sessionID, transcriptPath string) {
    43→		t.Helper()
    44→		input := map[string]string{
    45→			"session_id":      sessionID,
    46→			"transcript_path": transcriptPath,
    47→		}
    48→		inputJSON, _ := json.Marshal(input)
    49→		cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "claude-code", "user-prompt-submit")
    50→		cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
    51→		cmd.Stdin = bytes.NewReader(inputJSON)
    52→		cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
    53→			"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+env.ClaudeProjectDir,
    54→		)
    55→		if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
    56→			t.Fatalf("user-prompt-submit failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
    57→		}
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	submitWithTranscriptPath(sess.ID, sess.TranscriptPath)
    61→
    62→	// Verify session is ACTIVE
    63→	state, err := env.GetSessionState(sess.ID)
    64→	if err != nil {
    65→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState failed: %v", err)
    66→	}
    67→	if state == nil {
    68→		t.Fatal("Session state should exist")
    69→	}
    70→	if state.Phase != session.PhaseActive {
    71→		t.Errorf("Phase after first prompt should be %q, got %q", session.PhaseActive, state.Phase)
    72→	}
    73→
    74→	// Create a file and transcript, then stop (first checkpoint)
    75→	env.WriteFile("feature.go", "package main\n\nfunc Feature() {}\n")
    76→	sess.CreateTranscript("Create feature function", []FileChange{
    77→		{Path: "feature.go", Content: "package main\n\nfunc Feature() {}\n"},
    78→	})
    79→	if err := env.SimulateStop(sess.ID, sess.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
    80→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 1) failed: %v", err)
    81→	}
    82→
    83→	// Verify session is IDLE after stop
    84→	state, err = env.GetSessionState(sess.ID)
    85→	if err != nil {
    86→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState failed: %v", err)
    87→	}
    88→	if state == nil {
    89→		t.Fatal("Session state should exist")
    90→	}
    91→	if state.Phase != session.PhaseIdle {
    92→		t.Errorf("Phase after first stop should be %q, got %q", session.PhaseIdle, state.Phase)
    93→	}
    94→	if state.StepCount != 1 {
    95→		t.Errorf("StepCount after first checkpoint should be 1, got %d", state.StepCount)
    96→	}
    97→
    98→	// Verify shadow branch was created
    99→	initialHead := state.BaseCommit
   100→	shadowBranch := env.GetShadowBranchNameForCommit(initialHead)
   101→	if !env.BranchExists(shadowBranch) {
   102→		t.Fatalf("Shadow branch %s should exist after first checkpoint", shadowBranch)
   103→	}
   104→
   105→	// ========================================
   106→	// Phase 2: Start new turn and create more work
   107→	// ========================================
   108→	t.Log("Phase 2: Start new turn, create more work")
   109→
   110→	submitWithTranscriptPath(sess.ID, sess.TranscriptPath)
   111→
   112→	state, err = env.GetSessionState(sess.ID)
   113→	if err != nil {
   114→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState failed: %v", err)
   115→	}
   116→	if state.Phase != session.PhaseActive {
   117→		t.Errorf("Phase after second prompt should be %q, got %q", session.PhaseActive, state.Phase)
   118→	}
   119→
   120→	// Create another file and write updated transcript
   121→	env.WriteFile("utils.go", "package main\n\nfunc Util() {}\n")
   122→	sess.TranscriptBuilder = NewTranscriptBuilder()
   123→	sess.CreateTranscript("Create utils", []FileChange{
   124→		{Path: "utils.go", Content: "package main\n\nfunc Util() {}\n"},
   125→	})
   126→
   127→	// Do NOT stop yet -- the agent is still ACTIVE
   128→
   129→	// ========================================
   130→	// Phase 3: User commits while agent is ACTIVE
   131→	// ========================================
   132→	t.Log("Phase 3: User commits while agent is ACTIVE")
   133→
   134→	headBefore := env.GetHeadHash()
   135→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add feature and utils", "feature.go", "utils.go")
   136→	commitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
   137→
   138→	if commitHash == headBefore {
   139→		t.Fatal("Commit was not created")
   140→	}
   141→
   142→	// Verify checkpoint trailer was added
   143→	checkpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commitHash)
   144→	if checkpointID == "" {
   145→		t.Log("Note: checkpoint trailer may not be present if no shadow branch content was detected (mid-session commit scenario)")
   146→	} else {
   147→		t.Logf("Commit has checkpoint trailer: %s", checkpointID)
   148→	}
   149→
   150→	// CRITICAL: Verify session phase is ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   151→	state, err = env.GetSessionState(sess.ID)
   152→	if err != nil {
   153→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState failed: %v", err)
   154→	}
   155→	if state == nil {
   156→		t.Fatal("Session state should exist after commit")
   157→	}
   158→	if state.Phase != session.PhaseActiveCommitted {
   159→		t.Errorf("Phase after commit-while-active should be %q, got %q",
   160→			session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase)
   161→	}
   162→	t.Logf("Session phase after mid-turn commit: %s", state.Phase)
   163→
   164→	// Verify shadow branch was migrated to the new HEAD
   165→	// The old shadow branch (based on initialHead) may still exist or be cleaned up.
   166→	// The important thing is that the session's BaseCommit was updated.
   167→	if state.BaseCommit == initialHead {
   168→		t.Logf("Note: BaseCommit not yet updated (may happen during migration)")
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	// ========================================
   172→	// Phase 4: Agent finishes turn (SimulateStop)
   173→	// ========================================
   174→	t.Log("Phase 4: Agent finishes turn (SimulateStop)")
   175→
   176→	if err := env.SimulateStop(sess.ID, sess.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   177→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (after commit) failed: %v", err)
   178→	}
   179→
   180→	// Verify session transitions to IDLE
   181→	state, err = env.GetSessionState(sess.ID)
   182→	if err != nil {
   183→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState failed: %v", err)
   184→	}
   185→	if state == nil {
   186→		t.Fatal("Session state should exist after stop")
   187→	}
   188→	if state.Phase != session.PhaseIdle {
   189→		t.Errorf("Phase after stop from ACTIVE_COMMITTED should be %q, got %q",
   190→			session.PhaseIdle, state.Phase)
   191→	}
   192→	t.Logf("Session phase after stop: %s (StepCount: %d)", state.Phase, state.StepCount)
   193→
   194→	// Deferred condensation should have fired during TurnEnd (ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE).
   195→	// Verify StepCount was reset and metadata was persisted to entire/checkpoints/v1.
   196→	if state.StepCount != 0 {
   197→		t.Errorf("StepCount should be 0 after TurnEnd condensation, got %d", state.StepCount)
   198→	}
   199→
   200→	if !env.BranchExists(paths.MetadataBranchName) {
   201→		t.Fatal("entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist after TurnEnd condensation")
   202→	}
   203→	latestCheckpointID := env.TryGetLatestCheckpointID()
   204→	if latestCheckpointID != "" {
   205→		summaryPath := CheckpointSummaryPath(latestCheckpointID)
   206→		if !env.FileExistsInBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, summaryPath) {
   207→			t.Errorf("Checkpoint metadata should exist at %s", summaryPath)
   208→		} else {
   209→			t.Logf("Condensed data exists at checkpoint %s", latestCheckpointID)
   210→		}
   211→	}
   212→
   213→	t.Log("CommitBeforeStop test completed successfully")
   214→}
   215→
   216→// TestShadow_AmendPreservesTrailer tests that `git commit --amend` preserves
   217→// the checkpoint trailer from the original commit.
   218→//
   219→// When a user amends a commit that has an Entire-Checkpoint trailer, the
   220→// prepare-commit-msg hook (called with source="commit") should preserve the
   221→// existing trailer. No duplicate condensation should occur.
   222→//
   223→// Hook behavior tested:
   224→//   - prepare-commit-msg with source="commit": preserves existing trailer
   225→//   - post-commit after amend: no duplicate condensation
   226→func TestShadow_AmendPreservesTrailer(t *testing.T) {
   227→	t.Parallel()
   228→
   229→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   230→
   231→	// ========================================
   232→	// Phase 1: Full workflow - create checkpoint and commit
   233→	// ========================================
   234→	t.Log("Phase 1: Create session, checkpoint, and commit")
   235→
   236→	sess := env.NewSession()
   237→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(sess.ID); err != nil {
   238→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   239→	}
   240→
   241→	// Create a file and checkpoint
   242→	env.WriteFile("main.go", "package main\n\nfunc main() {}\n")
   243→	sess.CreateTranscript("Create main.go", []FileChange{
   244→		{Path: "main.go", Content: "package main\n\nfunc main() {}\n"},
   245→	})
   246→	if err := env.SimulateStop(sess.ID, sess.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   247→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
   248→	}
   249→
   250→	// Commit with hooks (triggers condensation)
   251→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Initial implementation", "main.go")
   252→
   253→	originalCommitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
   254→	originalCheckpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(originalCommitHash)
   255→	if originalCheckpointID == "" {
   256→		t.Fatal("Original commit should have a checkpoint trailer")
   257→	}
   258→	t.Logf("Original commit %s has checkpoint ID: %s", originalCommitHash[:7], originalCheckpointID)
   259→
   260→	// Verify condensation happened
   261→	if !env.BranchExists(paths.MetadataBranchName) {
   262→		t.Fatal("entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist after condensation")
   263→	}
   264→
   265→	// Record the sessions branch state for later comparison
   266→	sessionsCommitBefore := env.GetLatestCommitMessageOnBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   267→	t.Logf("Sessions branch commit before amend:\n%s", sessionsCommitBefore)
   268→
   269→	// ========================================
   270→	// Phase 2: Amend the commit
   271→	// ========================================
   272→	t.Log("Phase 2: Amend the commit")
   273→
   274→	// Amend with the same message (simulating a minor message edit or staging additional files)
   275→	env.GitCommitAmendWithShadowHooks("Initial implementation (amended)")
   276→
   277→	amendedCommitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
   278→	t.Logf("Amended commit: %s", amendedCommitHash[:7])
   279→
   280→	// The amended commit hash should be different from the original
   281→	if amendedCommitHash == originalCommitHash {
   282→		t.Error("Amended commit should have a different hash")
   283→	}
   284→
   285→	// ========================================
   286→	// Phase 3: Verify trailer is preserved
   287→	// ========================================
   288→	t.Log("Phase 3: Verify checkpoint trailer is preserved")
   289→
   290→	amendedCheckpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(amendedCommitHash)
   291→	if amendedCheckpointID == "" {
   292→		t.Fatal("Amended commit should still have a checkpoint trailer")
   293→	}
   294→	t.Logf("Amended commit has checkpoint ID: %s", amendedCheckpointID)
   295→
   296→	// The checkpoint ID should be the SAME as the original
   297→	if amendedCheckpointID != originalCheckpointID {
   298→		t.Errorf("Amended commit should preserve original checkpoint ID.\nOriginal: %s\nAmended:  %s",
   299→			originalCheckpointID, amendedCheckpointID)
   300→	}
   301→
   302→	// ========================================
   303→	// Phase 4: Verify no duplicate condensation
   304→	// ========================================
   305→	t.Log("Phase 4: Verify no duplicate condensation")
   306→
   307→	// The sessions branch commit should not have changed (no new condensation)
   308→	sessionsCommitAfter := env.GetLatestCommitMessageOnBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   309→	if sessionsCommitBefore != sessionsCommitAfter {
   310→		t.Logf("Sessions branch commit changed after amend:\nBefore:\n%s\nAfter:\n%s",
   311→			sessionsCommitBefore, sessionsCommitAfter)
   312→		// This is not necessarily an error -- post-commit might trigger condensation
   313→		// if there's new content. But with no new session activity, it should be the same.
   314→		t.Log("Note: sessions branch was updated (may indicate duplicate condensation)")
   315→	} else {
   316→		t.Log("Sessions branch unchanged after amend (no duplicate condensation)")
   317→	}
   318→
   319→	// Verify the checkpoint data still exists and is accessible
   320→	summaryPath := CheckpointSummaryPath(originalCheckpointID)
   321→	if !env.FileExistsInBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, summaryPath) {
   322→		t.Errorf("Checkpoint metadata should still exist at %s after amend", summaryPath)
   323→	}
   324→
   325→	transcriptPath := SessionFilePath(originalCheckpointID, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   326→	if !env.FileExistsInBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, transcriptPath) {
   327→		t.Errorf("Transcript should still exist at %s after amend", transcriptPath)
   328→	}
   329→
   330→	t.Log("AmendPreservesTrailer test completed successfully")
   331→}
   332→

{
  "replace_all": false,

  "old_string": "// PostCommit is called by the git post-commit hook after a commit is created.\n// Uses the session state machine to determine what action to take per session:\n//   - ACTIVE → ACTIVE_COMMITTED: defer condensation (agent still working)\n//   - IDLE → condense immediately\n//   - ACTIVE_COMMITTED → migrate shadow branch (additional commit during same turn)\n//   - ENDED → condense if files touched, discard if empty\n//\n// Shadow branches are only deleted when ALL sessions sharing the branch are non-active.\n// During rebase/cherry-pick/revert operations, phase transitions are skipped entirely.\n//\n//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil",
  "new_string": "// PostCommit is called by the git post-commit hook after a commit is created.\n// Uses the session state machine to determine what action to take per session:\n//   - ACTIVE → condense immediately (each commit gets its own checkpoint)\n//   - IDLE → condense immediately\n//   - ENDED → condense if files touched, discard if empty\n//\n// Shadow branches are only deleted when ALL sessions sharing the branch are non-active\n// and were condensed during this PostCommit.\n// During rebase/cherry-pick/revert operations, phase transitions are skipped entirely.\n//\n//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil"
}

On condensation failure,\n\t\t// BaseCommit is intentionally NOT updated to preserve access to\n\t\t// the shadow branch (which is named after the old BaseCommit).\n\t\tfor _, action := range remaining {\n\t\t\tswitch action {\n\t\t\tcase session.ActionCondense:\n\t\t\t\tif hasNew {\n\t\t\t\t\ts.condenseAndUpdateState(logCtx, repo, checkpointID, state, head, shadowBranchName, newHead)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcase session.ActionDiscardIfNoFiles:\n\t\t\t\tif len(state.FilesTouched) == 0 {\n\t\t\t\t\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"post-commit: skipping empty ended session (no files to condense)\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", state.SessionID),\n\t\t\t\t\t)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\ts.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)\n\t\t\tcase session.ActionMigrateShadowBranch:\n\t\t\t\t// Deferred to pass 2 so condensation reads the old shadow branch first.\n\t\t\t\t// Migration updates BaseCommit as part of the rename.\n\t\t\t\t// Store checkpointID so HandleTurnEnd can reuse it for deferred condensation.\n\t\t\t\tstate.PendingCheckpointID = checkpointID.String()\n\t\t\t\tpendingMigrations = append(pendingMigrations, pendingMigration{state: state})\n\t\t\tcase session.ActionClearEndedAt, session.ActionUpdateLastInteraction:\n\t\t\t\t// Handled by session.ApplyCommonActions above\n\t\t\tcase session.ActionWarnStaleSession:\n\t\t\t\t// Not produced by EventGitCommit; listed for switch exhaustiveness\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Save the updated state\n\t\tif err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[entire] Warning: failed to update session state: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Track whether any session on this shadow branch is still active\n\t\tif state.Phase.IsActive() {\n\t\t\tactiveSessionsOnBranch[shadowBranchName] = true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Pass 2: Run deferred migrations now that all condensations are complete.\n\tfor _, pm := range pendingMigrations {\n\t\tif _, migErr := s.migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded(repo, pm.state); migErr != nil {\n\t\t\tlogging.Warn(logCtx, \"post-commit: shadow branch migration failed\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", pm.state.SessionID),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"error\", migErr.Error()),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Save the migrated state\n\t\tif err := — only delete when ALL sessions on the branch are non-active\n\tfor shadowBranchName := range shadowBranchesToDelete {\n\t\tif activeSessionsOnBranch[shadowBranchName] {\n\t\t\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, On condensation failure,\n\t\t// BaseCommit is intentionally NOT updated to preserve access to\n\t\t// the shadow branch (which is named after the old BaseCommit).\n\t\tcondensed := false\n\t\tfor _, action := range remaining {\n\t\t\tswitch action {\n\t\t\tcase session.ActionCondense:\n\t\t\t\tif hasNew {\n\t\t\t\t\ts.condenseAndUpdateState(logCtx, repo, checkpointID, state, head, shadowBranchName, shadowBranchesToDelete)\n\t\t\t\t\tcondensed = true\n\t\t\t\t\t// condenseAndUpdateState updates BaseCommit on success.\n\t\t\t\t\t// On failure, BaseCommit is preserved so the shadow branch remains accessible.\n\t\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t\t// No new content to condense — just update BaseCommit\n\t\t\t\t\ts.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcase session.ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched:\n\t\t\t\t// The state machine already gates this action on HasFilesTouched,\n\t\t\t\t// but hasNew is an additional content-level check (transcript has\n\t\t\t\t// new content beyond what was previously condensed).\n\t\t\t\tif len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 && hasNew {\n\t\t\t\t\ts.condenseAndUpdateState(logCtx, repo, checkpointID, state, head, shadowBranchName, shadowBranchesToDelete)\n\t\t\t\t\tcondensed = true\n\t\t\t\t\t// On failure, BaseCommit is preserved (same as ActionCondense).\n\t\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t\ts.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcase session.ActionDiscardIfNoFiles:\n\t\t\t\tif len(state.FilesTouched) == 0 {\n\t\t\t\t\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"post-commit: skipping empty ended session (no files to condense)\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", state.SessionID),\n\t\t\t\t\t)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\ts.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)\n\t\t\tcase session.ActionClearEndedAt, session.ActionUpdateLastInteraction:\n\t\t\t\t// Handled by session.ApplyCommonActions above\n\t\t\tcase session.ActionWarnStaleSession:\n\t\t\t\t// Not produced by EventGitCommit; listed for switch exhaustiveness\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Save the updated state\n\t\tif err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[entire] Warning: failed to update session state: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Only preserve shadow branch for active sessions that were NOT condensed.\n\t\t// Condensed sessions already have their data on entire/checkpoints/v1.\n\t\tif state.Phase.IsActive() && !condensed {\n\t\t\tuncondensedActiveOnBranch[shadowBranchName] = true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Clean up shadow branches — only delete when no uncondensed active sessions need them\n\tfor shadowBranchName := range shadowBranchesToDelete {\n\t\tif uncondensedActiveOnBranch[shadowBranchName] {\n\t\t\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"post-commit: preserving shadow branch (uncondensed active session exists)\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"shadow_branch\", shadowBranchName),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}"

This prevents stale sessions from injecting\n\t// unrelated checkpoint IDs.\n\trepo, repoErr := OpenRepository()\n\tif repoErr != nil {\n\t\treturn nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure\n\t}\n\thead, headErr := repo.Head()\n\tif headErr != nil {\n\t\treturn nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure\n\t}\n\tcurrentHead := head.Hash().String()\n\n\t// Find first matching session with PendingCheckpointID or LastCheckpointID to restore.\n\t// PendingCheckpointID is set during ACTIVE_COMMITTED (deferred condensation).\n\t// LastCheckpointID is set after condensation completes.\n\tfor _, state := range sessions {\n\t\tif state.BaseCommit != currentHead {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tvar cpID id.CheckpointID\n\t\tsource := \"\"\n\n\t\tif state.PendingCheckpointID != \"\" {\n\t\t\tif parsed, parseErr := id.NewCheckpointID(state.PendingCheckpointID); parseErr == nil {\n\t\t\t\tcpID = parsed\n\t\t\t\tsource = \"PendingCheckpointID\"\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif cpID.IsEmpty() && !state.LastCheckpointID.IsEmpty() {\n\t\t\tcpID = state.LastCheckpointID\n\t\t\tsource = \"LastCheckpointID\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tif cpID.IsEmpty() {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}",
  "new_string": "\t// For amend, HEAD^ is the commit being amended, and HEAD is where we are now.\n\t// We need to match sessions whose BaseCommit equals HEAD (the commit being amended\n\t// was created from this base). This prevents stale sessions from injecting\n\t// unrelated checkpoint IDs.\n\trepo, repoErr := OpenRepository()\n\tif repoErr != nil {\n\t\treturn nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure\n\t}\n\thead, headErr := repo.Head()\n\tif headErr != nil {\n\t\treturn nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure\n\t}\n\tcurrentHead := head.Hash().String()\n\n\t// Find first matching session with LastCheckpointID to restore.\n\t// LastCheckpointID is set after condensation completes.\n\tfor _, state := range sessions {\n\t\tif state.BaseCommit != currentHead {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif state.LastCheckpointID.IsEmpty() {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tcpID := state.LastCheckpointID\n\t\tsource := \"LastCheckpointID\""

Restoring doesn't need user confirmation\n\t// since the data is already committed — this handles git commit --amend -m \"...\"\n\t// and non-interactive environments (e.g., Claude doing commits).\n\tisRestoringExisting := false\n\tif !hasNewContent && reusedSession != nil {\n\t\t// Reusing LastCheckpointID from a previous condensation\n\t\tisRestoringExisting = true\n\t} else if hasNewContent {\n\t\tfor _, state := range sessionsWithContent {\n\t\t\tif state.PendingCheckpointID != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tisRestoringExisting = true\n\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}",

Restoring doesn't need user confirmation\n\t// since the data is already committed — this handles git commit --amend -m \"...\"\n\t// and non-interactive environments (e.g., Claude doing commits).\n\tisRestoringExisting := !hasNewContent && reusedSession != nil"
}
