    20→// TestSessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript_NormalizesAbsolutePaths verifies
    21→// that sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript correctly normalizes absolute paths
    22→// from the transcript to repo-relative paths before comparing with staged files.
    23→//
    24→// Bug: ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset returns absolute paths (e.g., /tmp/repo/src/main.go)
    25→// but getStagedFiles returns repo-relative paths (e.g., src/main.go). The exact-string
    26→// comparison in hasOverlappingFiles never matches, causing "no content to link".
    27→func TestSessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript_NormalizesAbsolutePaths(t *testing.T) {
    28→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
    29→	t.Chdir(dir)
    30→
    31→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
    32→	require.NoError(t, err)
    33→
    34→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
    35→
    36→	// Create a file that we'll stage
    37→	srcDir := filepath.Join(dir, "src")
    38→	require.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(srcDir, 0o755))
    39→	testFile := filepath.Join(srcDir, "main.go")
    40→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte("package main\n"), 0o644))
    41→
    42→	wt, err := repo.Worktree()
    43→	require.NoError(t, err)
    44→	_, err = wt.Add("src/main.go")
    45→	require.NoError(t, err)
    46→
    47→	// Get the resolved worktree path first — on macOS, t.TempDir() returns /var/...
    48→	// but git resolves symlinks to /private/var/... . Claude Code uses the resolved
    49→	// path in its transcript, so we must too.
    50→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
    51→	require.NoError(t, err)
    52→	worktreeID, err := paths.GetWorktreeID(worktreePath)
    53→	require.NoError(t, err)
    54→
    55→	// Create a transcript file that references the file by absolute path
    56→	// (this is what Claude Code does — tool_use Write has absolute file_path)
    57→	absFilePath := filepath.Join(worktreePath, "src", "main.go")
    58→	transcriptContent := `{"type":"human","message":{"content":"write a main.go file"}}
    59→{"type":"assistant","message":{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"` + absFilePath + `","content":"package main\n"}}]}}
    60→`
    61→	transcriptPath := filepath.Join(dir, "transcript.jsonl")
    62→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(transcriptPath, []byte(transcriptContent), 0o644))
    63→
    64→	// Create session state: no shadow branch (it was deleted after last condensation),
    65→	// transcript path points to the file, agent type is Claude Code
    66→	now := time.Now()
    67→
    68→	head, err := repo.Head()
    69→	require.NoError(t, err)
    70→
    71→	state := &SessionState{
    72→		SessionID:                 "test-abs-path-normalize",
    73→		BaseCommit:                head.Hash().String(),
    74→		WorktreePath:              worktreePath,
    75→		WorktreeID:                worktreeID,
    76→		StartedAt:                 now,
    77→		Phase:                     session.PhaseActive,
    78→		LastInteractionTime:       &now,
    79→		AgentType:                 agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode,
    80→		TranscriptPath:            transcriptPath,
    81→		CheckpointTranscriptStart: 0, // No prior condensation
    82→	}
    83→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
    84→
    85→	// Call sessionHasNewContent — should fall through to live transcript check
    86→	// since there's no shadow branch
    87→	hasNew, err := s.sessionHasNewContent(repo, state)
    88→	require.NoError(t, err)
    89→	assert.True(t, hasNew,
    90→		"sessionHasNewContent should return true when transcript has absolute paths "+
    91→			"that match repo-relative staged files after normalization")
    92→}
    93→
    94→// TestPostCommit_NoTrailer_UpdatesBaseCommit verifies that when a commit has no
    95→// Entire-Checkpoint trailer, PostCommit still updates BaseCommit for active sessions.
    96→//
    97→// Bug: PostCommit early-returns when no trailer is found (line ~530-536). EventGitCommit
    98→// never fires, BaseCommit never updates. All subsequent commits fail the
    99→// BaseCommit == currentHeadHash filter in PrepareCommitMsg.
   100→func TestPostCommit_NoTrailer_UpdatesBaseCommit(t *testing.T) {
   101→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   102→	t.Chdir(dir)
   103→
   104→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   105→	require.NoError(t, err)
   106→
   107→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   108→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-no-trailer"
   109→
   110→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   111→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   112→
   113→	// Set phase to ACTIVE
   114→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   115→	require.NoError(t, err)
   116→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   117→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   118→
   119→	originalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit
   120→
   121→	// Create a commit WITHOUT a trailer (user removed it, or mid-turn commit
   122→	// where PrepareCommitMsg couldn't add one due to Bug 1)
   123→	testFile := filepath.Join(dir, "test.txt")
   124→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte("no trailer commit"), 0o644))
   125→	wt, err := repo.Worktree()
   126→	require.NoError(t, err)
   127→	_, err = wt.Add("test.txt")
   128→	require.NoError(t, err)
   129→	_, err = wt.Commit("commit without trailer", &git.CommitOptions{
   130→		Author: &object.Signature{
   131→			Name:  "Test",
   132→			Email: "test@test.com",
   133→			When:  time.Now(),
   134→		},
   135→	})
   136→	require.NoError(t, err)
   137→
   138→	// Get the new HEAD
   139→	head, err := repo.Head()
   140→	require.NoError(t, err)
   141→	newHeadHash := head.Hash().String()
   142→	require.NotEqual(t, originalBaseCommit, newHeadHash, "HEAD should have changed")
   143→
   144→	// Run PostCommit
   145→	err = s.PostCommit()
   146→	require.NoError(t, err)
   147→
   148→	// Verify BaseCommit was updated to new HEAD
   149→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   150→	require.NoError(t, err)
   151→	assert.Equal(t, newHeadHash, state.BaseCommit,
   152→		"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD even when commit has no trailer")
   153→
   154→	// Phase should stay ACTIVE (no state machine transition, just BaseCommit update)
   155→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActive, state.Phase,
   156→		"Phase should remain ACTIVE when commit has no trailer")
   157→}
   158→
   159→// TestSaveChanges_PreservesPendingCheckpointID verifies that SaveChanges does NOT
   160→// clear PendingCheckpointID. This field is set by PostCommit for deferred condensation
   161→// and should persist through SaveChanges calls until consumed by handleTurnEndCondense.
   162→//
   163→// Bug: SaveChanges clears PendingCheckpointID at line ~120. When the agent stops,
   164→// handleTurnEndCondense finds it empty, generates a new ID, and the commit trailer
   165→// and condensed data end up with different IDs.
   166→func TestSaveChanges_PreservesPendingCheckpointID(t *testing.T) {
   167→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   168→	t.Chdir(dir)
   169→
   170→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   171→	require.NoError(t, err)
   172→
   173→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   174→	sessionID := "test-preserve-pending-cpid"
   175→
   176→	// Initialize session and save first checkpoint
   177→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   178→
   179→	// Set PendingCheckpointID (simulating what PostCommit does)
   180→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   181→	require.NoError(t, err)
   182→	const testPendingCpID = "abc123def456"
   183→	state.PendingCheckpointID = testPendingCpID
   184→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActiveCommitted
   185→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   186→
   187→	// Create metadata for a new checkpoint
   188→	metadataDir := ".entire/metadata/" + sessionID
   189→	metadataDirAbs := filepath.Join(dir, metadataDir)
   190→	// Transcript already exists from setupSessionWithCheckpoint
   191→
   192→	// Modify a file so the checkpoint has real changes
   193→	testFile := filepath.Join(dir, "src", "new_file.go")
   194→	require.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(testFile), 0o755))
   195→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte("package src\n"), 0o644))
   196→
   197→	// Call SaveChanges — this should NOT clear PendingCheckpointID
   198→	err = s.SaveChanges(SaveContext{
   199→		SessionID:      sessionID,
   200→		ModifiedFiles:  []string{},
   201→		NewFiles:       []string{"src/new_file.go"},
   202→		DeletedFiles:   []string{},
   203→		MetadataDir:    metadataDir,
   204→		MetadataDirAbs: metadataDirAbs,
   205→		CommitMessage:  "Checkpoint 2",
   206→		AuthorName:     "Test",
   207→		AuthorEmail:    "test@test.com",
   208→	})
   209→	require.NoError(t, err)
   210→
   211→	// Reload state and verify PendingCheckpointID is preserved
   212→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   213→	require.NoError(t, err)
   214→	assert.Equal(t, testPendingCpID, state.PendingCheckpointID,
   215→		"PendingCheckpointID should be preserved across SaveChanges calls, "+
   216→			"not cleared — it's needed for deferred condensation at turn end")
   217→}

    17→// TestShadow_CommitBeforeStop tests the "commit while agent is still working" flow.
    18→//
    19→// When the user commits while the agent is in the ACTIVE phase (between
    20→// SimulateUserPromptSubmit and SimulateStop), the session should transition to
    21→// ACTIVE_COMMITTED. This defers condensation because the agent is still working.
    22→// When the agent finishes its turn (SimulateStop), the deferred condensation fires
    23→// and the session transitions to IDLE with metadata persisted to entire/checkpoints/v1.
    24→//
    25→// State machine transitions tested:
    26→//   - ACTIVE + GitCommit -> ACTIVE_COMMITTED (defer condensation, migrate shadow branch)
    27→//   - ACTIVE_COMMITTED + TurnEnd -> IDLE + ActionCondense (deferred condensation fires)
    28→func TestShadow_CommitBeforeStop(t *testing.T) {
    29→	t.Parallel()
    30→
    31→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
    32→
    33→	// ========================================
    34→	// Phase 1: Start session and create initial checkpoint
    35→	// ========================================
    36→	t.Log("Phase 1: Start session and create initial work")
    37→
    38→	sess := env.NewSession()
    39→
    40→	// Pass transcript path in the hook input so it's stored in session state
    41→	// (needed for mid-session commit detection via live transcript)
    42→	submitWithTranscriptPath := func(sessionID, transcriptPath string) {
    43→		t.Helper()
    44→		input := map[string]string{
    45→			"session_id":      sessionID,
    46→			"transcript_path": transcriptPath,
    47→		}
    48→		inputJSON, _ := json.Marshal(input)
    49→		cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "claude-code", "user-prompt-submit")
    50→		cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
    51→		cmd.Stdin = bytes.NewReader(inputJSON)
    52→		cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
    53→			"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+env.ClaudeProjectDir,
    54→		)
    55→		if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
    56→			t.Fatalf("user-prompt-submit failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
    57→		}
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	submitWithTranscriptPath(sess.ID, sess.TranscriptPath)
    61→
    62→	// Verify session is ACTIVE
    63→	state, err := env.GetSessionState(sess.ID)
    64→	if err != nil {
    65→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState failed: %v", err)
    66→	}
    67→	if state == nil {
    68→		t.Fatal("Session state should exist")
    69→	}
    70→	if state.Phase != session.PhaseActive {
    71→		t.Errorf("Phase after first prompt should be %q, got %q", session.PhaseActive, state.Phase)
    72→	}
    73→
    74→	// Create a file and transcript, then stop (first checkpoint)
    75→	env.WriteFile("feature.go", "package main\n\nfunc Feature() {}\n")
    76→	sess.CreateTranscript("Create feature function", []FileChange{
    77→		{Path: "feature.go", Content: "package main\n\nfunc Feature() {}\n"},
    78→	})
    79→	if err := env.SimulateStop(sess.ID, sess.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
    80→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 1) failed: %v", err)
    81→	}
    82→
    83→	// Verify session is IDLE after stop
    84→	state, err = env.GetSessionState(sess.ID)
    85→	if err != nil {
    86→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState failed: %v", err)
    87→	}
    88→	if state == nil {
    89→		t.Fatal("Session state should exist")
    90→	}
    91→	if state.Phase != session.PhaseIdle {
    92→		t.Errorf("Phase after first stop should be %q, got %q", session.PhaseIdle, state.Phase)
    93→	}
    94→	if state.StepCount != 1 {
    95→		t.Errorf("StepCount after first checkpoint should be 1, got %d", state.StepCount)
    96→	}
    97→
    98→	// Verify shadow branch was created
    99→	initialHead := state.BaseCommit
   100→	shadowBranch := env.GetShadowBranchNameForCommit(initialHead)
   101→	if !env.BranchExists(shadowBranch) {
   102→		t.Fatalf("Shadow branch %s should exist after first checkpoint", shadowBranch)
   103→	}
   104→
   105→	// ========================================
   106→	// Phase 2: Start new turn and create more work
   107→	// ========================================
   108→	t.Log("Phase 2: Start new turn, create more work")
   109→
   110→	submitWithTranscriptPath(sess.ID, sess.TranscriptPath)
   111→
   112→	state, err = env.GetSessionState(sess.ID)
   113→	if err != nil {
   114→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState failed: %v", err)
   115→	}
   116→	if state.Phase != session.PhaseActive {
   117→		t.Errorf("Phase after second prompt should be %q, got %q", session.PhaseActive, state.Phase)
   118→	}
   119→
   120→	// Create another file and write updated transcript
   121→	env.WriteFile("utils.go", "package main\n\nfunc Util() {}\n")
   122→	sess.TranscriptBuilder = NewTranscriptBuilder()
   123→	sess.CreateTranscript("Create utils", []FileChange{
   124→		{Path: "utils.go", Content: "package main\n\nfunc Util() {}\n"},
   125→	})
   126→
   127→	// Do NOT stop yet -- the agent is still ACTIVE
   128→
   129→	// ========================================
   130→	// Phase 3: User commits while agent is ACTIVE
   131→	// ========================================
   132→	t.Log("Phase 3: User commits while agent is ACTIVE")
   133→
   134→	headBefore := env.GetHeadHash()
   135→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add feature and utils", "feature.go", "utils.go")
   136→	commitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
   137→
   138→	if commitHash == headBefore {
   139→		t.Fatal("Commit was not created")
   140→	}
   141→
   142→	// Verify checkpoint trailer was added
   143→	checkpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commitHash)
   144→	if checkpointID == "" {
   145→		t.Log("Note: checkpoint trailer may not be present if no shadow branch content was detected (mid-session commit scenario)")
   146→	} else {
   147→		t.Logf("Commit has checkpoint trailer: %s", checkpointID)
   148→	}
   149→
   150→	// CRITICAL: Verify session phase is ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   151→	state, err = env.GetSessionState(sess.ID)
   152→	if err != nil {
   153→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState failed: %v", err)
   154→	}
   155→	if state == nil {
   156→		t.Fatal("Session state should exist after commit")
   157→	}
   158→	if state.Phase != session.PhaseActiveCommitted {
   159→		t.Errorf("Phase after commit-while-active should be %q, got %q",
   160→			session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase)
   161→	}
   162→	t.Logf("Session phase after mid-turn commit: %s", state.Phase)
   163→
   164→	// Verify shadow branch was migrated to the new HEAD
   165→	// The old shadow branch (based on initialHead) may still exist or be cleaned up.
   166→	// The important thing is that the session's BaseCommit was updated.
   167→	if state.BaseCommit == initialHead {
   168→		t.Logf("Note: BaseCommit not yet updated (may happen during migration)")
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	// ========================================
   172→	// Phase 4: Agent finishes turn (SimulateStop)
   173→	// ========================================
   174→	t.Log("Phase 4: Agent finishes turn (SimulateStop)")
   175→
   176→	if err := env.SimulateStop(sess.ID, sess.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   177→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (after commit) failed: %v", err)
   178→	}
   179→
   180→	// Verify session transitions to IDLE
   181→	state, err = env.GetSessionState(sess.ID)
   182→	if err != nil {
   183→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState failed: %v", err)
   184→	}
   185→	if state == nil {
   186→		t.Fatal("Session state should exist after stop")
   187→	}
   188→	if state.Phase != session.PhaseIdle {
   189→		t.Errorf("Phase after stop from ACTIVE_COMMITTED should be %q, got %q",
   190→			session.PhaseIdle, state.Phase)
   191→	}
   192→	t.Logf("Session phase after stop: %s (StepCount: %d)", state.Phase, state.StepCount)
   193→
   194→	// Deferred condensation should have fired during TurnEnd (ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE).
   195→	// Verify StepCount was reset and metadata was persisted to entire/checkpoints/v1.
   196→	if state.StepCount != 0 {
   197→		t.Errorf("StepCount should be 0 after TurnEnd condensation, got %d", state.StepCount)
   198→	}
   199→
   200→	if !env.BranchExists(paths.MetadataBranchName) {
   201→		t.Fatal("entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist after TurnEnd condensation")
   202→	}
   203→	latestCheckpointID := env.TryGetLatestCheckpointID()
   204→	if latestCheckpointID != "" {
   205→		summaryPath := CheckpointSummaryPath(latestCheckpointID)
   206→		if !env.FileExistsInBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, summaryPath) {
   207→			t.Errorf("Checkpoint metadata should exist at %s", summaryPath)
   208→		} else {
   209→			t.Logf("Condensed data exists at checkpoint %s", latestCheckpointID)
   210→		}
   211→	}
   212→
   213→	t.Log("CommitBeforeStop test completed successfully")
   214→}
   215→
   216→// TestShadow_AmendPreservesTrailer tests that `git commit --amend` preserves
   217→// the checkpoint trailer from the original commit.
   218→//
   219→// When a user amends a commit that has an Entire-Checkpoint trailer, the
   220→// prepare-commit-msg hook (called with source="commit") should preserve the
   221→// existing trailer. No duplicate condensation should occur.
   222→//
   223→// Hook behavior tested:
   224→//   - prepare-commit-msg with source="commit": preserves existing trailer
   225→//   - post-commit after amend: no duplicate condensation
   226→func TestShadow_AmendPreservesTrailer(t *testing.T) {
   227→	t.Parallel()
   228→
   229→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   230→
   231→	// ========================================
   232→	// Phase 1: Full workflow - create checkpoint and commit
   233→	// ========================================
   234→	t.Log("Phase 1: Create session, checkpoint, and commit")
   235→
   236→	sess := env.NewSession()
   237→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(sess.ID); err != nil {
   238→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   239→	}
   240→
   241→	// Create a file and checkpoint
   242→	env.WriteFile("main.go", "package main\n\nfunc main() {}\n")
   243→	sess.CreateTranscript("Create main.go", []FileChange{
   244→		{Path: "main.go", Content: "package main\n\nfunc main() {}\n"},
   245→	})
   246→	if err := env.SimulateStop(sess.ID, sess.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   247→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
   248→	}
   249→
   250→	// Commit with hooks (triggers condensation)
   251→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Initial implementation", "main.go")
   252→
   253→	originalCommitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
   254→	originalCheckpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(originalCommitHash)
   255→	if originalCheckpointID == "" {
   256→		t.Fatal("Original commit should have a checkpoint trailer")
   257→	}
   258→	t.Logf("Original commit %s has checkpoint ID: %s", originalCommitHash[:7], originalCheckpointID)
   259→
   260→	// Verify condensation happened
   261→	if !env.BranchExists(paths.MetadataBranchName) {
   262→		t.Fatal("entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist after condensation")
   263→	}
   264→
   265→	// Record the sessions branch state for later comparison
   266→	sessionsCommitBefore := env.GetLatestCommitMessageOnBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   267→	t.Logf("Sessions branch commit before amend:\n%s", sessionsCommitBefore)
   268→
   269→	// ========================================
   270→	// Phase 2: Amend the commit
   271→	// ========================================
   272→	t.Log("Phase 2: Amend the commit")
   273→
   274→	// Amend with the same message (simulating a minor message edit or staging additional files)
   275→	env.GitCommitAmendWithShadowHooks("Initial implementation (amended)")
   276→
   277→	amendedCommitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
   278→	t.Logf("Amended commit: %s", amendedCommitHash[:7])
   279→
   280→	// The amended commit hash should be different from the original
   281→	if amendedCommitHash == originalCommitHash {
   282→		t.Error("Amended commit should have a different hash")
   283→	}
   284→
   285→	// ========================================
   286→	// Phase 3: Verify trailer is preserved
   287→	// ========================================
   288→	t.Log("Phase 3: Verify checkpoint trailer is preserved")
   289→
   290→	amendedCheckpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(amendedCommitHash)
   291→	if amendedCheckpointID == "" {
   292→		t.Fatal("Amended commit should still have a checkpoint trailer")
   293→	}
   294→	t.Logf("Amended commit has checkpoint ID: %s", amendedCheckpointID)
   295→
   296→	// The checkpoint ID should be the SAME as the original
   297→	if amendedCheckpointID != originalCheckpointID {
   298→		t.Errorf("Amended commit should preserve original checkpoint ID.\nOriginal: %s\nAmended:  %s",
   299→			originalCheckpointID, amendedCheckpointID)
   300→	}
   301→
   302→	// ========================================
   303→	// Phase 4: Verify no duplicate condensation
   304→	// ========================================
   305→	t.Log("Phase 4: Verify no duplicate condensation")
   306→
   307→	// The sessions branch commit should not have changed (no new condensation)
   308→	sessionsCommitAfter := env.GetLatestCommitMessageOnBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   309→	if sessionsCommitBefore != sessionsCommitAfter {
   310→		t.Logf("Sessions branch commit changed after amend:\nBefore:\n%s\nAfter:\n%s",
   311→			sessionsCommitBefore, sessionsCommitAfter)
   312→		// This is not necessarily an error -- post-commit might trigger condensation
   313→		// if there's new content. But with no new session activity, it should be the same.
   314→		t.Log("Note: sessions branch was updated (may indicate duplicate condensation)")
   315→	} else {
   316→		t.Log("Sessions branch unchanged after amend (no duplicate condensation)")
   317→	}
   318→
   319→	// Verify the checkpoint data still exists and is accessible
   320→	summaryPath := CheckpointSummaryPath(originalCheckpointID)
   321→	if !env.FileExistsInBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, summaryPath) {
   322→		t.Errorf("Checkpoint metadata should still exist at %s after amend", summaryPath)
   323→	}
   324→
   325→	transcriptPath := SessionFilePath(originalCheckpointID, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   326→	if !env.FileExistsInBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, transcriptPath) {
   327→		t.Errorf("Transcript should still exist at %s after amend", transcriptPath)
   328→	}
   329→
   330→	t.Log("AmendPreservesTrailer test completed successfully")
   331→}
   332→

  "old_string": "// TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_NoCondensation verifies that PostCommit on an\n// ACTIVE session transitions to ACTIVE_COMMITTED without condensing.\n// The shadow branch must be preserved because the session is still active.\nfunc TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_NoCondensation(t *testing.T) {\n\tdir := setupGitRepo(t)\n\tt.Chdir(dir)\n\n\trepo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\ts := &ManualCommitStrategy{}\n\tsessionID := \"test-postcommit-active\"\n\n\t// Initialize session and save a checkpoint so there is shadow branch content\n\tsetupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)\n\n\t// Set phase to ACTIVE (simulating agent mid-turn)\n\tstate, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tstate.Phase = session.PhaseActive\n\trequire.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))\n\n\t// Create a commit WITH the Entire-Checkpoint trailer on the main branch\n\tcommitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, \"a1b2c3d4e5f6\")\n\n\t// Run PostCommit\n\terr = s.PostCommit()\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Verify phase transitioned to ACTIVE_COMMITTED\n\tstate, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, state)\n\tassert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase,\n\t\t\"ACTIVE session should transition to ACTIVE_COMMITTED on GitCommit\")\n\n\t// Verify shadow branch is NOT deleted (session is still active).\n\t// After PostCommit, BaseCommit is updated to new HEAD, so use the current state.\n\tshadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)\n\trefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)\n\t_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)\n\tassert.NoError(t, err,\n\t\t\"shadow branch should be preserved when session is still active\")\n}",
  "new_string": "// TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_Condenses verifies that PostCommit on an\n// ACTIVE session condenses immediately (no deferral) and stays ACTIVE.\nfunc TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_Condenses(t *testing.T) {\n\tdir := setupGitRepo(t)\n\tt.Chdir(dir)\n\n\trepo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\ts := &ManualCommitStrategy{}\n\tsessionID := \"test-postcommit-active\"\n\n\t// Initialize session and save a checkpoint so there is shadow branch content\n\tsetupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)\n\n\t// Set phase to ACTIVE (simulating agent mid-turn)\n\tstate, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tstate.Phase = session.PhaseActive\n\trequire.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))\n\n\t// Create a commit WITH the Entire-Checkpoint trailer on the main branch\n\tcommitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, \"a1b2c3d4e5f6\")\n\n\t// Run PostCommit\n\terr = s.PostCommit()\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Verify phase stays ACTIVE (no more ACTIVE_COMMITTED)\n\tstate, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, state)\n\tassert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActive, state.Phase,\n\t\t\"ACTIVE session should stay ACTIVE on GitCommit (immediate condensation)\")\n\n\t// Verify condensation happened: entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist\n\tsessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err, \"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist after immediate condensation\")\n\tassert.NotNil(t, sessionsRef)\n\n\t// Verify StepCount was reset by condensation\n\tassert.Equal(t, 0, state.StepCount,\n\t\t\"StepCount should be reset after condensation\")\n}"
}

state.StepCount\n\n\t// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer\n\tcommitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, \"d4e5f6a1b2c4\")\n\n\t// Run PostCommit\n\terr = s.PostCommit()\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Verify phase stays ACTIVE_COMMITTED\n\tstate, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tassert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, migration\")\n\n\t// StepCount unchanged (no condensation)\n\tassert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,\n\t\t\"StepCount should be unchanged - no condensation for ACTIVE_COMMITTED\")\n\n\t// entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should NOT exist (no condensation)\n\t_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)\n\trequire.Error(t, err,\n\t\t\"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should NOT exist - no condensation for ACTIVE_COMMITTED\")\n}\n\n// TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_EndedSession_PreservesShadowBranch",

String: // TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_UsesCommitCheckpointID
// verifies that HandleTurnEnd
// uses PendingCheckpointID (set by PostCommit) rather than generating a new one.
// This ensures the condensed metadata matches the commit trailer.
func TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_ReusesCheckpointID(t *testing.T) {
	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
	t.Chdir(dir)

	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
	require.NoError(t, err)

	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
	sessionID := "test-turnend-reuses-cpid"

	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)

	// Simulate PostCommit: transition to ACTIVE_COMMITTED with PendingCheckpointID
	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))

	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "d4e5f6a1b2c3")

	err = s.PostCommit()
	require.NoError(t, err)

	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.Equal(t, "d4e5f6a1b2c3", state.PendingCheckpointID)

	// Run TurnEnd
	result := session.Transition(state.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
	remaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(state, result)

	err = s.HandleTurnEnd(state, remaining)
	require.NoError(t, err)

	// Verify the condensed checkpoint ID matches the commit trailer
	// The LastCheckpointID is set by condenseAndUpdateState on success
	assert.Equal(t, id.CheckpointID("d4e5f6a1b2c3"), state.LastCheckpointID,
		"condensation should use PendingCheckpointID, not generate a new one")
}</tool_use_error>

This triggers PostCommit which:\n\t// - Transitions ACTIVE → ACTIVE_COMMITTED (defers condensation)\n\t// - Migrates shadow branch to new HEAD\n\t// - Updates BaseCommit to new HEAD\n\tcommitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, \"a1b2c3d4e5f6\")\n\n\terr = s.PostCommit()\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Reload state and verify the key invariant:\n\t// BaseCommit has moved to the new HEAD, but AttributionBaseCommit stays at original\n\tstate, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase)\n\n\thead, err := repo.Head()\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tnewHeadHash := head.Hash().String()\n\n\tassert.Equal(t, newHeadHash, state.BaseCommit,\n\t\t\"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD after migration\")\n\tassert.Equal(t, originalBaseCommit, state.AttributionBaseCommit,\n\t\t\"AttributionBaseCommit should still point to original base (commit A)\")\n\tassert.NotEqual(t, state.BaseCommit, state.AttributionBaseCommit,\n\t\t\"BaseCommit and AttributionBaseCommit should diverge after mid-turn commit\")\n\n\t// Now simulate TurnEnd (agent finishes) — deferred condensation runs\n\tresult := session.Transition(state.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})\n\tremaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(state, result)\n\trequire.Contains(t, remaining, session.ActionCondense)\n\n\terr = s.HandleTurnEnd(state, remaining)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// After condensation, verify AttributionBaseCommit is updated to match BaseCommit\n\tassert.Equal(t, state.BaseCommit, state.AttributionBaseCommit,\n\t\t\"AttributionBaseCommit should be updated after successful condensation\")\n\n\t// Verify condensation actually happened\n\t_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err, \"entire/sessions branch should exist after deferred condensation\")\n\n\t// Read back the committed metadata and verify attribution is non-zero.\n\t// The agent modified test.txt (added a line), so AgentLines should be > 0.\n\tstore := checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)\n\tcpID := id.MustCheckpointID(\"a1b2c3d4e5f6\")\n\tcontent, err := store.ReadSessionContent(context.Background(), cpID, 0)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err, \"should be able to read condensed session content\")\n\trequire.NotNil(t, content)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, content.Metadata.InitialAttribution,\n\t\t\"condensed metadata should include attribution\")\n\tassert.Positive(t, content.Metadata.InitialAttribution.TotalCommitted,\n\t\t\"attribution TotalCommitted should be non-zero (agent modified test.txt)\")\n}",
  "new_string": "// TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_AttributionUsesOriginalBase verifies that\n// immediate condensation during PostCommit uses AttributionBaseCommit\n// instead of BaseCommit for attribution, so the diff is non-zero.\n//\n// Scenario: agent modifies a file, user commits mid-turn.\n// PostCommit condenses immediately using AttributionBaseCommit for correct attribution.\nfunc TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_AttributionUsesOriginalBase(t *testing.T) {\n\tdir := setupGitRepo(t)\n\tt.Chdir(dir)\n\n\trepo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\ts := &ManualCommitStrategy{}\n\tsessionID := \"test-immediate-attribution\"\n\n\t// Initialize session and save a checkpoint that includes a modified file.\n\t// The \"agent\" modifies test.txt before saving the checkpoint.\n\tsetupSessionWithFileChange(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)\n\n\t// Record the original base commit (commit A)\n\tstate, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\toriginalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit\n\n\t// Verify AttributionBaseCommit is set at session init\n\tassert.Equal(t, originalBaseCommit, state.AttributionBaseCommit,\n\t\t\"AttributionBaseCommit should equal BaseCommit at session start\")\n\n\t// Set phase to ACTIVE (simulating agent mid-turn)\n\tstate.Phase = session.PhaseActive\n\tstate.FilesTouched = []string{\"test.txt\"}\n\trequire.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))\n\n\t// User commits (creates commit B). This triggers PostCommit which:\n\t// - Stays ACTIVE (immediate condensation, no deferral)\n\t// - Condenses using AttributionBaseCommit for correct attribution\n\t// - Updates BaseCommit to new HEAD\n\tcommitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, \"a1b2c3d4e5f6\")\n\n\terr = s.PostCommit()\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Reload state and verify condensation happened immediately\n\tstate, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tassert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActive, state.Phase,\n\t\t\"phase should stay ACTIVE after immediate condensation\")\n\n\t// After condensation, verify AttributionBaseCommit is updated to match BaseCommit\n\tassert.Equal(t, state.BaseCommit, state.AttributionBaseCommit,\n\t\t\"AttributionBaseCommit should be updated after successful condensation\")\n\n\t// Verify condensation actually happened\n\t_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err, \"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist after immediate condensation\")\n\n\t// Read back the committed metadata and verify attribution is non-zero.\n\t// The agent modified test.txt (added a line), so AgentLines should be > 0.\n\tstore := checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)\n\tcpID := id.MustCheckpointID(\"a1b2c3d4e5f6\")\n\tcontent, err := store.ReadSessionContent(context.Background(), cpID, 0)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err, \"should be able to read condensed session content\")\n\trequire.NotNil(t, content)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, content.Metadata.InitialAttribution,\n\t\t\"condensed metadata should include attribution\")\n\tassert.Positive(t, content.Metadata.InitialAttribution.TotalCommitted,\n\t\t\"attribution TotalCommitted should be non-zero (agent modified test.txt)\")\n}"

PrepareCommitMsg restores\n// the trailer from PendingCheckpointID in session state.\nfunc TestPrepareCommitMsg_AmendRestoresTrailerFromPendingCheckpointID(t *testing.T) {\n\tdir := setupGitRepo(t)\n\tt.Chdir(dir)\n\n\ts := &ManualCommitStrategy{}\n\n\tsessionID := \"test-session-amend-restore\"\n\terr := s.InitializeSession(sessionID, agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode, \"\", \"\")\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Simulate state after condensation: PendingCheckpointID is set\n\tstate, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, state)\n\tstate.PendingCheckpointID = \"abc123def456\"\n\terr = s.saveSessionState(state)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Write a commit message file with NO trailer (user did --amend -m \"new message\")\n\tcommitMsgFile := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), \"COMMIT_EDITMSG\")\n\tnewMsg := \"New amended message\\n\"\n\trequire.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(newMsg), 0o644))\n\n\t// Call PrepareCommitMsg with source=\"commit\" (amend)\n\terr = s.PrepareCommitMsg(commitMsgFile, \"commit\")\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Read the file back - trailer should be restored from PendingCheckpointID\n\tcontent, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\tcpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(string(content))\n\tassert.True(t, found,\n\t\t\"trailer should be restored from PendingCheckpointID on amend\")\n\tassert.Equal(t, \"abc123def456\", cpID.String(),\n\t\t\"restored trailer should use PendingCheckpointID value\")\n}",

PrepareCommitMsg restores\n// the trailer from LastCheckpointID in session state.\nfunc TestPrepareCommitMsg_AmendRestoresTrailerFromLastCheckpointID(t *testing.T) {\n\tdir := setupGitRepo(t)\n\tt.Chdir(dir)\n\n\ts := &ManualCommitStrategy{}\n\n\tsessionID := \"test-session-amend-restore\"\n\terr := s.InitializeSession(sessionID, agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode, \"\", \"\")\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Simulate state after condensation: LastCheckpointID is set\n\tstate, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, state)\n\tstate.LastCheckpointID = \"abc123def456\"\n\terr = s.saveSessionState(state)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Write a commit message file with NO trailer (user did --amend -m \"new message\")\n\tcommitMsgFile := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), \"COMMIT_EDITMSG\")\n\tnewMsg := \"New amended message\\n\"\n\trequire.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(newMsg), 0o644))\n\n\t// Call PrepareCommitMsg with source=\"commit\" (amend)\n\terr = s.PrepareCommitMsg(commitMsgFile, \"commit\")\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Read the file back - trailer should be restored from LastCheckpointID\n\tcontent, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\tcpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(string(content))\n\tassert.True(t, found,\n\t\t\"trailer should be restored from LastCheckpointID on amend\")\n\tassert.Equal(t, \"abc123def456\", cpID.String(),\n\t\t\"restored trailer should use LastCheckpointID value\")\n}"

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/phase_prepare_commit_msg_test.go",
  "old_string": "// TestPrepareCommitMsg_NormalCommitUsesPendingCheckpointID verifies that during\n// a normal commit (source=\"\"), if the session is in ACTIVE_COMMITTED phase with\n// a PendingCheckpointID, the pending ID is reused instead of generating a new one.\n// This ensures idempotent checkpoint IDs across prepare-commit-msg invocations.\nfunc TestPrepareCommitMsg_NormalCommitUsesPendingCheckpointID(t *testing.T) {\n\tdir := setupGitRepo(t)\n\tt.Chdir(dir)\n\n\ts := &ManualCommitStrategy{}\n\n\tsessionID := \"test-session-normal-pending\"\n\terr := s.InitializeSession(sessionID, agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode, \"\", \"\")\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Create content on the shadow branch so filterSessionsWithNewContent finds it\n\tcreateShadowBranchWithTranscript(t, dir, sessionID)\n\n\t// Set the session to ACTIVE_COMMITTED with a PendingCheckpointID\n\tstate, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, state)\n\tstate.Phase = session.PhaseActiveCommitted\n\tstate.PendingCheckpointID = \"fedcba987654\"\n\t// Ensure StepCount reflects that a checkpoint exists on the shadow branch\n\tstate.StepCount = 1\n\terr = s.saveSessionState(state)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Write a commit message file with no trailer (normal editor flow)\n\tcommitMsgFile := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), \"COMMIT_EDITMSG\")\n\tnormalMsg := \"Feature: add new functionality\\n\"\n\trequire.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(normalMsg), 0o644))\n\n\t// Call PrepareCommitMsg with source=\"\" (normal commit, editor flow)\n\terr = s.PrepareCommitMsg(commitMsgFile, \"\")\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Read the file back - trailer should use PendingCheckpointID\n\tcontent, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\tcpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(string(content))\n\tassert.True(t, found,\n\t\t\"trailer should be present for normal commit with active session content\")\n\tassert.Equal(t, \"fedcba987654\", cpID.String(),\n\t\t\"normal commit should reuse PendingCheckpointID instead of generating a new one\")\n}",
  "new_string": "// TestPrepareCommitMsg_NormalCommitGeneratesFreshID verifies that during\n// a normal commit (source=\"\"), if the session has new content on the shadow\n// branch, a fresh checkpoint ID is generated.\nfunc TestPrepareCommitMsg_NormalCommitGeneratesFreshID(t *testing.T) {\n\tdir := setupGitRepo(t)\n\tt.Chdir(dir)\n\n\ts := &ManualCommitStrategy{}\n\n\tsessionID := \"test-session-normal-fresh-id\"\n\terr := s.InitializeSession(sessionID, agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode, \"\", \"\")\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Create content on the shadow branch so filterSessionsWithNewContent finds it\n\tcreateShadowBranchWithTranscript(t, dir, sessionID)\n\n\t// Set the session to ACTIVE with a checkpoint on the shadow branch\n\tstate, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, state)\n\tstate.Phase = session.PhaseActive\n\t// Ensure StepCount reflects that a checkpoint exists on the shadow branch\n\tstate.StepCount = 1\n\terr = s.saveSessionState(state)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Write a commit message file with no trailer (normal editor flow)\n\tcommitMsgFile := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), \"COMMIT_EDITMSG\")\n\tnormalMsg := \"Feature: add new functionality\\n\"\n\trequire.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(normalMsg), 0o644))\n\n\t// Call PrepareCommitMsg with source=\"\" (normal commit, editor flow)\n\terr = s.PrepareCommitMsg(commitMsgFile, \"\")\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Read the file back - trailer should be present with a fresh ID\n\tcontent, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\tcpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(string(content))\n\tassert.True(t, found,\n\t\t\"trailer should be present for normal commit with active session content\")\n\tassert.NotEmpty(t, cpID.String(),\n\t\t\"normal commit should generate a fresh checkpoint ID\")\n}"
}

s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tassert.Equal(t, testPendingCpID, state.PendingCheckpointID,\n\t\t\"PendingCheckpointID should be preserved across SaveChanges calls, {
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/phase_transitions_test.go",
  "old_string": "// TestShadow_CommitBeforeStop tests the \"commit while agent is still working\" flow.\n//\n// When the user commits while the agent is in the ACTIVE phase (between\n// SimulateUserPromptSubmit and SimulateStop), the session should transition to\n// ACTIVE_COMMITTED. This defers condensation because the agent is still working.\n// When the agent finishes its turn (SimulateStop), the deferred condensation fires\n// and the session transitions to IDLE with metadata persisted to entire/checkpoints/v1.\n//\n// State machine transitions tested:\n//   - ACTIVE + GitCommit -> ACTIVE_COMMITTED (defer condensation, migrate shadow branch)\n//   - ACTIVE_COMMITTED + TurnEnd -> IDLE + ActionCondense (deferred condensation fires)\nfunc TestShadow_CommitBeforeStop(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\tenv := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)\n\n\t// ========================================\n\t// Phase 1: Start session and create initial checkpoint\n\t// ========================================\n\tt.Log(\"Phase 1: Start session and create initial work\")\n\n\tsess := env.NewSession()\n\n\t// Pass transcript path in the hook input so it's stored in session state\n\t// (needed for mid-session commit detection via live transcript)\n\tsubmitWithTranscriptPath := func(sessionID, transcriptPath string) {\n\t\tt.Helper()\n\t\tinput := map[string]string{\n\t\t\t\"session_id\":      sessionID,\n\t\t\t\"transcript_path\": transcriptPath,\n\t\t}\n\t\tinputJSON, _ := json.Marshal(input)\n\t\tcmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), \"hooks\", \"claude-code\", \"user-prompt-submit\")\n\t\tcmd.Dir = env.RepoDir\n\t\tcmd.Stdin = bytes.NewReader(inputJSON)\n\t\tcmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),\n\t\t\t\"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"+env.ClaudeProjectDir,\n\t\t)\n\t\tif output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"user-prompt-submit failed: %v\\nOutput: %s\", err, output)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tsubmitWithTranscriptPath(sess.ID, sess.TranscriptPath)\n\n\t// Verify session is ACTIVE\n\tstate, err := env.GetSessionState(sess.ID)\n\tif err != nil     28→		Long: `Scan for stuck or problematic sessions and offer to fix them.
    29→
    30→A session is considered stuck if:
    31→  - It is in ACTIVE or ACTIVE_COMMITTED phase with no interaction for over 1 hour
    32→  - It is in ENDED phase with uncondensed checkpoint data on a shadow branch
    33→
    34→For each stuck session, you can choose to:
    35→  - Condense: Save session data to permanent storage (entire/checkpoints/v1 branch)
    36→  - Discard: Remove the session state and shadow branch data
    37→  - Skip: Leave the session as-is

```
     1→# Sessions and Checkpoints
     2→
     3→## Overview
     4→
     5→Entire CLI creates checkpoints for AI coding sessions. The system is agent-agnostic - it works with Claude Code, Cursor, Copilot, or any tool that triggers Entire hooks.
     6→
     7→## Domain Model
     8→
     9→### Session
    10→
    11→A **Session** is a unit of work. Defined in `strategy/session.go`:
    12→
    13→```go
    14→type Session struct {
    15→    ID          string       // e.g., "2025-12-01-8f76b0e8-b8f1-4a87-9186-848bdd83d62e"
    16→    Description string       // Human-readable summary (first prompt or derived)
    17→    Strategy    string       // Strategy that created this session
    18→    StartTime   time.Time
    19→    Checkpoints []Checkpoint
    20→}
    21→```
    22→
    23→### Checkpoint
    24→
    25→A **Checkpoint** captures a point-in-time within a session. Defined in `strategy/session.go`:
    26→
    27→```go
    28→type Checkpoint struct {
    29→    CheckpointID     id.CheckpointID // Stable 12-hex-char identifier
    30→    Message          string          // Commit message or checkpoint description
    31→    Timestamp        time.Time
    32→    IsTaskCheckpoint bool            // Task checkpoint (subagent) vs session checkpoint
    33→    ToolUseID        string          // Tool use ID for task checkpoints (empty for session)
    34→}
    35→```
    36→
    37→### Checkpoint Types
    38→
    39→The low-level `checkpoint.Type` (from `checkpoint/checkpoint.go`) indicates storage location:
    40→
    41→```go
    42→type Type int
    43→
    44→const (
    45→    Temporary Type = iota // Full state snapshot, shadow branch
    46→    Committed             // Metadata + commit ref, entire/checkpoints/v1
    47→)
    48→```
    49→
    50→| Type | Contents | Use Case |
    51→|------|----------|----------|
    52→| Temporary | Full state (code + metadata) | Intra-session rewind, pre-commit |
    53→| Committed | Metadata + commit reference | Permanent record, post-commit rewind |
    54→
    55→## Interface
    56→
    57→### Session Operations
    58→
    59→Sessions are accessed via standalone functions in `strategy/session.go`:
    60→
    61→```go
    62→// ListSessions returns all sessions from entire/checkpoints/v1,
    63→// plus additional sessions from strategies implementing SessionSource.
    64→func ListSessions() ([]Session, error)
    65→
    66→// GetSession finds a session by ID (supports prefix matching).
    67→func GetSession(sessionID string) (*Session, error)
    68→```
    69→
    70→### Checkpoint Storage (Low-Level)
    71→
    72→The `checkpoint.Store` interface (from `checkpoint/checkpoint.go`) provides primitives for reading/writing checkpoints. Used by strategies.
    73→
    74→```go
    75→type Store interface {
    76→    // Temporary checkpoint operations (shadow branches - full state)
    77→    WriteTemporary(ctx context.Context, opts WriteTemporaryOptions) (WriteTemporaryResult, error)
    78→    ReadTemporary(ctx context.Context, baseCommit, worktreeID string) (*ReadTemporaryResult, error)
    79→    ListTemporary(ctx context.Context) ([]TemporaryInfo, error)
    80→
    81→    // Committed checkpoint operations (entire/checkpoints/v1 branch - metadata only)
    82→    WriteCommitted(ctx context.Context, opts WriteCommittedOptions) error
    83→    ReadCommitted(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (*CheckpointSummary, error)
    84→    ReadSessionContent(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, sessionIndex int) (*SessionContent, error)
    85→    ReadSessionContentByID(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, sessionID string) (*SessionContent, error)
    86→    ListCommitted(ctx context.Context) ([]CommittedInfo, error)
    87→}
    88→```
    89→
    90→Key option types (abbreviated):
    91→
    92→```go
    93→type WriteTemporaryOptions struct {
    94→    SessionID      string
    95→    BaseCommit     string
    96→    WorktreeID     string   // Internal git worktree identifier (empty for main)
    97→    ModifiedFiles  []string
    98→    NewFiles       []string
    99→    DeletedFiles   []string
   100→    MetadataDir    string   // Relative path to metadata directory
   101→    MetadataDirAbs string   // Absolute path
   102→    CommitMessage  string
   103→    // ...
   104→}
   105→
   106→type WriteCommittedOptions struct {
   107→    CheckpointID id.CheckpointID
   108→    SessionID    string
   109→    Strategy     string
   110→    Branch       string
   111→    Transcript   []byte
   112→    Prompts      []string
   113→    Context      []byte
   114→    FilesTouched []string
   115→    TokenUsage   *agent.TokenUsage
   116→    // ...
   117→}
   118→```
   119→
   120→Token usage is defined in `agent/types.go`:
   121→
   122→```go
   123→type TokenUsage struct {
   124→    InputTokens         int         `json:"input_tokens"`
   125→    CacheCreationTokens int         `json:"cache_creation_tokens"`
   126→    CacheReadTokens     int         `json:"cache_read_tokens"`
   127→    OutputTokens        int         `json:"output_tokens"`
   128→    APICallCount        int         `json:"api_call_count"`
   129→    SubagentTokens      *TokenUsage `json:"subagent_tokens,omitempty"`
   130→}
   131→```
   132→
   133→### Strategy-Level Operations
   134→
   135→Strategies compose low-level primitives into higher-level workflows.
   136→
   137→**Manual-commit** has condensation logic:
   138→
   139→```go
   140→// CondenseSession reads accumulated temporary state and writes a committed checkpoint.
   141→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CondenseSession(
   142→    repo *git.Repository,
   143→    checkpointID id.CheckpointID,
   144→    state *SessionState,
   145→) (*CondenseResult, error)
   146→```
   147→
   148→**Auto-commit** writes committed checkpoints directly:
   149→
   150→```go
   151→// SaveChanges creates a commit on the active branch and writes metadata.
   152→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) SaveChanges(ctx SaveContext) error
   153→```
   154→
   155→## Storage
   156→
   157→| Type | Location | Contents |
   158→|------|----------|----------|
   159→| Session State | `.git/entire-sessions/<id>.json` | Active session tracking |
   160→| Temporary | `entire/<commit[:7]>-<worktreeHash[:6]>` branch | Full state (code + metadata) |
   161→| Committed | `entire/checkpoints/v1` branch (sharded) | Metadata + commit reference |
   162→
   163→### Session State
   164→
   165→Location: `.git/entire-sessions/<session-id>.json`
   166→
   167→Stored in git common dir (shared across worktrees). Tracks active session info.
   168→
   169→### Temporary Checkpoints
   170→
   171→Branch: `entire/<commit[:7]>-<worktreeHash[:6]>`
   172→
   173→Contains full worktree snapshot plus metadata overlay. **Multiple concurrent sessions** can share the same shadow branch - their checkpoints interleave:
   174→
   175→```
   176→<worktree files...>
   177→.entire/metadata/<session-id-1>/
   178→├── full.jsonl           # Session 1 transcript
   179→├── prompt.txt           # User prompts
   180→├── context.md           # Generated context
   181→└── tasks/<tool-use-id>/ # Task checkpoints
   182→.entire/metadata/<session-id-2>/
   183→├── full.jsonl           # Session 2 transcript (concurrent)
   184→├── ...
   185→```
   186→
   187→Tied to a base commit. Condensed to committed on user commit.
   188→
   189→**Shadow branch lifecycle:**
   190→- Created on first checkpoint for a base commit
   191→- Migrated automatically if base commit changes (stash → pull → apply scenario)
   192→- Deleted after condensation to `entire/checkpoints/v1`
   193→- Reset if orphaned (no session state file exists)
   194→
   195→### Committed Checkpoints
   196→
   197→Branch: `entire/checkpoints/v1`
   198→
   199→Metadata only, sharded by checkpoint ID. Supports **multiple sessions per checkpoint**:
   200→
   201→```
   202→<id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/
   203→├── metadata.json        # CheckpointSummary (aggregated stats)
   204→├── 0/                   # First session (0-based indexing)
   205→│   ├── metadata.json    # Session-specific CommittedMetadata
   206→│   ├── full.jsonl
   207→│   ├── prompt.txt
   208→│   ├── context.md
   209→│   └── content_hash.txt
   210→├── 1/                   # Second session
   211→│   ├── metadata.json
   212→│   ├── full.jsonl
   213→│   └── ...
   214→└── 2/                   # Third session...
   215→```
   216→
   217→**Root-level metadata.json (`CheckpointSummary`):**
   218→```json
   219→{
   220→  "checkpoint_id": "abc123def456",
   221→  "strategy": "manual-commit",
   222→  "branch": "main",
   223→  "checkpoints_count": 3,
   224→  "files_touched": ["file1.txt", "file2.txt"],
   225→  "sessions": [
   226→    {
   227→      "metadata": "/ab/c123def456/0/metadata.json",
   228→      "transcript": "/ab/c123def456/0/full.jsonl",
   229→      "context": "/ab/c123def456/0/context.md",
   230→      "content_hash": "/ab/c123def456/0/content_hash.txt",
   231→      "prompt": "/ab/c123def456/0/prompt.txt"
   232→    }
   233→  ],
   234→  "token_usage": {
   235→    "input_tokens": 1500,
   236→    "cache_creation_tokens": 200,
   237→    "cache_read_tokens": 800,
   238→    "output_tokens": 500,
   239→    "api_call_count": 3
   240→  }
   241→}
   242→```
   243→
   244→When condensing multiple concurrent sessions:
   245→- All sessions are stored in numbered subdirectories using 0-based indexing (`0/`, `1/`, `2/`, ...)
   246→- Each `session_id` is assigned a stable index; subsequent writes for the same session reuse the same numbered folder
   247→- New `session_id` values are appended at the next index, so higher-numbered folders correspond to more recently introduced sessions, not necessarily the chronologically latest activity
   248→- `sessions` array in `CheckpointSummary` maps each session to its file paths
   249→- `files_touched` is merged from all sessions
   250→
   251→### Checkpoint ID Linking
   252→
   253→The checkpoint ID is the **stable identifier** that links user commits to metadata across branches.
   254→
   255→**Format:** 12-hex-character random ID (e.g., `a3b2c4d5e6f7`)
   256→
   257→**Generation:**
   258→- Manual-commit: Generated during condensation (post-commit hook)
   259→- Auto-commit: Generated when creating the commit
   260→
   261→**Usage:**
   262→
   263→1. **User commit trailer** (both strategies):
   264→   - `Entire-Checkpoint: a3b2c4d5e6f7` added to user's commit message
   265→   - Auto-commit: Added programmatically
   266→   - Manual-commit: Added by `prepare-commit-msg` hook (user can remove)
   267→
   268→2. **Directory sharding** on `entire/checkpoints/v1`:
   269→   - Path: `<id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/` (e.g., `a3/b2c4d5e6f7/`)
   270→   - First 2 chars = shard (256 possible shards)
   271→   - Remaining 10 chars = directory name
   272→
   273→3. **Commit subject** on `entire/checkpoints/v1`:
   274→   - Format: `Checkpoint: a3b2c4d5e6f7`
   275→   - Makes `git log entire/checkpoints/v1` readable
   276→
   277→**Bidirectional Lookup:**
   278→
   279→```
   280→User commit → Metadata:
   281→  1. Extract "Entire-Checkpoint: a3b2c4d5e6f7" from commit message
   282→  2. Look up metadata:
   283→     - Approach A: Read entire/checkpoints/v1 tree at a3/b2c4d5e6f7/
   284→     - Approach B: Search git log entire/checkpoints/v1 for "Checkpoint: a3b2c4d5e6f7"
   285→
   286→Metadata → User commits:
   287→  Given checkpoint ID a3b2c4d5e6f7
   288→  → Search branch history for commits with "Entire-Checkpoint: a3b2c4d5e6f7"
   289→```
   290→
   291→**Example Flow:**
   292→
   293→```
   294→                    User creates commit
   295→                           ↓
   296→           prepare-commit-msg hook adds trailer
   297→                           ↓
   298→┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
   299→│ Commit on main branch:                           │
   300→│   "Implement login feature                       │
   301→│                                                   │
   302→│   Entire-Checkpoint: a3b2c4d5e6f7"               │
   303→└──────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
   304→                           ↓
   305→                  post-commit hook runs
   306→                           ↓
   307→          Condense shadow → entire/checkpoints/v1
   308→                           ↓
   309→┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
   310→│ Commit on entire/checkpoints/v1:                 │
   311→│   Subject: "Checkpoint: a3b2c4d5e6f7"            │
   312→│                                                   │
   313→│   Tree: a3/b2c4d5e6f7/                           │
   314→│     ├── metadata.json                            │
   315→│     │   (checkpoint_id: "a3b2c4d5e6f7")          │
   316→│     ├── 0/                                       │
   317→│     │   ├── full.jsonl                           │
   318→│     │   ├── prompt.txt                           │
   319→│     │   └── context.md                           │
   320→│     └── ...                                      │
   321→│                                                   │
   322→│   Trailers:                                      │
   323→│     Entire-Session: 2026-01-20-uuid              │
   324→│     Entire-Strategy: manual-commit               │
   325→└──────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
   326→```
   327→
   328→The checkpoint ID creates a **bidirectional link**: user commits can find their metadata, and metadata can find the commits that reference it.
   329→
   330→### Package Structure
   331→
   332→```
   333→strategy/
   334→├── session.go           # Session and Checkpoint types, ListSessions(), GetSession()
   335→
   336→session/
   337→├── state.go             # Active session state (StateStore, .git/entire-sessions/)
   338→├── phase.go             # Session phase state machine (ACTIVE, IDLE, ENDED, etc.)
   339→
   340→checkpoint/
   341→├── checkpoint.go        # checkpoint.Type, checkpoint.Store interface, CheckpointSummary, etc.
   342→├── store.go             # GitStore implementation
   343→├── temporary.go         # Shadow branch storage
   344→├── committed.go         # Metadata branch storage
   345→├── id/                  # CheckpointID type and generation
   346→│   └── id.go
   347→```
   348→
   349→Strategies use `checkpoint.Store` primitives - storage details are encapsulated.
   350→
   351→## Strategy Role
   352→
   353→Strategies determine checkpoint timing and type:
   354→
   355→| Strategy | On Save | On Task Complete | On User Commit |
   356→|----------|---------|------------------|----------------|
   357→| Manual-commit | Temporary | Temporary | Condense → Committed |
   358→| Auto-commit | Committed | Committed | — |
   359→
   360→## Rewind
   361→
   362→Each `RewindPoint` includes `SessionID` and `SessionPrompt` to help identify which checkpoint belongs to which session when multiple sessions are interleaved.
   363→
   364→## Concurrent Sessions
   365→
   366→Multiple AI sessions can run concurrently on the same base commit:
   367→
   368→1. **Warning on start** - When a second session starts while another has uncommitted checkpoints, a warning is shown
   369→2. **Both proceed** - User can continue; checkpoints interleave on the same shadow branch
   370→3. **Identification** - Each checkpoint is tagged with its session ID; rewind UI shows session prompt
   371→4. **Condensation** - On commit, all sessions are condensed together with archived subfolders
   372→
   373→### Conflict Handling
   374→
   375→| Scenario | Behavior |
   376→|----------|----------|
   377→| Concurrent sessions (same worktree) | Warning shown, both proceed |
   378→| Orphaned shadow branch (no state file) | Branch reset, new session proceeds |
   379→| Cross-worktree conflict (state file exists) | `SessionIDConflictError` returned |
   380→
   381→### Shadow Branch Migration
   382→
   383→If user does stash → pull → apply (HEAD changes without commit):
   384→- Detection: base commit changed AND old shadow branch still exists
   385→- Action: branch renamed from `entire/<old-commit[:7]>-<worktreeHash[:6]>` to `entire/<new-commit[:7]>-<worktreeHash[:6]>`
   386→- Result: session continues with checkpoints preserved
   387→
   388→---
   389→
   390→## Appendix: Legacy Names
   391→
   392→| Current | Legacy |
   393→|---------|--------|
   394→| Manual-commit | Shadow |
   395→| Auto-commit | Dual |
   396→

But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.
</system-reminder>

```

     1→# Entire - CLI 
     2→
     3→This repo contains the CLI for Entire.
     4→
     5→## Architecture
     6→
     7→- CLI build with github.com/spf13/cobra and github.com/charmbracelet/huh 
     8→
     9→## Key Directories
    10→
    11→### Commands (`cmd/`)
    12→- `entire/`: Main CLI entry point
    13→- `entire/cli`: CLI utilities and helpers
    14→- `entire/cli/commands`: actual command implementations
    15→- `entire/cli/strategy`: strategy implementations - see section below
    16→- `entire/cli/checkpoint`: checkpoint storage abstractions (temporary and committed)
    17→- `entire/cli/session`: session state management
    18→- `entire/cli/integration_test`: integration tests
    19→
    20→## Tech Stack
    21→
    22→- Language: Go 1.25.x
    23→- Build tool: mise, go modules
    24→- Linting: golangci-lint
    25→
    26→## Development
    27→
    28→### Running Tests
    29→```bash
    30→mise run test
    31→```
    32→
    33→### Running Integration Tests
    34→```bash
    35→mise run test:integration
    36→```
    37→
    38→### Running All Tests (CI)
    39→```bash
    40→mise run test:ci
    41→```
    42→
    43→Integration tests use the `//go:build integration` build tag and are located in `cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/`.
    44→
    45→### Test Parallelization
    46→
    47→**Always use `t.Parallel()` in tests.** Every top-level test function and subtest should call `t.Parallel()` unless it modifies process-global state (e.g., `os.Chdir()`).
    48→
    49→```go
    50→func TestFeature_Foo(t *testing.T) {
    51→    t.Parallel()
    52→    // ...
    53→}
    54→
    55→// Integration tests: RunForAllStrategies handles t.Parallel() for subtests internally,
    56→// but the top-level test still needs it
    57→func TestFeature_Bar(t *testing.T) {
    58→    t.Parallel()
    59→    RunForAllStrategies(t, func(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, strategyName string) {
    60→        // ...
    61→    })
    62→}
    63→```
    64→
    65→**Exception:** Tests that modify process-global state cannot be parallelized. This includes `os.Chdir()`/`t.Chdir()` and `os.Setenv()`/`t.Setenv()` — Go's test framework will panic if these are used after `t.Parallel()`.
    66→
    67→### Linting and Formatting
    68→```bash
    69→mise run fmt && mise run lint
    70→```
    71→
    72→### Before Every Commit (REQUIRED)
    73→
    74→**CI will fail if you skip these steps:**
    75→
    76→```bash
    77→mise run fmt      # Format code (CI enforces gofmt)
    78→mise run lint     # Lint check (CI enforces golangci-lint)
    79→mise run test:ci  # Run all tests (unit + integration)
    80→```
    81→
    82→Or combined: `mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci`
    83→
    84→**Common CI failures from skipping this:**
    85→- `gofmt` formatting differences → run `mise run fmt`
    86→- Lint errors → run `mise run lint` and fix issues
    87→- Test failures → run `mise run test` and fix
    88→
    89→### Code Duplication Prevention
    90→
    91→Before implementing Go code, use `/go:discover-related` to find existing utilities and patterns that might be reusable.
    92→
    93→**Check for duplication:**
    94→```bash
    95→mise run dup           # Comprehensive check (threshold 50) with summary
    96→mise run dup:staged    # Check only staged files
    97→mise run lint          # Normal lint includes dupl at threshold 75 (new issues only)
    98→mise run lint:full     # All issues at threshold 75
    99→```
   100→

   325→
   326→#### Session Phase State Machine
   327→
   328→Sessions track their lifecycle through phases managed by a state machine in `session/phase.go`:
   329→
   330→**Phases:** `ACTIVE`, `ACTIVE_COMMITTED`, `IDLE`, `ENDED`
   331→
   332→**Events:**
   333→- `TurnStart` - Agent begins a turn (UserPromptSubmit hook)
   334→- `TurnEnd` - Agent finishes a turn (Stop hook)
   335→- `GitCommit` - A git commit was made (PostCommit hook)
   336→- `SessionStart` - New session started
   337→- `SessionStop` - Session explicitly stopped
   338→
   339→**Key transitions:**
   340→- `IDLE + TurnStart → ACTIVE` - Agent starts working
   341→- `ACTIVE + TurnEnd → IDLE` - Agent finishes turn
   342→- `ACTIVE + GitCommit → ACTIVE_COMMITTED` - User commits while agent is working (condensation deferred)
   343→- `ACTIVE_COMMITTED + TurnEnd → IDLE` - Agent finishes after commit (condense now)
   344→- `IDLE + GitCommit → IDLE` - User commits between turns (condense immediately)
   345→- `ENDED + GitCommit → ENDED` - Post-session commit (condense if files touched)
   346→
   347→The state machine emits **actions** (e.g., `ActionCondense`, `ActionMigrateShadowBranch`, `ActionDeferCondensation`) that hook handlers dispatch to strategy-specific implementations.
   348→
   349→#### Metadata Structure
   350→
   351→**Shadow Strategy** - Shadow branches (`entire/<commit-hash[:7]>-<worktreeHash[:6]>`):
   352→```
   353→.entire/metadata/<session-id>/
   354→├── full.jsonl               # Session transcript

<system-reminder>

But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.
</system-reminder>

   605→		)
   606→	}
   607→
   608→	// Track shadow branch names and whether they can be deleted
   609→	shadowBranchesToDelete := make(map[string]struct{})
   610→	// Track sessions that are still active AFTER transitions and were NOT condensed.
   611→	// Only uncondensed active sessions should prevent shadow branch deletion.
   612→	uncondensedActiveOnBranch := make(map[string]bool)
   613→
   614→	newHead := head.Hash().String()
   615→
   616→	for _, state := range sessions {
   617→		shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   618→
   619→		// Check for new content (needed for TransitionContext and condensation).
   620→		// Fail-open: if content check errors, assume new content exists so we
   621→		// don't silently skip data that should have been condensed.
   622→		hasNew, contentErr := s.sessionHasNewContent(repo, state)
   623→		if contentErr != nil {
   624→			hasNew = true
   625→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: error checking session content, assuming new content",
   626→				slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   627→				slog.String("error", contentErr.Error()),
   628→			)
   629→		}
   630→		transitionCtx.HasFilesTouched = len(state.FilesTouched) > 0
   631→
   632→		// Run the state machine transition
   633→		remaining := TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventGitCommit, transitionCtx)
   634→
   635→		// Dispatch strategy-specific actions.
   636→		// Each branch handles its own BaseCommit update so there is no
   637→		// fallthrough conditional at the end. On condensation failure,
   638→		// BaseCommit is intentionally NOT updated to preserve access to
   639→		// the shadow branch (which is named after the old BaseCommit).
   640→		condensed := false
   641→		for _, action := range remaining {
   642→			switch action {
   643→			case session.ActionCondense:
   644→				if hasNew {
   645→					s.condenseAndUpdateState(logCtx, repo, checkpointID, state, head, shadowBranchName, shadowBranchesToDelete)
   646→					condensed = true
   647→					// condenseAndUpdateState updates BaseCommit on success.
   648→					// On failure, BaseCommit is preserved so the shadow branch remains accessible.
   649→				} else {
   650→					// No new content to condense — just update BaseCommit
   651→					s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)
   652→				}
   653→			case session.ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched:
   654→				// The state machine already gates this action on HasFilesTouched,
   655→				// but hasNew is an additional content-level check (transcript has
   656→				// new content beyond what was previously condensed).
   657→				if len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 && hasNew {
   658→					s.condenseAndUpdateState(logCtx, repo, checkpointID, state, head, shadowBranchName, shadowBranchesToDelete)
   659→					condensed = true
   660→					// On failure, BaseCommit is preserved (same as ActionCondense).
   661→				} else {
   662→					s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)
   663→				}
   664→			case session.ActionDiscardIfNoFiles:
   665→				if len(state.FilesTouched) == 0 {
   666→					logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: skipping empty ended session (no files to condense)",
   667→						slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   668→					)
   669→				}
   670→				s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)
   671→			case session.ActionClearEndedAt, session.ActionUpdateLastInteraction:
   672→				// Handled by session.ApplyCommonActions above
   673→			case session.ActionWarnStaleSession:
   674→				// Not produced by EventGitCommit; listed for switch exhaustiveness
   675→			}
   676→		}
   677→
   678→		// Save the updated state
   679→		if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   680→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", err)
   681→		}
   682→
   683→		// Only preserve shadow branch for active sessions that were NOT condensed.
   684→		// Condensed sessions already have their data on entire/checkpoints/v1.
   685→		if state.Phase.IsActive() && !condensed {
   686→			uncondensedActiveOnBranch[shadowBranchName] = true
   687→		}
   688→	}
   689→
   690→	// Clean up shadow branches — only delete when no uncondensed active sessions need them
   691→	for shadowBranchName := range shadowBranchesToDelete {

> AGENT

I see the issue. The ACTIVE session now gets `ActionCondense` (from the state machine change), which means it gets condensed. After condensation, `condensed = true`, so it does NOT get added to `uncondensedActiveOnBranch`. This means the shadow branch gets deleted.

But the test expects the shadow branch to still exist for the active session after PostCommit. The question is: should the shadow branch be preserved for ACTIVE sessions that were just condensed?

In the new model, ACTIVE + GitCommit → ACTIVE + ActionCondense. The session is condensed, data is on `entire/checkpoints/v1`, and BaseCommit is updated. The shadow branch for the OLD base commit is no longer needed. A new shadow branch (for the new base commit) would be created on the next `SaveChanges` call.

So the test expectation is wrong. Let me look at what the test is actually checking — it's looking for `activeState.BaseCommit`'s shadow branch after PostCommit. After condensation, BaseCommit gets updated to the new HEAD, so it would look for a shadow branch at the new commit hash, which wouldn't exist yet (it would be created on the next SaveChanges).

The test needs to be updated: after PostCommit, the active session should be condensed, its BaseCommit updated to new HEAD, and the old shadow branch deleted. The shadow branch for the new base doesn't exist until the next SaveChanges.

- After condensation, BaseCommit is updated to the new HEAD for both
- The old shadow branch is deleted (both sessions were condensed, so neither is "uncondensed active")

> AGENT

Two issues:
1. The ACTIVE session's StepCount was NOT reset (still 1) — meaning the active session was not condensed
2. The shadow branch was NOT deleted

The active session has StepCount=1 but only a session state file — it doesn't have actual content on the shadow branch (the `setupSessionWithCheckpoint` was only for the idle session). The ACTIVE session with StepCount=1 has `hasNew` check. Let me check what `sessionHasNewContent` returns for a session state that was manually created without actual content on the shadow branch.

The ACTIVE session was created manually (not through `setupSessionWithCheckpoint`), so it has no actual transcript data on the shadow branch. The `hasNew` check might return false because there's nothing new to condense, OR it might error out and fall through to `hasNew = true`.

Let me trace through the logic: the active session has `StepCount: 1` but no actual data on the shadow branch. When `sessionHasNewContent` is called, it would try to read the shadow branch content and compare with what was previously condensed. Since there's no content on the shadow branch for this session, it would likely return `false` (no new content).

When `hasNew` is `false` for ActionCondense, it goes to the else branch: `s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)` — just updates BaseCommit without condensing. This means `condensed` stays `false`, and the session gets marked as an uncondensed active session, preserving the shadow branch.

This is actually correct behavior! The active session has no content to condense (it was just created with a manual state but has no actual shadow branch data). The test setup needs to be different — either the active session needs actual content, or we should accept that a content-less active session correctly preserves the branch.

Let me reconsider what this test is really about. The original test was about: "when you have two sessions on the same branch, one IDLE and one ACTIVE, the shadow branch should be preserved after PostCommit because the active session still needs it."

In the new model, if the active session has content, it gets condensed too and the branch CAN be deleted. If the active session has no content, it's not condensed and the branch is preserved (correct behavior — the idle session's data was condensed from the same branch).

Let me update the test to properly reflect the scenario: the active session has no new content, so it's not condensed, and the shadow branch is correctly preserved.

**Refactoring: Condense at Each Commit (Remove Deferred Condensation)**

All 9 steps are done. `mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci` passes clean.

**What changed:**
- **State machine**: Removed `ACTIVE_COMMITTED` phase and `ActionMigrateShadowBranch`. `ACTIVE + GitCommit → ACTIVE + [ActionCondense]` (immediate condensation)
- **Session state**: Removed `PendingCheckpointID` field (backward compat: old JSON files silently ignore it)
- **PostCommit**: Single-pass architecture — no more two-pass pending migrations. Shadow branch cleanup tracks condensed vs uncondensed active sessions
- **PrepareCommitMsg**: Only checks `LastCheckpointID` for amend trailer restoration
- **HandleTurnEnd**: No longer dispatches `ActionCondense` (TurnEnd is just `ActionUpdateLastInteraction`)
- **Deleted**: `handleTurnEndCondense`, `hasOtherActiveSessionsOnBranch`, `transitionFromActiveCommitted`, `pendingMigration` type
- **Tests**: All updated — removed ACTIVE_COMMITTED tests, added immediate condensation tests, fixed duplicate names

question: if claude would split up code changes into multiple commits, so basically:

1. generates file changes in 3 files
2. makes a commit with 2 files
3. makes a commit with the remaining one file

That wouldn't matter from a shadow branch perspective, since there isn't even one yet if this is one prompt in total right?

> AGENT

Good question, but there actually **would** be a shadow branch by that point. Each file edit/write triggers a `PostToolUse` hook that calls `SaveChanges()`, which creates checkpoints on the shadow branch. So the timeline is:

1. Claude edits file1 → PostToolUse[Write] → `SaveChanges()` → shadow branch created
2. Claude edits file2 → PostToolUse[Write] → checkpoint added
3. Claude edits file3 → PostToolUse[Write] → checkpoint added
4. Claude runs `git commit` (2 files) → post-commit → condenses all 3 checkpoints → shadow branch deleted, StepCount reset to 0
5. Claude runs `git commit` (1 file) → post-commit → StepCount=0, no new content → **no condensation** (nothing to condense)

So it works correctly, but the second commit wouldn't get an `Entire-Checkpoint` trailer since `prepare-commit-msg` sees StepCount=0. All the session data (transcript, prompts, context for all 3 edits) was already captured with commit #1.

The only scenario where there truly wouldn't be a shadow branch is if Claude somehow committed files without going through the Write/Edit tools first (which shouldn't happen in practice).

> DEVELOPER

5. Claude runs git commit (1 file) → post-commit → StepCount=0, no new content → no condensation (nothing to condense)

hmm this is wrong then, we would want the same data as in the other checkpoint just with a new id

> AGENT

You're right. Each commit should be traceable back to the session, even if the session data hasn't changed much between commits.

The problem is: after commit #1 condenses, the shadow branch gets deleted and StepCount resets to 0. When commit #2 happens, there's nothing left to condense from.

The simplest fix would be: **don't delete the shadow branch when the session is still ACTIVE**. The shadow branch stays around for the rest of the turn, so subsequent commits can condense from it. It only gets cleaned up when the session transitions to IDLE (TurnEnd) or on the next commit where no active session needs it.

In the PostCommit cleanup logic, that would mean: an ACTIVE session always preserves its shadow branch, regardless of whether it was just condensed.
