basically I think what would work best is: 

1. commit is made, condensation happen
2. are there still files left that were part of the shadow branch?
-> create a new shadow branch with the files left, but include all metadata from before

Does this make sense?

> AGENT

Yes, that makes sense. After condensation:

1. Compare `state.FilesTouched` against the files in the commit
2. If there are remaining touched files not yet committed → create a new shadow branch at the new HEAD with those files + carry forward all metadata (transcript, prompts, context)
3. Keep `StepCount > 0` so the next commit knows there's data to condense

This way each commit gets its own checkpoint ID, and the in-progress work is never lost between commits. It's essentially a `SaveChanges()` call after condensation for the leftover state.

Perfect! Now I have everything I need. Let me provide a comprehensive exploration report:

## PostCommit and Condensation Flow - Comprehensive Exploration

I've thoroughly analyzed the PostCommit and condensation flow in the manual-commit strategy. Here's a detailed breakdown covering all your questions:

---

### 1. **PostCommit() Method** (`manual_commit_hooks.go:553-710`)

#### **High-Level Flow:**
The `PostCommit()` hook is called after a git commit is created. It:
1. Checks if the commit has an `Entire-Checkpoint` trailer (if not, just updates BaseCommit for active sessions and returns)
2. Finds all active sessions for the current worktree
3. Runs the session state machine to determine what actions to take (condense, skip, etc.)
4. Dispatches strategy-specific actions for each session
5. Cleans up shadow branches when appropriate

#### **Condensation Loop** (lines 616-688):
```
For each session:
  1. Check if session has new content (in shadow branch or live transcript)
  2. Run state machine: TransitionAndLog(state, EventGitCommit, context) 
  3. Dispatch returned actions (ActionCondense, ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched, etc.)
  4. Save updated session state
  5. Track which shadow branches can be cleaned up
  6. Clean up shadow branches (only if no uncondensed active sessions need them)
```

#### **How `shadowBranchesToDelete` and `uncondensedActiveOnBranch` Interact:**

```go
shadowBranchesToDelete := make(map[string]struct{})              // Lines 609
uncondensedActiveOnBranch := make(map[string]bool)              // Line 612

// During condensation (line 645):
s.condenseAndUpdateState(logCtx, repo, checkpointID, state, head, shadowBranchName, shadowBranchesToDelete)
// ^ Adds shadowBranchName to shadowBranchesToDelete if condensation succeeds

// After condensation check (lines 683-687):
if state.Phase.IsActive() && !condensed {
    uncondensedActiveOnBranch[shadowBranchName] = true
}

// Cleanup (lines 691-707):
for shadowBranchName := range shadowBranchesToDelete {
    if uncondensedActiveOnBranch[shadowBranchName] {
        // Skip deletion - uncondensed active session still using it
        continue
    }
    // Delete the branch
}
```

**Key Insight:** A shadow branch is only deleted if:
1. At least one session that used it was successfully condensed (added to `shadowBranchesToDelete`), AND
2. No OTHER session is still active on that branch and hasn't been condensed (`uncondensedActiveOnBranch` is empty for that branch)

This handles concurrent sessions on the same branch correctly.

#### **The `condenseAndUpdateState` Function** (lines 714-765):

```go
func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) condenseAndUpdateState(
    logCtx context.Context,
    repo *git.Repository,
    checkpointID id.CheckpointID,
    state *SessionState,
    head *plumbing.Reference,
    shadowBranchName string,
    shadowBranchesToDelete map[string]struct{},
) {
    // 1. Call CondenseSession to write metadata to entire/checkpoints/v1
    result, err := s.CondenseSession(repo, checkpointID, state)
    
    // 2. On success, update state:
    state.BaseCommit = newHead                       // Update to new HEAD
    state.AttributionBaseCommit = newHead            // Update attribution base too
    state.StepCount = 0                              // Reset checkpoint count (no shadow checkpoints yet)
    state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = result.TotalTranscriptLines  // Update to end of transcript
    state.PromptAttributions = nil                   // Clear accumulated attributions
    state.PendingPromptAttribution = nil             // Clear pending attribution
    state.FilesTouched = nil                         // Clear tracked files (condensed to metadata)
    state.LastCheckpointID = checkpointID            // Save for reuse on split commits
    
    // 3. Add to cleanup list
    shadowBranchesToDelete[shadowBranchName] = struct{}{}
}
```

**State Updates After Condensation:**
- **BaseCommit/AttributionBaseCommit**: Updated to current HEAD (the commit being condensed)
- **StepCount**: Reset to 0 (no checkpoints on shadow branch yet, session can start fresh from this commit)
- **CheckpointTranscriptStart**: Set to total lines in transcript (future checkpoints know where they start)
- **FilesTouched**: Cleared (these are now stored in the condensed checkpoint metadata on `entire/checkpoints/v1`)
- **PromptAttributions**: Cleared (already used for condensation)
- **LastCheckpointID**: Saved (so split commits can reuse this checkpoint ID)

---

### 2. **saveCheckpointToShadowBranch** (in `manual_commit_git.go`)

This function is actually **`WriteTemporary` in `checkpoint/temporary.go:56-141`**. The manual-commit strategy uses the checkpoint store's `WriteTemporary` method.

#### **Function Signature:**
```go
func (s *GitStore) WriteTemporary(ctx context.Context, opts WriteTemporaryOptions) (WriteTemporaryResult, error)
```

#### **Parameters (WriteTemporaryOptions):**
```go
type WriteTemporaryOptions struct {
    SessionID         string      // Session identifier
    BaseCommit        string      // Base commit hash (for shadow branch naming)
    WorktreeID        string      // Worktree identifier (for shadow branch naming)
    ModifiedFiles     []string    // Files to include (modified tracked files)
    NewFiles          []string    // Files to include (untracked files)
    DeletedFiles      []string    // Files to exclude from tree
    MetadataDir       string      // Relative path to metadata directory
    MetadataDirAbs    string      // Absolute path to metadata directory
    CommitMessage     string      // Commit message
    AuthorName        string      // Commit author name
    AuthorEmail       string      // Commit author email
    IsFirstCheckpoint bool        // First checkpoint of session (includes all changed files)
}
```

#### **Return Value (WriteTemporaryResult):**
```go
type WriteTemporaryResult struct {
    CommitHash plumbing.Hash  // Hash of the created commit
    Skipped    bool           // True if deduplication skipped the checkpoint
}
```

#### **What WriteTemporary Does:**
1. **Gets or creates shadow branch** (`entire/<base-commit-hash[:7]>-<worktreeHash[:6]>`)
2. **Gets parent hash** (previous checkpoint on shadow branch, or ZeroHash if new)
3. **Builds tree** with code changes + metadata directory
4. **Deduplication** check: if new tree hash == last commit's tree hash, skip (return `Skipped: true`)
5. **Creates commit** with trailers (Entire-Session, Entire-Metadata, Entire-Strategy)
6. **Updates branch reference** to point to new commit

---

### 3. **CondenseSession** (`manual_commit_condensation.go:110-208`)

#### **What It Does:**
Condenses a session's shadow branch to permanent storage on `entire/checkpoints/v1`.

#### **Data Flow:**

**Input:**
- `checkpointID`: The 12-hex-char ID from the `Entire-Checkpoint` trailer
- `state`: SessionState containing file tracking, transcript position, attribution data
- `repo`: Git repository

**Process:**
1. **Get shadow branch** by name: `entire/<state.BaseCommit>-<state.WorktreeID>`
2. **Extract session data** via `extractSessionData()`:
   - **Transcript**: From shadow branch (or live file if more recent)
   - **Prompts**: Extracted from transcript via agent-specific parsers
   - **Context**: Generated from prompts as `context.md`
   - **FilesTouched**: From `state.FilesTouched` (tracked during session)
   - **Token Usage**: Calculated from transcript slice (since `CheckpointTranscriptStart`)
3. **Calculate attribution** via `calculateSessionAttributions()`:
   - Compares base tree → shadow tree → HEAD tree
   - Uses accumulated prompt attributions
   - Determines agent vs human line contributions
4. **Generate summary** (if summarize enabled):
   - Scopes transcript to this checkpoint's portion
   - Calls `summarize.GenerateFromTranscript()`
5. **Write to metadata branch** via `store.WriteCommitted()`:
   - Creates commit on `entire/checkpoints/v1`
   - Stores at sharded path: `<checkpoint-id[:2]>/<checkpoint-id[2:]>/`
   - Includes `metadata.json`, transcript, prompts, context, content_hash
   - For multi-session checkpoints, uses numbered subfolders (`0/`, `1/`, etc.)

**Output (CondenseResult):**
```go
type CondenseResult struct {
    CheckpointID         id.CheckpointID  // The checkpoint ID (for confirmation)
    SessionID            string           // The session being condensed
    CheckpointsCount     int              // Number of checkpoints in the shadow branch
    FilesTouched         []string         // Files that were touched
    TotalTranscriptLines int              // Total lines in transcript after this condensation
}
```

---

### 4. **SaveChanges** (`manual_commit.go:23-159`)

#### **SaveContext Structure:**
```go
type SaveContext struct {
    SessionID                  string                  // Session ID (extracted from MetadataDir)
    ModifiedFiles             []string                // Modified tracked files
    NewFiles                  []string                // Untracked files
    DeletedFiles              []string                // Deleted tracked files
    MetadataDir               string                  // Relative: .entire/metadata/<session-id>
    MetadataDirAbs            string                  // Absolute path for filesystem operations
    CommitMessage             string                  // Commit message
    TranscriptPath            string                  // Path to transcript file
    AuthorName                string                  // Git author name
    AuthorEmail               string                  // Git author email
    AgentType                 agent.AgentType         // Agent type (Claude Code, Gemini CLI, etc.)
    StepTranscriptIdentifier  string                  // Transcript position at step start
    StepTranscriptStart       int                     // Transcript line offset at step start
    TokenUsage                *agent.TokenUsage       // Token usage for this checkpoint
}
```

#### **SaveChanges Flow:**
1. **Load or initialize session state**
2. **Check if HEAD changed** (e.g., from rebase via tool call)
3. **Get prompt attribution** from state (calculated at prompt start in `InitializeSession`)
4. **Call `store.WriteTemporary()`** with options:
   - Merges ModifiedFiles + NewFiles
   - Uses MetadataDir and MetadataDirAbs
   - Sets IsFirstCheckpoint based on StepCount
5. **On success, update session state:**
   - Increment StepCount
   - Append PromptAttribution
   - Merge FilesTouched with new files
   - Accumulate TokenUsage
   - Save state
6. **Output**: Message to user about shadow branch creation/update

#### **Deduplication Handling:**
If WriteTemporary returns `Skipped: true`, SaveChanges returns early without updating state (no checkpoint created).

---

### 5. **Getting Files from a Commit**

To get the list of files that were part of a git commit, you can:

#### **Option A: Get all files in a commit's tree** (simple):
```go
commit, _ := repo.CommitObject(commitHash)
tree, _ := commit.Tree()
// Flatten tree to get all files
checkpoint.FlattenTree(repo, tree, "", entries)
// entries is map[string]object.TreeEntry with all file paths
```

#### **Option B: Calculate diff between commits** (to see what changed):
```go
// Get commit and parent
commit, _ := repo.CommitObject(commitHash)
parentCommit, _ := repo.CommitObject(commit.ParentHashes[0])

// Get trees
parentTree, _ := parentCommit.Tree()
commitTree, _ := commit.Tree()

// Compute patch (differences)
patch, _ := object.GetPatch(parentTree, commitTree)
// patch.FilePatches() returns []FileProtosal with file changes
```

#### **Option C: From session state** (most direct for "carry forward"):
Files are already tracked in `state.FilesTouched` after SaveChanges. This is the merged list of all modified/new/deleted files during the session.

---

### 6. **checkpoint/temporary.go - WriteTemporary Options**

Already detailed above in section 2. The key points:

**Special Handling:**
- **First checkpoint** (`IsFirstCheckpoint: true`): Includes ALL changed files from `git status` (respects .gitignore)
- **Subsequent checkpoints**: Only include specified ModifiedFiles + NewFiles
- **Deduplication**: Skips if new tree == last checkpoint's tree
- **Metadata directory**: Automatically included (transcripts, prompts, etc.)

---

### 7. **Complete Data Flow Summary**

```
Session Active → SaveChanges() → WriteTemporary() → Shadow branch checkpoint
     ↓
     (files tracked in state.FilesTouched)
     ↓
User commits → PostCommit() → CondenseSession() → entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
     ↓
Session state updated:
  - BaseCommit → new HEAD
  - StepCount → 0
  - FilesTouched → cleared (stored in checkpoint metadata)
  - CheckpointTranscriptStart → total transcript lines
  - LastCheckpointID → saved checkpoint ID

"Carry Forward" Feature Idea:
  After condensation, if state.FilesTouched still has files:
  → Create new shadow branch checkpoint with remaining files + all metadata
  → Allow session to continue without losing uncommitted work
```

---

### 8. **Key Files and Line References**

| Component | File | Lines | Key Functions |
|-----------|------|-------|---|
| PostCommit flow | `manual_commit_hooks.go` | 553-765 | `PostCommit()`, `condenseAndUpdateState()`, `PostCommit Hook Handler` |
| Condensation | `manual_commit_condensation.go` | 110-208 | `CondenseSession()`, `extractSessionData()`, `calculateSessionAttributions()` |
| SaveChanges | `manual_commit.go` | 23-159 | `SaveChanges()`, `SaveTaskCheckpoint()` |
| WriteTemporary | `checkpoint/temporary.go` | 56-141 | `WriteTemporary()` with deduplication logic |
| Rewind detection | `manual_commit_rewind.go` | 26-124 | `GetRewindPoints()`, `GetLogsOnlyRewindPoints()` |
| State machine | `session/phase.go` | (not fully read) | Transitions, Actions, phase handling |
| Common utilities | `common.go` | 81-195 | `ListCheckpoints()`, tree flattening, git operations |

---

This should give you everything you need to implement the "carry forward" feature where after condensation, remaining uncommitted files from the session are preserved in a new shadow checkpoint along with all metadata.

    56→func (s *GitStore) WriteTemporary(ctx context.Context, opts WriteTemporaryOptions) (WriteTemporaryResult, error) {
    57→	// Validate base commit - required for shadow branch naming
    58→	if opts.BaseCommit == "" {
    59→		return WriteTemporaryResult{}, errors.New("BaseCommit is required for temporary checkpoint")
    60→	}
    61→
    62→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
    63→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(opts.SessionID); err != nil {
    64→		return WriteTemporaryResult{}, fmt.Errorf("invalid temporary checkpoint options: %w", err)
    65→	}
    66→
    67→	// Get shadow branch name
    68→	shadowBranchName := ShadowBranchNameForCommit(opts.BaseCommit, opts.WorktreeID)
    69→
    70→	// Get or create shadow branch
    71→	parentHash, baseTreeHash, err := s.getOrCreateShadowBranch(shadowBranchName)
    72→	if err != nil {
    73→		return WriteTemporaryResult{}, fmt.Errorf("failed to get shadow branch: %w", err)
    74→	}
    75→
    76→	// Get the last checkpoint's tree hash for deduplication
    77→	var lastTreeHash plumbing.Hash
    78→	if parentHash != plumbing.ZeroHash {
    79→		if lastCommit, err := s.repo.CommitObject(parentHash); err == nil {
    80→			lastTreeHash = lastCommit.TreeHash
    81→		}
    82→	}
    83→
    84→	// Collect all files to include
    85→	var allFiles []string
    86→	var allDeletedFiles []string
    87→	if opts.IsFirstCheckpoint {
    88→		// For the first checkpoint, capture all changed files (modified tracked + untracked)
    89→		// using `git status --porcelain -z` which respects both repo and global .gitignore.
    90→		// This is much faster than filesystem walk. The base tree from HEAD already contains
    91→		// all unchanged tracked files. We also capture user's pre-existing deletions.
    92→		result, err := collectChangedFiles(ctx, s.repo)
    93→		if err != nil {
    94→			return WriteTemporaryResult{}, fmt.Errorf("failed to collect changed files: %w", err)
    95→		}
    96→		allFiles = result.Changed
    97→		// Merge user's pre-existing deletions with agent's deletions
    98→		allDeletedFiles = result.Deleted
    99→		allDeletedFiles = append(allDeletedFiles, opts.DeletedFiles...)
   100→	} else {
   101→		// For subsequent checkpoints, only include modified/new files
   102→		allFiles = make([]string, 0, len(opts.ModifiedFiles)+len(opts.NewFiles))
   103→		allFiles = append(allFiles, opts.ModifiedFiles...)
   104→		allFiles = append(allFiles, opts.NewFiles...)
   105→		allDeletedFiles = opts.DeletedFiles
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	// Build tree with changes
   109→	treeHash, err := s.buildTreeWithChanges(baseTreeHash, allFiles, allDeletedFiles, opts.MetadataDir, opts.MetadataDirAbs)
   110→	if err != nil {
   111→		return WriteTemporaryResult{}, fmt.Errorf("failed to build tree: %w", err)
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	// Deduplication: skip if tree hash matches the last checkpoint
   115→	if lastTreeHash != plumbing.ZeroHash && treeHash == lastTreeHash {
   116→		return WriteTemporaryResult{
   117→			CommitHash: parentHash,
   118→			Skipped:    true,
   119→		}, nil
   120→	}
   121→
   122→	// Create checkpoint commit with trailers
   123→	commitMsg := trailers.FormatShadowCommit(opts.CommitMessage, opts.MetadataDir, opts.SessionID)
   124→
   125→	commitHash, err := s.createCommit(treeHash, parentHash, commitMsg, opts.AuthorName, opts.AuthorEmail)
   126→	if err != nil {
   127→		return WriteTemporaryResult{}, fmt.Errorf("failed to create commit: %w", err)

   135→	}
   136→
   137→	return WriteTemporaryResult{
   138→		CommitHash: commitHash,
   139→		Skipped:    false,
   140→	}, nil
   141→}

Good — when a new shadow branch is created, `getOrCreateShadowBranch` uses HEAD's tree as the base. This means a carry-forward WriteTemporary call will start with the new HEAD's tree and overlay the remaining files + metadata. That's exactly what we need.

    32→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getStateStore() (*session.StateStore, error) {
    33→	s.stateStoreOnce.Do(func() {
    34→		store, err := session.NewStateStore()
    35→		if err != nil {
    36→			s.stateStoreErr = fmt.Errorf("failed to create state store: %w", err)
    37→			return
    38→		}
    39→		s.stateStore = store
    40→	})
    41→	return s.stateStore, s.stateStoreErr
    42→}
    43→
    44→// getCheckpointStore returns the checkpoint store, initializing it lazily if needed.
    45→// Thread-safe via sync.Once.
    46→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getCheckpointStore() (*checkpoint.GitStore, error) {
    47→	s.checkpointStoreOnce.Do(func() {
    48→		repo, err := OpenRepository()
    49→		if err != nil {
    50→			s.checkpointStoreErr = fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
    51→			return
    52→		}
    53→		s.checkpointStore = checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
    54→	})
    55→	return s.checkpointStore, s.checkpointStoreErr
    56→}

   553→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PostCommit() error {
   554→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   555→
   556→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   557→	if err != nil {
   558→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   559→	}
   560→
   561→	// Get HEAD commit to check for trailer
   562→	head, err := repo.Head()
   563→	if err != nil {
   564→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   565→	}
   566→
   567→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   568→	if err != nil {
   569→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   570→	}
   571→
   572→	// Check if commit has checkpoint trailer (ParseCheckpoint validates format)
   573→	checkpointID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(commit.Message)
   574→	if !found {
   575→		// No trailer — user removed it or it was never added (mid-turn commit).
   576→		// Still update BaseCommit for active sessions so future commits can match.
   577→		s.postCommitUpdateBaseCommitOnly(logCtx, head)
   578→		return nil
   579→	}
   580→
   581→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   582→	if err != nil {
   583→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   584→	}
   585→
   586→	// Find all active sessions for this worktree
   587→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   588→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   589→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: no active sessions despite trailer",
   590→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   591→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   592→		)
   593→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: hooks must be silent on failure

   598→	transitionCtx := session.TransitionContext{
   599→		IsRebaseInProgress: isRebase,
   600→	}
   601→
   602→	if isRebase {
   603→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: rebase/sequence in progress, skipping phase transitions",
   604→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   605→		)
   606→	}

     1→# Refactor: Condense at Each Commit (Remove Deferred Condensation)
     2→
     3→## Context
     4→
     5→The manual-commit strategy currently defers condensation when an agent is ACTIVE during a commit. This creates a complex lifecycle involving `PendingCheckpointID`, the `ACTIVE_COMMITTED` phase, `handleTurnEndCondense`, two-pass PostCommit processing, and shadow branch migration — all of which have been a source of bugs (see `soph/fix-issue-with-reusing-checkpoint-id`).
     6→
     7→**Goal:** Always condense at commit time, regardless of whether the agent is active. Each commit gets its own checkpoint ID and metadata entry. This eliminates `PendingCheckpointID`, the `ACTIVE_COMMITTED` phase, and the entire deferred condensation flow.
     8→
     9→## Branch
    10→
    11→Create `soph/single-commit-per-checkpoint` from `main`.
    12→
    13→## What Changes
    14→
    15→| Before | After |
    16→|--------|-------|
    17→| 4 phases (IDLE, ACTIVE, ACTIVE_COMMITTED, ENDED) | 3 phases (IDLE, ACTIVE, ENDED) |
    18→| ACTIVE + GitCommit → ACTIVE_COMMITTED + Migrate | ACTIVE + GitCommit → ACTIVE + Condense |
    19→| Condensation at TurnEnd (deferred) | Condensation at commit time (immediate) |
    20→| PendingCheckpointID lifecycle | Eliminated |
    21→| Two-pass PostCommit (condense + migrate) | Single-pass PostCommit |
    22→| handleTurnEndCondense function | Deleted |
    23→| Multiple commits share one checkpoint ID | Each commit gets unique checkpoint ID |
    24→
    25→## What Stays
    26→
    27→- `LastCheckpointID` — still used for amend trailer restoration
    28→- `migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded` — still needed in SaveChanges/InitializeSession for rebase detection
    29→- `postCommitUpdateBaseCommitOnly` — still needed for commits without trailers
    30→- Attribution correctness — `BaseCommit` still holds old value when `CondenseSession` runs (updated after)
    31→- `AttributionBaseCommit` — kept for backward compat (now redundant but harmless)
    32→
    33→## Implementation Steps
    34→
    35→### Step 1: State Machine (`cmd/entire/cli/session/phase.go`)
    36→
    37→- Remove `PhaseActiveCommitted` constant and from `allPhases`
    38→- `PhaseFromString("active_committed")` → `PhaseActive` (backward compat)
    39→- `IsActive()`: remove `|| p == PhaseActiveCommitted`
    40→- Remove `transitionFromActiveCommitted` function entirely
    41→- Change `transitionFromActive` `EventGitCommit`:
    42→  ```go
    43→  return TransitionResult{
    44→      NewPhase: PhaseActive,
    45→      Actions:  []Action{ActionCondense, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
    46→  }
    47→  ```
    48→- Remove `ActionMigrateShadowBranch` constant, its `String()` case, and passthrough in `ApplyCommonActions`
    49→- Remove `case PhaseActiveCommitted` in `Transition` switch
    50→
    51→### Step 2: Session State (`cmd/entire/cli/session/state.go`)
    52→
    53→- Remove `PendingCheckpointID string` field from `State` struct (line 62-64)
    54→- Old state files with this field are silently ignored by Go JSON unmarshaling
    55→
    56→### Step 3: PostCommit Simplification (`cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go`)
    57→
    58→- Remove two-pass architecture: delete `pendingMigration` type, `pendingMigrations` slice, Pass 2 loop (lines 644-647, 728-739)
    59→- Remove `ActionMigrateShadowBranch` case from action dispatch (lines 703-708)
    60→- Shadow branch cleanup: replace `activeSessionsOnBranch` with `condensedOnBranch` tracking — only preserve branches for ACTIVE sessions that were NOT condensed:
    61→  ```go
    62→  condensed := false
    63→  // ... in ActionCondense: condensed = true
    64→  if state.Phase.IsActive() && !condensed {
    65→      uncondensedActiveOnBranch[shadowBranchName] = true
    66→  }
    67→  ```
    68→- In `condenseAndUpdateState` (line 805): remove `state.PendingCheckpointID = ""` (field gone)
    69→
    70→### Step 4: PrepareCommitMsg Simplification (`cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go`)
    71→
    72→- `handleAmendCommitMsg`: remove `PendingCheckpointID` check (lines 532-536), only check `LastCheckpointID`
    73→- Remove `isRestoringExisting` logic checking `PendingCheckpointID` (lines 419-430)
    74→- Fresh ID generation and `addTrailerForAgentCommit` unchanged (already correct from recent fix)
    75→
    76→### Step 5: HandleTurnEnd Simplification (`cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go`)
    77→
    78→- Remove `ActionCondense` dispatch from `HandleTurnEnd` (line 1449)
    79→- Delete `handleTurnEndCondense` function entirely (lines 1465-1537)
    80→- Delete `hasOtherActiveSessionsOnBranch` function (lines 1542-1556, only caller was handleTurnEndCondense)
    81→- `HandleTurnEnd` becomes a no-op (ACTIVE + TurnEnd emits only `ActionUpdateLastInteraction`, handled by `ApplyCommonActions`)
    82→
    83→### Step 6: InitializeSession & Other Cleanup
    84→
    85→- `InitializeSession` (line 1231-1233): remove `PendingCheckpointID` clearing
    86→- `manual_commit_git.go` (lines 118-121): remove `PendingCheckpointID` preservation comment
    87→- `manual_commit_condensation.go` (line 609): remove `state.PendingCheckpointID = ""` in `CondenseSessionByID`
    88→
    89→### Step 7: Test Updates
    90→
    91→**`session/phase_test.go`:**
    92→- `TestPhaseFromString`: `"active_committed"` → expect `PhaseActive`
    93→- `TestPhase_IsActive`: remove ACTIVE_COMMITTED case
    94→- `TestTransitionFromActive`: GitCommit → ACTIVE + ActionCondense (not ACTIVE_COMMITTED + Migrate)
    95→- Delete `TestTransitionFromActiveCommitted` entirely
    96→- Update `TestApplyCommonActions_*` and `TestMermaidDiagram` references
    97→- Remove `ActionMigrateShadowBranch` from action string tests
    98→
    99→**`strategy/phase_postcommit_test.go`:**
   100→- `TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_NoCondensation` → rewrite as `TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_Condenses`
   101→- Delete: `TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_SetsPendingCheckpointID`, `TestPostCommit_ActiveCommitted_MigratesShadowBranch`
   102→- Delete all TurnEnd condensation tests: `TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_*`, `TestTurnEnd_ConcurrentSession_*`
   103→- Rewrite `TestTurnEnd_DeferredCondensation_AttributionUsesOriginalBase` → `TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_AttributionUsesOriginalBase`
   104→- Keep `TestTurnEnd_Active_NoActions` (update comment)
   105→- Add: `TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch`
   106→
   107→**`strategy/phase_prepare_commit_msg_test.go`:**
   108→- Rewrite amend test to use `LastCheckpointID` only (remove `PendingCheckpointID`)
   109→- Update `ConsecutiveCommits` test: phase → ACTIVE (not ACTIVE_COMMITTED), remove `PendingCheckpointID`
   110→
   111→**`strategy/mid_turn_commit_test.go`:**
   112→- Delete `TestSaveChanges_PreservesPendingCheckpointID`
   113→
   114→**`integration_test/phase_transitions_test.go`:**
   115→- `TestShadow_CommitBeforeStop`: verify immediate condensation (not deferred), phase stays ACTIVE (not ACTIVE_COMMITTED), no condensation at TurnEnd
   116→
   117→### Step 8: Documentation Updates
   118→
   119→**`docs/architecture/sessions-and-checkpoints.md`:**
   120→- Remove `PendingCheckpointID` row and deferred condensation sections (added earlier today)
   121→
   122→**`CLAUDE.md`:**
   123→- Remove ACTIVE_COMMITTED from phases, ActionMigrateShadowBranch from actions
   124→- Update transitions: `ACTIVE + GitCommit → ACTIVE + [ActionCondense]`
   125→- Remove PendingCheckpointID references throughout
   126→
   127→## Verification
   128→
   129→```bash
   130→mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci
   131→```
   132→
   133→## Backward Compatibility
   134→
   135→- Old state files with `"pending_checkpoint_id"` → silently ignored
   136→- Old state files with `"phase": "active_committed"` → mapped to `"active"` via PhaseFromString
   137→- In-flight ACTIVE_COMMITTED sessions: next commit condenses immediately (one-time migration)
   138→

{
  "file_path": "/Users/soph/.claude/plans/majestic-plotting-valiant.md",
  "content": "# Carry Forward: Preserve Uncommitted Files After Condensation\n\n## Context\n\nWhen an agent makes multiple commits within a single turn (e.g., commits 2 of 3 edited files, then commits the third), the second commit currently gets no checkpoint. After the first commit condenses, `StepCount` resets to 0, `FilesTouched` is cleared, and the shadow branch is deleted. The second commit sees no session data to condense.\n\n**Goal:** After condensation, if the session still has uncommitted files that were touched during the session, create a new shadow branch at the new HEAD carrying those remaining files + all metadata. This ensures every commit gets its own checkpoint.\n\n## What Changes\n\nAfter `condenseAndUpdateState` succeeds for an ACTIVE session:\n1. Compute which files from the session were NOT part of the just-committed commit\n2. If remaining files exist, create a new shadow branch at the new HEAD with those files + metadata\n3. Update state: `FilesTouched = remaining`, `StepCount = 1`\n\nThe old shadow branch is still deleted (it was at the old base commit). The new one is at the new HEAD.\n\n## Implementation\n\n### Step 1: Helper to get committed files (`manual_commit_hooks.go`)\n\nAdd `filesChangedInCommit(repo, commit)` that diffs the commit against its parent:\n\n```go\nfunc filesChangedInCommit(repo *git.Repository, commit *object.Commit) map[string]struct{} {\n    // Get parent tree (empty tree if initial commit)\n    // Diff parent tree vs commit tree\n    // Return set of changed file paths (From.Name or To.Name)\n}\n```\n\nPattern exists in `explain.go:1543-1563` using `parentTree.Diff(commitTree)`.\n\n### Step 2: Carry-forward logic in PostCommit loop (`manual_commit_hooks.go`)\n\nIn the PostCommit session loop, after condensation:\n\n```go\ncondensed := false\n// ... existing action dispatch ...\n\n// After condensation, carry forward remaining files for ACTIVE sessions\nif condensed && state.Phase.IsActive() {\n    remaining := subtractFiles(savedFilesTouched, committedFileSet)\n    if len(remaining) > 0 {\n        s.carryForwardToNewShadowBranch(logCtx, repo, state, remaining)\n    }\n}\n```\n\nKey details:\n- Save `filesTouchedBefore := state.FilesTouched` BEFORE the action loop (since condensation clears it)\n- Compute `committedFileSet` once before the session loop using `filesChangedInCommit`\n- `subtractFiles` returns files in `filesTouchedBefore` that are NOT in `committedFileSet`\n\n### Step 3: `carryForwardToNewShadowBranch` method (`manual_commit_hooks.go`)\n\n```go\nfunc (s *ManualCommitStrategy) carryForwardToNewShadowBranch(\n    logCtx context.Context,\n    repo *git.Repository,\n    state *SessionState,\n    remainingFiles []string,\n) {\n    store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()\n    // ...\n\n    metadataDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(state.SessionID)\n    metadataDirAbs := filepath.Join(state.WorktreePath, metadataDir)\n\n    result, err := store.WriteTemporary(context.Background(), checkpoint.WriteTemporaryOptions{\n        SessionID:         state.SessionID,\n        BaseCommit:        state.BaseCommit,        // Already updated to new HEAD by condensation\n        WorktreeID:        state.WorktreeID,\n        ModifiedFiles:     remainingFiles,           // All remaining files as \"modified\"\n        MetadataDir:       metadataDir,\n        MetadataDirAbs:    metadataDirAbs,\n        CommitMessage:     \"carry forward: uncommitted session files\",\n        IsFirstCheckpoint: false,                    // Only include specified files, not all dirty files\n    })\n    // On success:\n    state.FilesTouched = remainingFiles\n    state.StepCount = 1\n    // On failure or skip: log warning, session continues without carry-forward\n}\n```\n\nThe new shadow branch is at `state.BaseCommit` (new HEAD), which is different from the old one. It won't be in `shadowBranchesToDelete`, so it survives cleanup. And since `condensed = true`, it won't be in `uncondensedActiveOnBranch` either — which is correct because it now HAS a shadow branch with data.\n\n### Step 4: `subtractFiles` helper (`manual_commit_hooks.go`)\n\n```go\nfunc subtractFiles(files []string, exclude map[string]struct{}) []string {\n    var remaining []string\n    for _, f := range files {\n        if _, excluded := exclude[f]; !excluded {\n            remaining = append(remaining, f)\n        }\n    }\n    return remaining\n}\n```\n\n### Step 5: Tests (`strategy/phase_postcommit_test.go`)\n\nAdd `TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_CarriesForwardRemainingFiles`:\n1. Initialize session, save checkpoint with 3 files\n2. Create a commit that only includes 2 of the 3 files\n3. Run PostCommit\n4. Verify: condensation happened, new shadow branch exists at new HEAD, `state.FilesTouched` = [remaining file], `state.StepCount = 1`\n\nAdd `TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_NoCarryForward_WhenAllFilesCommitted`:\n1. Initialize session, save checkpoint with 2 files\n2. Create a commit that includes both files\n3. Run PostCommit\n4. Verify: condensation happened, shadow branch deleted, `state.FilesTouched = nil`, `state.StepCount = 0`\n\n### Step 6: Integration test update (`integration_test/phase_transitions_test.go`)\n\nUpdate `TestShadow_CommitBeforeStop` or add a new test that verifies the carry-forward flow end-to-end with multiple commits.\n\n## Files to Modify\n\n| File | Change |\n|------|--------|\n| `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` | Add `filesChangedInCommit`, `carryForwardToNewShadowBranch`, `subtractFiles`; modify PostCommit loop |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/phase_postcommit_test.go` | Add carry-forward tests |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/phase_transitions_test.go` | Optional: add integration test |\n\n## Existing Code to Reuse\n\n- `parentTree.Diff(commitTree)` pattern from `explain.go:1543-1563` — for computing committed files\n- `checkpoint.WriteTemporary()` from `checkpoint/temporary.go:56` — for creating the carry-forward shadow branch\n- `paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID()` from `paths/paths.go:179` — for metadata dir path\n- `checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit()` — shadow branch naming\n\n## Verification\n\n```bash\nmise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci\n```\n"
}
```

s.carryForwardToNewShadowBranch(logCtx, repo, state, remaining)\n    }\n}\n```\n\nKey details:\n- Save `filesTouchedBefore := state.FilesTouched` BEFORE the action loop (since condensation clears it)\n- Compute `committedFileSet` once before the session loop using `filesChangedInCommit`\n- `subtractFiles` returns files in `filesTouchedBefore` that are NOT in `committedFileSet`\n\n### Step 3: `carryForwardToNewShadowBranch` method (`manual_commit_hooks.go`)\n\n```go\nfunc (s *ManualCommitStrategy) carryForwardToNewShadowBranch(\n    logCtx context.Context,\n    repo *git.Repository,\n    state *SessionState,\n    remainingFiles []string,\n) {\n    store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()\n    // ...\n\n    metadataDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(state.SessionID)\n    metadataDirAbs := filepath.Join(state.WorktreePath, metadataDir)\n\n    result, err := store.WriteTemporary(context.Background(), checkpoint.WriteTemporaryOptions{\n        SessionID:         state.SessionID,\n        BaseCommit:        state.BaseCommit,        // Already updated to new HEAD by condensation\n        WorktreeID:        state.WorktreeID,\n        ModifiedFiles:     remainingFiles,           // All remaining files as \"modified\"\n        MetadataDir:       metadataDir,\n        MetadataDirAbs:    metadataDirAbs,\n        CommitMessage:     \"carry forward: uncommitted session files\",\n        IsFirstCheckpoint: false,                    // Only include specified files, not all dirty files\n    })\n    // On success:\n    state.FilesTouched = remainingFiles\n    state.StepCount = 1\n    // On failure or skip: log warning, session continues without carry-forward\n}\n```\n\nThe new shadow branch is at `state.BaseCommit` (new HEAD), which is different from the old one. It won't be in `shadowBranchesToDelete`, so it survives cleanup. And since `condensed = true`, it won't be in `uncondensedActiveOnBranch` either — which is correct because it now HAS a shadow branch with data.\n\n### Step 4: `subtractFiles` helper (`manual_commit_hooks.go`)\n\n```go\nfunc subtractFiles(files []string, exclude map[string]struct{}) []string {\n    var remaining []string\n    for _, f := range files {\n        if _, excluded := exclude[f]; !excluded {\n            remaining = append(remaining, f)\n        }\n    }\n    return remaining\n}\n```\n\n### Step 5: Tests (`strategy/phase_postcommit_test.go`)\n\nAdd `TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_CarriesForwardRemainingFiles`:\n1.
